5592: function calculateProfits() {
5593:             const purchasePrice = parseFloat(document.getElementById('purchasePrice')?.value) || 0;
5594:             const sellingPrice = parseFloat(document.getElementById('sellingPrice')?.value) || 0;
5595:             const siteAds = parseFloat(document.getElementById('siteAdsExpense')?.value) || 0;
5596: 
5597:             // 1. Website Profit
5598:             const websiteProfit = sellingPrice > 0 ? (sellingPrice - purchasePrice - siteAds) : 0;
5599:             const webBadge = document.getElementById('websiteProfitBadge');
5600:             if (webBadge) {
5601:                 webBadge.textContent = `صافي الربح: ${websiteProfit.toFixed(2)} ج.م`;
5602:                 webBadge.style.background = websiteProfit >= 0 ? '#2e7d32' : '#c62828';
5603:             }
5604: 
5605:             // 2. Amazon Profit
5606:             const amazonPrice = parseFloat(document.getElementById('amazonPrice')?.value) || 0;
5607:             const amazonCommission = parseFloat(document.getElementById('amazonCommission')?.value) || 0;
5608:             const amazonAds = parseFloat(document.getElementById('amazonAdsExpense')?.value) || 0;
5609:             const amazonProfit = amazonPrice > 0 ? (amazonPrice - (amazonPrice * amazonCommission / 100) - amazonAds - purchasePrice) : 0;
5610:             const amzBadge = document.getElementById('amazonProfitBadge');
5611:             if (amzBadge) {
5612:                 amzBadge.textContent = `صافي الربح: ${amazonProfit.toFixed(2)} ج.م`;
5613:                 amzBadge.style.background = amazonProfit >= 0 ? '#e65100' : '#c62828';
5614:             }
5615: 
5616:             // 3. Noon Profit
5617:             const noonPrice = parseFloat(document.getElementById('noonPrice')?.value) || 0;
5618:             const noonCommission = parseFloat(document.getElementById('noonCommission')?.value) || 0;
5619:             const noonShipping = parseFloat(document.getElementById('noonShipping')?.value) || 0;
5620:             const noonAds = parseFloat(document.getElementById('noonAdsExpense')?.value) || 0;
5621:             const noonProfit = noonPrice > 0 ? (noonPrice - (noonPrice * noonCommission / 100) - noonShipping - noonAds - purchasePrice) : 0;
5622:             const noonBadge = document.getElementById('noonProfitBadge');
5623:             if (noonBadge) {
5624:                 noonBadge.textContent = `صافي الربح: ${noonProfit.toFixed(2)} ج.م`;
5625:                 noonBadge.style.background = noonProfit >= 0 ? '#f57f17' : '#c62828';
5626:             }
5627: 
5628:             // 4. Jumia Profit
5629:             const jumiaPrice = parseFloat(document.getElementById('jumiaPrice')?.value) || 0;
5630:             const jumiaCommission = parseFloat(document.getElementById('jumiaCommission')?.value) || 0;
5631:             const jumiaShipping = parseFloat(document.getElementById('jumiaShipping')?.value) || 0;
5632:             const jumiaAds = parseFloat(document.getElementById('jumiaAdsExpense')?.value) || 0;
5633:             const jumiaProfit = jumiaPrice > 0 ? (jumiaPrice - (jumiaPrice * jumiaCommission / 100) - jumiaShipping - jumiaAds - purchasePrice) : 0;
5634:             const jumiaBadge = document.getElementById('jumiaProfitBadge');
5635:             if (jumiaBadge) {
5636:                 jumiaBadge.textContent = `صافي الربح: ${jumiaProfit.toFixed(2)} ج.م`;
5637:                 jumiaBadge.style.background = jumiaProfit >= 0 ? '#d84315' : '#c62828';
5638:             }
5639: 
5640:             // 5. Dynamic Custom Stores Profits
5641:             const customItems = document.querySelectorAll('.custom-store-item');
5642:             customItems.forEach(item => {
5643:                 const price = parseFloat(item.querySelector('.custom-store-price')?.value) || 0;
5644:                 const commission = parseFloat(item.querySelector('.custom-store-commission')?.value) || 0;
5645:                 const shipping = parseFloat(item.querySelector('.custom-store-shipping')?.value) || 0;
5646:                 const ads = parseFloat(item.querySelector('.custom-store-ads')?.value) || 0;
5647:                 
5648:                 const customProfit = price > 0 ? (price - (price * commission / 100) - shipping - ads - purchasePrice) : 0;
5649:                 const badge = item.querySelector('.custom-store-profit-badge');
5650:                 if (badge) {
5651:                     badge.textContent = `الربح: ${customProfit.toFixed(2)} ج.م`;
5652:                     badge.style.background = customProfit >= 0 ? '#8e24aa' : '#c62828';
5653:                 }
5654:             });
5655:             
5656:             if (typeof calculatePlatformProfits === 'function') {
5657:                 calculatePlatformProfits();
5658:             }
5659:         }
5660: 
5661:         // Tax Calculation Function (14% VAT on Purchase Price)
5662:         function calculateProfitsWithTax() {
5663:             const TAX_RATE = 0.14;
5664:             
5665:             // Get purchase price from the product-level field
5666:             const purchasePriceInput = document.getElementById('purchasePrice');
5667:             const purchasePrice = purchasePriceInput ? (parseFloat(purchasePriceInput.value) || 0) : 0;
5668:             
5669:             const sellingPrice = parseFloat(document.getElementById('sellingPrice').value) || 0;
5670:             const discountPercent = parseFloat(document.getElementById('discountPercent').value) || 0;
5671:             const siteAds = parseFloat(document.getElementById('siteAdsExpense').value) || 0;
5672:             
5673:             console.log('calculateProfitsWithTax:', { purchasePrice, sellingPrice });
5674:             
5675:             // Calculate 14% tax on purchase price
5676:             const taxAmount = purchasePrice * TAX_RATE;
5677:             const totalWithTax = purchasePrice + taxAmount;
5678:             
5679:             // Calculate discount on selling price
5680:             const discountValue = sellingPrice * (discountPercent / 100);
5681:             const netSellingPrice = sellingPrice - discountValue;
5682:             
5683:             // Update tax amount display
5684:             const taxAmountEl = document.getElementById('taxAmount');
5685:             if (taxAmountEl) taxAmountEl.value = taxAmount.toFixed(2) + ' جنيه';
5686:             
5687:             // Update breakdown display
5688:             const breakdownPurchase = document.getElementById('breakdownPurchase');
5689:             const breakdownTax = document.getElementById('breakdownTax');
5690:             const breakdownTotal = document.getElementById('breakdownTotal');
5691:             const breakdownSelling = document.getElementById('breakdownSelling');
5692:             const breakdownDiscountPercent = document.getElementById('breakdownDiscountPercent');
5693:             const breakdownDiscountValue = document.getElementById('breakdownDiscountValue');
5694:             const breakdownNetSelling = document.getElementById('breakdownNetSelling');
5695:             
5696:             if (breakdownPurchase) breakdownPurchase.textContent = purchasePrice.toFixed(2) + ' جنيه';
5697:             if (breakdownTax) breakdownTax.textContent = taxAmount.toFixed(2) + ' جنيه';
5698:             if (breakdownTotal) breakdownTotal.textContent = totalWithTax.toFixed(2) + ' جنيه';
5699:             if (breakdownSelling) breakdownSelling.textContent = sellingPrice.toFixed(2) + ' جنيه';
5700:             if (breakdownDiscountPercent) breakdownDiscountPercent.textContent = discountPercent;
5701:             if (breakdownDiscountValue) breakdownDiscountValue.textContent = discountValue.toFixed(2) + ' جنيه';
5702:             if (breakdownNetSelling) breakdownNetSelling.textContent = netSellingPrice.toFixed(2) + ' جنيه';
5703:             
5704:             // Show/hide tax breakdown
5705:             const taxBreakdown = document.getElementById('taxBreakdownDisplay');
5706:             const sellingAfterTax = document.getElementById('sellingPriceAfterTax');
5707:             const websiteProfitDisplay = document.getElementById('websiteProfitDisplay');
5708:             
5709:             if (purchasePrice > 0 || sellingPrice > 0) {
5710:                 if (taxBreakdown) taxBreakdown.style.display = 'block';
5711:                 if (sellingAfterTax) sellingAfterTax.style.display = 'block';
5712:             } else {
5713:                 if (taxBreakdown) taxBreakdown.style.display = 'none';
5714:                 if (sellingAfterTax) sellingAfterTax.style.display = 'none';
5715:             }
5716:             
5717:             // Website Profit (using net selling price after discount)
5718:             if (netSellingPrice > 0 && purchasePrice > 0) {
5719:                 const websiteProfit = netSellingPrice - totalWithTax - siteAds;
5720:                 if (document.getElementById('websiteProfit')) {
5721:                     document.getElementById('websiteProfit').textContent = websiteProfit.toFixed(2) + ' جنيه';
5722:                     document.getElementById('websiteProfit').style.color = websiteProfit >= 0 ? '#28a745' : '#dc3545';
5723:                 }
5724:                 if (websiteProfitDisplay) websiteProfitDisplay.style.display = 'block';
5725:             } else {
5726:                 if (websiteProfitDisplay) websiteProfitDisplay.style.display = 'none';
5727:             }
5728:             
5729:             // Also call calculateProfits for noon/jumia
5730:             calculateProfits();
5731:         }

5929:         // Pagination rendering function
5930:         function renderPagination(totalItems, currentPage, paginationId, loadFunction) {
5931:             const totalPages = Math.ceil(totalItems / itemsPerPage);
5932:             const paginationContainer = document.getElementById(paginationId);
5933:             
5934:             if (!paginationContainer || totalPages <= 1) {
5935:                 if (paginationContainer) paginationContainer.innerHTML = '';
5936:                 return;
5937:             }
5938:             
5939:             let paginationHTML = '';
5940:             
5941:             // Previous button
5942:             if (currentPage > 1) {
5943:                 paginationHTML += `<button onclick="${loadFunction}(${currentPage - 1})" class="btn" style="padding: 5px 10px; margin: 0 2px;"><i class="fas fa-chevron-right"></i></button>`;
5944:             }
5945:             
5946:             // Show first page always
5947:             paginationHTML += `<button onclick="${loadFunction}(1)" class="btn ${currentPage === 1 ? 'btn-primary' : ''}" style="padding: 5px 10px; margin: 0 2px;">1</button>`;
5948:             
5949:             // Show ellipsis if needed
5950:             if (currentPage > 3) {
5951:                 paginationHTML += `<span style="margin: 0 5px;">...</span>`;
5952:             }
5953:             
5954:             // Show pages around current page
5955:             let startPage = Math.max(2, currentPage - 1);
5956:             let endPage = Math.min(totalPages - 1, currentPage + 1);
5957:             
5958:             for (let i = startPage; i <= endPage; i++) {
5959:                 paginationHTML += `<button onclick="${loadFunction}(${i})" class="btn ${currentPage === i ? 'btn-primary' : ''}" style="padding: 5px 10px; margin: 0 2px;">${i}</button>`;
5960:             }
5961:             
5962:             // Show ellipsis if needed
5963:             if (currentPage < totalPages - 2) {
5964:                 paginationHTML += `<span style="margin: 0 5px;">...</span>`;
5965:             }
5966:             
5967:             // Show last page if more than 1 page
5968:             if (totalPages > 1) {
5969:                 paginationHTML += `<button onclick="${loadFunction}(${totalPages})" class="btn ${currentPage === totalPages ? 'btn-primary' : ''}" style="padding: 5px 10px; margin: 0 2px;">${totalPages}</button>`;
5970:             }
5971:             
5972:             // Next button
5973:             if (currentPage < totalPages) {
5974:                 paginationHTML += `<button onclick="${loadFunction}(${currentPage + 1})" class="btn" style="padding: 5px 10px; margin: 0 2px;"><i class="fas fa-chevron-left"></i></button>`;
5975:             }
5976:             
5977:             paginationContainer.innerHTML = paginationHTML;
5978:         }

5989:             if (products.length === 0) {
5990:                 tbody.innerHTML = '<tr><td colspan="11" style="text-align: center; padding: 50px; color: #666;"><i class="fas fa-box" style="font-size: 3rem; margin-bottom: 15px; opacity: 0.3;"></i><br>لا توجد منتجات حالياً</td></tr>';
5991:                 renderPagination(products.length, productsPage, 'productsPagination', 'loadProducts');
5992:                 return;
5993:             }
5994: 
5995:             const startIndex = (productsPage - 1) * itemsPerPage;
5996:             const endIndex = startIndex + itemsPerPage;
5997:             const paginatedProducts = products.slice(startIndex, endIndex);
5998:             
5999:             tbody.innerHTML = paginatedProducts.map(product => {
6000:                 let soldQty = 0;
6001:                 let totalRevenue = 0;
6002:                 let totalProfit = 0;
6003: 
6004:                 if (window.invoices && Array.isArray(window.invoices)) {
6005:                     window.invoices.forEach(inv => {
6006:                         if (inv.items && Array.isArray(inv.items)) {
6007:                             inv.items.forEach(item => {
6008:                                 if (item.productId === product.id || item.code === product.code || item.name === product.name) {
6009:                                     const qty = item.quantity || 1;
6010:                                     const price = item.price || product.sellingPrice || 0;
6011:                                     const cost = product.purchasePrice || 0;
6012:                                     soldQty += qty;
6013:                                     totalRevenue += (qty * price);
6014:                                     totalProfit += (qty * (price - cost));
6015:                                 }
6016:                             });
6017:                         }
6018:                     });
6019:                 }
6020: 
6021:                 const avgProfit = soldQty > 0 ? (totalProfit / soldQty) : ((product.sellingPrice || 0) - (product.purchasePrice || 0));
6022:                 const profitColor = avgProfit >= 0 ? '#2e7d32' : '#c62828';
6023:                 
6024:                 let colorsDisplay = '';
6025:                 if (product.colors && product.colors.length > 0) {
6026:                     colorsDisplay = product.colors.map(color => 
6027:                         `<span style="background: ${color.quantity === 0 ? '#ffcccc' : '#e3f2fd'}; color: ${color.quantity === 0 ? '#dc3545' : '#1976d2'}; padding: 2px 6px; border-radius: 10px; font-size: 0.75rem; margin: 1px; display: inline-block;">${color.name} (${color.quantity})</span>`
6028:                     ).join(' ');
6029:                 } else if (product.color) {
6030:                     colorsDisplay = `<span style="background: ${product.quantity === 0 ? '#ffcccc' : '#e3f2fd'}; color: ${product.quantity === 0 ? '#dc3545' : '#1976d2'}; padding: 2px 6px; border-radius: 10px; font-size: 0.75rem;">${product.color} (${product.quantity || 0})</span>`;
6031:                 }
6032: 
6033:                 const totalQuantity = product.totalQuantity || product.quantity || 0;
6034:                 const isOutOfStock = totalQuantity === 0;
6035:                 const isLowStock = totalQuantity > 0 && totalQuantity <= 5;
6036:                 const rowStyle = isOutOfStock ? 'background: #ffe6e6;' : (isLowStock ? 'background: #fff3e0;' : '');
6037:                 
6038:                 const imgSrc = product.image || product.imageUrl || 'https://cdn-icons-png.flaticon.com/512/679/679922.png';
6039: 
6040:                 return `
6041:                 <tr style="${rowStyle}">
6042:                     <td style="padding: 12px; text-align: center;">
6043:                         <input type="checkbox" class="product-checkbox" value="${product.id}" style="transform: scale(1.1);">
6044:                     </td>
6045:                     <td style="padding: 12px; text-align: center;">
6046:                         <img src="${imgSrc}" alt="${product.name}" style="width: 50px; height: 50px; object-fit: cover; border-radius: 8px; box-shadow: 0 2px 6px rgba(0,0,0,0.15); cursor: pointer;" onclick="showImageModal('${imgSrc}', '${product.name}')" onerror="this.src='https://cdn-icons-png.flaticon.com/512/679/679922.png'">
6047:                     </td>
6048:                     <td style="padding: 12px;">
6049:                         <strong style="color: var(--primary); font-size: 0.95rem;">${product.name}</strong>
6050:                         <div style="margin-top: 4px;">${colorsDisplay}</div>
6051:                     </td>
6052:                     <td style="padding: 12px;"><strong>${product.code}</strong></td>
6053:                     <td style="padding: 12px;">${product.collection || '-'}</td>
6054:                     <td style="padding: 12px;">
6055:                         <div style="display: flex; flex-direction: column; gap: 3px; font-size: 0.8rem;">
6056:                             <span style="background:#e8f5e9; color:#2e7d32; padding:2px 6px; border-radius:4px;">🌐 الموقع: <b>${product.sellingPrice || 0}</b>ج</span>
6057:                             <span style="background:#fff8e1; color:#ff8f00; padding:2px 6px; border-radius:4px;">📦 أمازون: <b>${product.amazonPrice || product.sellingPrice || 0}</b>ج</span>
6058:                             <span style="background:#e3f2fd; color:#1976d2; padding:2px 6px; border-radius:4px;">🛍️ نون: <b>${product.noonPrice || 0}</b>ج</span>
6059:                             <span style="background:#fff3e0; color:#e65100; padding:2px 6px; border-radius:4px;">🟧 جوميا: <b>${product.jumiaPrice || 0}</b>ج</span>
6060:                             ${product.otherPrice ? `<span style="background:#f3e5f5; color:#7b1fa2; padding:2px 6px; border-radius:4px;">📱 أخرى: <b>${product.otherPrice}</b>ج</span>` : ''}
6061:                         </div>
6062:                     </td>
6063:                     <td style="padding: 12px; font-weight: bold; color: #e65100;">${product.purchasePrice || 0} ج.م</td>
6064:                     <td style="padding: 12px;">
6065:                         <span style="background: #e8f5e9; color: #1b5e20; padding: 4px 8px; border-radius: 6px; font-size: 0.85rem; font-weight: bold; display: inline-block;">
6066:                             ${soldQty} قطعة (${totalRevenue.toFixed(2)} ج.م)
6067:                         </span>
6068:                     </td>
6069:                     <td style="padding: 12px;">
6070:                         <span style="background: ${avgProfit >= 0 ? '#e8f5e9' : '#ffebee'}; color: ${profitColor}; padding: 4px 8px; border-radius: 6px; font-weight: bold; font-size: 0.85rem; display: inline-block;">
6071:                             ${avgProfit.toFixed(2)} ج.م
6072:                         </span>
6073:                     </td>
6074:                     <td style="padding: 12px; font-weight: bold; text-align: center; color: ${isOutOfStock ? '#dc3545' : '#333'};">${totalQuantity}</td>
6075:                     <td style="padding: 12px; text-align: center;">
6076:                         <div class="product-actions" style="display: flex; gap: 5px; justify-content: center;">
6077:                             <button onclick="openProductModal(${product.id})" class="btn btn-edit" title="تعديل" style="padding: 5px 8px;"><i class="fas fa-edit"></i></button>
6078:                             <button onclick="deleteProduct(${product.id})" class="btn btn-delete" title="حذف" style="padding: 5px 8px;"><i class="fas fa-trash"></i></button>
6079:                         </div>
6080:                     </td>
6081:                 </tr>
6082:                 `;
6083:             }).join('');
6084:             
6085:             renderPagination(products.length, productsPage, 'productsPagination', 'loadProducts');
6086:         }

6090:                 products = products.filter(p => p.id !== id);
6091:                 saveData();
6092:                 loadProducts();
6093:                 showSuccessMessage('تم حذف المنتج بنجاح!');
6094:             }

6108:             const selectedCheckboxes = document.querySelectorAll('.product-checkbox:checked');
6109:             if (selectedCheckboxes.length === 0) {
6110:                 showErrorMessage('الرجاء اختيار المنتجات المراد حذفها');
6111:                 return;
6112:             }
6113:             
6114:             if (confirm(`هل أنت متأكد من حذف ${selectedCheckboxes.length} منتجات؟`)) {
6115:                 const selectedIds = Array.from(selectedCheckboxes).map(cb => cb.value);
6116:                 products = products.filter(p => !selectedIds.includes(p.id));
6117:                 saveData();
6118:                 loadProducts();
6119:                 // Reset select all checkbox
6120:                 document.getElementById('selectAllProducts').checked = false;
6121:                 showSuccessMessage(`تم حذف ${selectedCheckboxes.length} منتجات بنجاح!`);
6122:             }

6189:         // Print barcodes - using online API with better loading
6190:         function printBarcodes() {
6191:             const copies = parseInt(document.getElementById('barcodeCopies').value) || 1;
6192:             
6193:             // Use stored IDs instead of re-querying checkboxes
6194:             // Convert both to strings for proper comparison
6195:             const selectedProducts = products.filter(p => barcodeSelectedIds.map(id => String(id)).includes(String(p.id)));
6196:             
6197:             if (selectedProducts.length === 0) {
6198:                 alert('الرجاء اختيار منتجات أولاً');
6199:                 return;
6200:             }
6201:             
6202:             // Build HTML content with barcode images
6203:             let barcodeItems = '';
6204:             selectedProducts.forEach(product => {
6205:                 for (let i = 0; i < copies; i++) {
6206:                     const sellingPrice = product.sellingPrice || 0;
6207:                     const productName = product.name.substring(0, 25);
6208:                     
6209:                     // Generate barcode as SVG inline instead of external image
6210:                     barcodeItems += '<div class="barcode-sticker" style="width:58mm;height:38mm;margin:0;padding:2mm;border:1px dashed #000;box-sizing:border-box;page-break-after:always;display:flex;flex-direction:column;align-items:center;justify-content:center;background:white;">' +
6211:                         '<div style="font-size:10px;font-weight:bold;margin-bottom:2px;">' + productName.substring(0, 20) + '</div>' +
6212:                         '<svg id="print-barcode-' + product.id + '-' + i + '" style="width:100%;max-width:50mm;height:15mm;"></svg>' +
6213:                         '<div style="font-size:12px;font-weight:bold;margin-top:2px;">' + sellingPrice + ' ج.م</div></div>';
6214:                 }
6215:             });
6216:             
6217:             // Open print window
6218:             const printWindow = window.open('', '_blank', 'width=800,height=600');
6219:             
6220:             // Write content
6221:             printWindow.document.write('<!DOCTYPE html>');
6222:             printWindow.document.write('<html dir="rtl" lang="ar"><head><title>طباعة الباركود</title><meta charset="UTF-8"><style>');
6223:             printWindow.document.write('body { font-family: Arial, sans-serif; margin: 0; padding: 0; }');
6224:             printWindow.document.write('.barcode-sticker { width: 58mm; height: 38mm; margin: 0; padding: 2mm; border: 1px dashed #000; box-sizing: border-box; page-break-after: always; display: flex; flex-direction: column; align-items: center; justify-content: center; }');
6225:             printWindow.document.write('.barcode-sticker:last-child { page-break-after: auto; }');
6226:             printWindow.document.write('@media print { body { margin: 0; } .barcode-sticker { border: none; width: 58mm; height: 38mm; } }');
6227:             printWindow.document.write('</style>');
6228:             
6229:             // Include JsBarcode library in the print window
6230:             printWindow.document.write('<script src="https://cdn.jsdelivr.net/npm/jsbarcode@3.11.5/dist/JsBarcode.all.min.js"><\/script>');
6231:             
6232:             printWindow.document.write('</head><body>');
6233:             printWindow.document.write(barcodeItems);
6234:             
6235:             // Generate barcodes after content is written
6236:             printWindow.document.write('<scr' + 'ipt>');
6237:             printWindow.document.write('setTimeout(function() {');
6238:             
6239:             selectedProducts.forEach((product, idx) => {
6240:                 for (let i = 0; i < copies; i++) {
6241:                     printWindow.document.write('try { JsBarcode("#print-barcode-' + product.id + '-' + i + '", "' + barcodeValue + '", {format: "CODE128", width: 1.2, height: 30, displayValue: true, fontSize: 10, margin: 0}); } catch(e) { console.error("Barcode error:", e); }');
6242:                 }
6243:             });
6244:             
6245:             printWindow.document.write('setTimeout(function() { window.print(); }, 300);');
6246:             printWindow.document.write('}, 100);');
6247:             printWindow.document.write('</scr' + 'ipt>');

6253:         // Clear all product filters and show all products
6254:         function filterProducts() { 
6255:             const searchTerm = document.getElementById('productSearchFilter').value.toLowerCase();
6256:             const codeTerm = document.getElementById('productCodeFilter').value.toLowerCase();
6257:             const collectionFilter = document.getElementById('collectionFilter').value;
6258: 
6259:             const filteredProducts = products.filter(product => {
6260:                 console.log('Filtering product:', product.name);
6261:                 const matchesSearch = product.name.toLowerCase().includes(searchTerm);
6262:                 const matchesCode = product.code.toLowerCase().includes(codeTerm);
6263:                 const matchesCollection = !collectionFilter || product.collection === collectionFilter;
6264:                 return matchesSearch && matchesCode && matchesCollection;
6265:             });
6266: 
6267:             const tbody = document.getElementById('productsTableBody');
6268:             if (filteredProducts.length === 0) {
6269:                 tbody.innerHTML = '<tr><td colspan="10" style="text-align: center; padding: 50px; color: #666;">لا توجد منتجات تطابق البحث</td></tr>';
6270:                 return;
6271:             }
6272: 
6273:             tbody.innerHTML = filteredProducts.map(product => {
6274:                 const websiteProfit = (product.sellingPrice || 0) - (product.purchasePrice || 0);
6275:                 const profitColor = websiteProfit >= 0 ? 'var(--accent)' : 'var(--danger)';
6276:                 
6277:                 // Display colors info
6278:                 let colorsDisplay = '';
6279:                 if (product.colors && product.colors.length > 0) {
6280:                     colorsDisplay = product.colors.map(color => 
6281:                         `<span style="background: #e3f2fd; color: #1976d2; padding: 2px 8px; border-radius: 12px; font-size: 0.8rem; margin: 2px;">${color.name} (${color.quantity})</span>`
6282:                     ).join(' ');
6283:                 } else if (product.color) {
6284:                     // For backward compatibility with old products
6285:                     colorsDisplay = `<span style="background: #e3f2fd; color: #1976d2; padding: 2px 8px; border-radius: 12px; font-size: 0.8rem;">${product.color} (${product.quantity || 0})</span>`;
6286:                 }
6287: 
6288:                 const totalQuantity = product.totalQuantity || product.quantity || 0;
6289:                 
6290:                 return `
6291:                 <tr>
6292:                     <td style="padding: 15px; text-align: center;">
6293:                         <input type="checkbox" class="product-checkbox" value="${product.id}" style="transform: scale(1.2);">
6294:                     </td>
6295:                     <td style="padding: 15px;">
6296:                         <div style="display: flex; align-items: center; gap: 15px;">
6297:                             ${product.image ? '<img src="' + product.image + '" alt="' + product.name + '" class="img-thumb" onclick="showImageModal(\'' + product.image + '\', \'" + product.name + "\')">' : '<div style="width: 70px; height: 70px; background: #f0f0f0; border-radius: 10px; display: flex; align-items: center; justify-content: center; border: 2px solid #ddd;"><i class="fas fa-image" style="color: #ccc;"></i></div>'}
6298:                             <div>
6299:                                 <strong style="color: var(--primary); font-size: 0.95rem;">${product.name}</strong>
6300:                                 <br><div style="margin-top: 5px;">${colorsDisplay}</div>
6301:                             </div>
6302:                         </div>
6303:                     </td>
6304:                     <td style="padding: 15px;"><strong>${product.code}</strong></td>
6305:                     <td style="padding: 15px;">${product.barcode || '-'}</td>
6306:                     <td style="padding: 15px;">${product.collection}</td>
6307:                     <td style="padding: 15px; font-weight: bold; color: #e65100;">${product.purchasePrice || 0} جنيه</td>
6308:                     <td style="padding: 15px; font-weight: bold;">${product.sellingPrice} جنيه</td>
6309:                     <td style="padding: 15px; color: ${profitColor}; font-weight: bold;">${websiteProfit.toFixed(2)} جنيه</td>
6310:                     <td style="padding: 15px; font-weight: bold;">${totalQuantity}</td>

6313:                             <button onclick="openProductModal(${product.id})" class="btn btn-edit">

6325:         // Invoice Product Search Functions
6326:         function searchProducts() {
6327:             const searchTerm = document.getElementById('productSearchInput').value.toLowerCase();
6328:             const dropdown = document.getElementById('productDropdown');
6329:             const collectionFilter = document.getElementById('invoiceCollectionFilter').value;
6330:             
6331:             if (searchTerm.length < 1 && !collectionFilter) {
6332:                 dropdown.style.display = 'none';
6333:                 return;
6334:             }
6335: 
6336:             const filteredProducts = products.filter(product => {
6337:                 const matchesSearch = searchTerm.length < 1 || product.name.toLowerCase().includes(searchTerm) || 
6338:                                        product.code.toLowerCase().includes(searchTerm);
6339:                 const matchesCollection = !collectionFilter || product.collection === collectionFilter;
6340:                 return matchesSearch && matchesCollection;
6341:             });
6342: 
6343:             if (filteredProducts.length === 0) {
6344:                 dropdown.innerHTML = '<div style="padding: 15px; text-align: center; color: #666;">لا توجد منتجات تطابق البحث</div>';
6345:                 dropdown.style.display = 'block';
6346:                 return;
6347:             }
6348: 
6349:             dropdown.innerHTML = filteredProducts.map(product => {
6350:                 const totalQuantity = product.totalQuantity || product.quantity || 0;
6351:                 const hasMultipleColors = product.colors && product.colors.length > 1;
6352:                 
6353:                 return `
6354:                     <div onclick="selectProductForInvoice(${product.id})" style="padding: 15px; cursor: pointer; border-bottom: 1px solid #eee; display: flex; align-items: center; gap: 15px; transition: background 0.2s;" onmouseover="this.style.background='#f8f9fa'" onmouseout="this.style.background='white'">
6355:                         ${product.image ? 
6356:                             `<img src="${product.image}" alt="${product.name}" style="width: 60px; height: 60px; object-fit: cover; border-radius: 8px; border: 1px solid #ddd;">` : 
6357:                             '<div style="width: 60px; height: 60px; background: #f0f0f0; border-radius: 8px; display: flex; align-items: center; justify-content: center; border: 1px solid #ddd;"><i class="fas fa-image" style="color: #ccc;"></i></div>'
6358:                         }
6359:                         <div style="flex: 1;">
6360:                             <strong style="color: var(--primary); font-size: 0.95rem;">${product.name}</strong>
6361:                             <br><small style="color: #666;">كود: ${product.code}</small>
6362:                             <br><small style="color: #666;">المخزون: ${totalQuantity} قطعة</small>
6363:                             ${hasMultipleColors ? '<br><small style="color: var(--info); font-weight: bold;"><i class="fas fa-palette"></i> ألوان متعددة</small>' : ''}
6364:                         </div>
6365:                         <div style="text-align: right;">
6366:                             <strong style="color: var(--accent); font-size: 1.1rem;">${product.sellingPrice} جنيه</strong>
6367:                         </div>
6368:                     </div>
6369:                 `;
6370:             }).join('');
6371:             
6372:             dropdown.style.display = 'block';
6373:         }

6375:         function showProductDropdown() {
6376:             const dropdown = document.getElementById('productDropdown');
6377:             const searchInput = document.getElementById('productSearchInput');
6378:             const collectionFilter = document.getElementById('invoiceCollectionFilter').value;
6379:             
6380:             if (searchInput.value.length === 0) {
6381:                 // Show products filtered by collection when focused with empty search
6382:                 const filteredProducts = collectionFilter 
6383:                     ? products.filter(product => product.collection === collectionFilter)
6384:                     : products;
6385:                 
6386:                 dropdown.innerHTML = filteredProducts.map(product => {
6387:                     const totalQuantity = product.totalQuantity || product.quantity || 0;
6388:                     const hasMultipleColors = product.colors && product.colors.length > 1;
6389:                     
6390:                     return `
6391:                         <div onclick="selectProductForInvoice(${product.id})" style="padding: 15px; cursor: pointer; border-bottom: 1px solid #eee; display: flex; align-items: center; gap: 15px; transition: background 0.2s;" onmouseover="this.style.background='#f8f9fa'" onmouseout="this.style.background='white'">
6392:                             ${product.image ? 
6393:                                 `<img src="${product.image}" alt="${product.name}" style="width: 60px; height: 60px; object-fit: cover; border-radius: 8px; border: 1px solid #ddd;">` : 
6394:                                 '<div style="width: 60px; height: 60px; background: #f0f0f0; border-radius: 8px; display: flex; align-items: center; justify-content: center; border: 1px solid #ddd;"><i class="fas fa-image" style="color: #ccc;"></i></div>'
6395:                             }
6396:                             <div style="flex: 1;">
6397:                                 <strong style="color: var(--primary); font-size: 0.95rem;">${product.name}</strong>
6398:                                 <br><small style="color: #666;">كود: ${product.code}</small>
6399:                                 <br><small style="color: #666;">المخزون: ${totalQuantity} قطعة</small>
6400:                                 ${hasMultipleColors ? '<br><small style="color: var(--info); font-weight: bold;"><i class="fas fa-palette"></i> ألوان متعددة</small>' : ''}
6401:                             </div>
6402:                             <div style="text-align: right;">
6403:                                 <strong style="color: var(--accent); font-size: 1.1rem;">${product.sellingPrice} جنيه</strong>
6404:                             </div>

6406:                     `;
6407:                 }).join('');

6412:         function searchByProductCode() {
6413:             const code = (document.getElementById('productCodeInput').value || '').trim().toLowerCase();
6414:             const dropdown = document.getElementById('productDropdown');
6415:             const collectionFilter = document.getElementById('invoiceCollectionFilter').value;
6416:             
6417:             if (!code) {
6418:                 dropdown.style.display = 'none';
6419:                 return;
6420:             }
6421: 
6422:             // Try exact code match first (respecting collection filter)
6423:             const exact = products.find(p => (p.code || '').toLowerCase() === code && (!collectionFilter || p.collection === collectionFilter));
6424:             if (exact) {
6425:                 selectProductForInvoice(exact.id);
6426:                 return;
6427:             }
6428: 
6429:             // Fallback: partial matches by code or name (respecting collection filter)
6430:             const matches = products.filter(p => {
6431:                 const matchesCodeOrName = (p.code || '').toLowerCase().includes(code) || (p.name || '').toLowerCase().includes(code);
6432:                 const matchesCollection = !collectionFilter || p.collection === collectionFilter;
6433:                 return matchesCodeOrName && matchesCollection;
6434:             });
6435:             if (matches.length === 0) {
6436:                 dropdown.innerHTML = '<div style="padding: 15px; text-align: center; color: #666;">لا يوجد منتجات بهذا الكود</div>';
6437:                 dropdown.style.display = 'block';
6438:                 return;
6439:             }
6440: 
6441:             dropdown.innerHTML = matches.map(product => {
6442:                 const totalQuantity = product.totalQuantity || product.quantity || 0;
6443:                 const hasMultipleColors = product.colors && product.colors.length > 1;
6444:                 return `
6445:                     <div onclick="selectProductForInvoice(${product.id})" style="padding: 15px; cursor: pointer; border-bottom: 1px solid #eee; display: flex; align-items: center; gap: 15px; transition: background 0.2s;" onmouseover="this.style.background='#f8f9fa'" onmouseout="this.style.background='white'">
6446:                         ${product.image ? 
6447:                             `<img src="${product.image}" alt="${product.name}" style="width: 60px; height: 60px; object-fit: cover; border-radius: 8px; border: 1px solid #ddd;">` : 
6448:                             '<div style="width: 60px; height: 60px; background: #f0f0f0; border-radius: 8px; display: flex; align-items: center; justify-content: center; border: 1px solid #ddd;"><i class="fas fa-image" style="color: #ccc;"></i></div>'
6449:                         }
6450:                         <div style="flex: 1;">
6451:                             <strong style="color: var(--primary); font-size: 0.95rem;">${product.name}</strong>
6452:                             <br><small style="color: #666;">كود: ${product.code}</small>
6453:                             <br><small style="color: #666;">المخزون: ${totalQuantity} قطعة</small>
6454:                             ${hasMultipleColors ? '<br><small style="color: var(--info); font-weight: bold;"><i class="fas fa-palette"></i> ألوان متعددة</small>' : ''}
6455:                         </div>
6456:                         <div style="text-align: right;">
6457:                             <strong style="color: var(--accent); font-size: 1.1rem;">${product.sellingPrice} جنيه</strong>
6458:                         </div>
6459:                     </div>
6460:                 `;
6461:             }).join('');
6462:             dropdown.style.display = 'block';
6463:         }

6465:         function selectProductForInvoice(productId) {
6466:             const product = products.find(p => p.id === productId);
6467:             if (!product) return;
6468: 
6469:             document.getElementById('invoiceProduct').value = productId;
6470:             document.getElementById('invoiceSupplier').value = product.supplier || '';
6471:             document.getElementById('productSearchInput').value = product.name;
6472:             document.getElementById('productCodeInput').value = '';
6473:             document.getElementById('productDropdown').style.display = 'none';
6474:             
6475:             // Show selected product display
6476:             const display = document.getElementById('selectedProductDisplay');
6477:             const info = document.getElementById('selectedProductInfo');
6478:             // Check if discount is active
6479:             const activePrice = getActivePrice(product);
6480:             let priceDisplay = '';
6481:             if (activePrice.isDiscount) {
6482:                 priceDisplay = `<span style="text-decoration: line-through; color: #999; margin-right: 10px;">${activePrice.originalPrice} جنيه</span><span style="color: var(--danger); font-weight: bold;">${activePrice.price} جنيه (خصم)</span>`;
6483:             } else {
6484:                 priceDisplay = `<span style="color: var(--accent); font-weight: bold;">${activePrice.price} جنيه</span>`;
6485:             }
6486:             
6487:             info.innerHTML = `
6488:                 <div style="display: flex; align-items: center; gap: 15px;">
6489:                     ${product.image ? '<img src="' + product.image + '" style="width: 80px; height: 80px; object-fit: cover; border-radius: 10px; border: 2px solid var(--accent);">' : ''}
6490:                     <div style="flex: 1;">
6491:                         <h4 style="color: var(--primary); margin-bottom: 5px;">${product.name}</h4>
6492:                         <p style="margin: 0; color: #666;">كود: ${product.code}</p>
6493:                         <p style="margin: 5px 0; color: #666;">السعر: ${priceDisplay}</p>
6494:                         <p style="margin: 0; color: #666;">التاجر: ${product.supplier || '-'}</p>
6495:                         <p style="margin: 0; color: var(--accent); font-weight: bold;">المخزون: ${product.totalQuantity || product.quantity || 0} قطعة</p>
6496:                     </div>
6497:                 </div>
6498:             `;
6499:             display.style.display = 'flex';
6500: 
6501:             // Handle color selection if product has multiple colors
6502:             const colorDiv = document.getElementById('colorSelectionDiv');
6503:             const colorSelect = document.getElementById('productColorSelect');
6504:             
6505:             if (product.colors && product.colors.length > 1) {
6506:                 colorSelect.innerHTML = '<option value="">اختر اللون</option>';
6507:                 product.colors.forEach(color => {
6508:                     colorSelect.innerHTML += `<option value="${color.name}" data-quantity="${color.quantity}">${color.name} (${color.quantity} قطعة)</option>`;
6509:                 });
6510:                 colorDiv.style.display = 'block';
6511:                 colorSelect.required = true;
6512:             } else if (product.colors && product.colors.length === 1) {
6513:                 // Single color - auto select
6514:                 document.getElementById('selectedProductColor').value = product.colors[0].name;
6515:                 colorDiv.style.display = 'none';
6516:                 colorSelect.required = false;
6517:             } else {
6518:                 // Old format or no colors
6519:                 document.getElementById('selectedProductColor').value = product.color || '';
6520:                 colorDiv.style.display = 'none';
6521:                 colorSelect.required = false;
6522:             }
6523:         }

6525:         // Price Selection Functions
6526:         function updatePriceOptions() {
6527:             const platform = document.getElementById('salesPlatform').value;
6528:             const priceDiv = document.getElementById('priceSelectionDiv');
6529:             const priceSelect = document.getElementById('selectedPriceOption');
6530:             const productId = document.getElementById('invoiceProduct').value;
6531:             
6532:             if (!platform || !productId) {
6533:                 priceDiv.style.display = 'none';
6534:                 return;
6535:             }
6536:             
6537:             const product = products.find(p => p.id == productId);
6538:             if (!product) return;
6539:             
6540:             // Clear existing options
6541:             priceSelect.innerHTML = '<option value="">اختر السعر</option>';
6542:             
6543:             // Get base price
6544:             const basePrice = product.sellingPrice || 0;
6545:             
6546:             // Always add base price option
6547:             priceSelect.innerHTML += `<option value="base">السعر الأساسي - ${basePrice} جنيه</option>`;
6548:             
6549:             // Check for platform-specific discount price
6550:             let discountPrice = null;
6551:             if (product.platformDiscounts && product.platformDiscounts.length > 0) {
6552:                 discountPrice = product.platformDiscounts.find(d => d.platform === platform);
6553:             }
6554:             
6555:             // Add discount price if exists
6556:             if (discountPrice && discountPrice.price > 0) {
6557:                 priceSelect.innerHTML += `<option value="discount">سعر ${platform === 'website' ? 'الموقع' : (platform === 'jumia' ? 'جوميا' : 'نون')} - ${discountPrice.price} جنيه</option>`;
6558:             }
6559:             
6560:             // Add platform original price (jumiaPrice/noonPrice) if different from base
6561:             if (platform === 'jumia' && product.jumiaPrice && product.jumiaPrice > 0 && product.jumiaPrice !== basePrice) {
6562:                 priceSelect.innerHTML += `<option value="jumia_orig">سعر جوميا الأصلي - ${product.jumiaPrice} جنيه</option>`;
6563:             }
6564:             if (platform === 'noon' && product.noonPrice && product.noonPrice > 0 && product.noonPrice !== basePrice) {
6565:                 priceSelect.innerHTML += `<option value="noon_orig">سعر نون الأصلي - ${product.noonPrice} جنيه</option>`;
6566:             }
6567:             
6568:             priceDiv.style.display = 'block';
6569:         }

6615: 
6616:         function calculateItemTotals(product, quantity, platform) {
6617:             let unitPrice = product.sellingPrice || 0;
6618:             let grossTotal = unitPrice * quantity;
6619:             let commission = 0;
6620:             let shipping = 0;
6621:             let isDiscountApplied = false;
6622: 
6623:             // Get platform-specific price (final selling price) if available
6624:             let platformPrice = null;
6625:             if (product.platformDiscounts && product.platformDiscounts.length > 0) {
6626:                 platformPrice = product.platformDiscounts.find(d => d.platform === platform);
6627:             }
6628: 
6629:             // Apply platform-specific price (final selling price)
6630:             if (platformPrice && platformPrice.price > 0) {
6631:                 unitPrice = platformPrice.price;
6632:                 grossTotal = unitPrice * quantity;
6633:                 isDiscountApplied = true;
6634:             }
6635: 
6636:             // For noon/jumia platforms, calculate commission and shipping on the final price
6637:             if (platform === 'noon') {
6638:                 commission = grossTotal * (product.noonCommission || 0) / 100;
6639:                 shipping = (product.noonShipping || 0) * quantity;
6640:             } else if (platform === 'jumia') {
6641:                 commission = grossTotal * (product.jumiaCommission || 0) / 100;
6642:                 shipping = (product.jumiaShipping || 0) * quantity;
6643:             }
6644: 
6645:             const netTotal = grossTotal - commission - shipping;
6646:             return { unitPrice, grossTotal, commission, shipping, netTotal, isDiscountApplied };
6647:         }
6648: 
6649:         function normalizeInvoiceItems(invoice) {
6650:             if (invoice && Array.isArray(invoice.items) && invoice.items.length > 0) {
6651:                 return invoice.items.map(i => ({ ...i }));
6652:             }
6653:             if (!invoice || !invoice.productId) return [];
6654:             return [{
6655:                 productId: invoice.productId,
6656:                 selectedColor: invoice.selectedColor || '',
6657:                 quantity: invoice.quantity || 0,
6658:                 unitPrice: invoice.unitPrice || 0,
6659:                 grossTotal: invoice.grossTotal || 0,
6660:                 commission: invoice.commission || 0,
6661:                 shipping: invoice.shipping || 0,
6662:                 netTotal: invoice.netTotal || invoice.total || 0
6663:             }];
6664:         }
6665: 
6666:         function getInvoiceRevenue(invoice) {
6667:             const items = normalizeInvoiceItems(invoice);
6668:             if (!items.length) return invoice.total || invoice.netTotal || 0;
6669:             return items.reduce((sum, item) => sum + (parseFloat(item.netTotal || 0)), 0);
6670:         }
6671: 
6672:         function renderInvoiceItems() {
6673:             const area = document.getElementById('invoiceItemsArea');
6674:             const list = document.getElementById('invoiceItemsList');
6675:             const totalEl = document.getElementById('invoiceItemsTotal');
6676:             const sortEl = document.getElementById('invoiceItemsSort');
6677:             const productField = document.getElementById('invoiceProduct');
6678:             const qtyField = document.getElementById('invoiceQuantity');
6679: 
6680:             if (!currentInvoiceItems || currentInvoiceItems.length === 0) {
6681:                 area.style.display = 'none';
6682:                 list.innerHTML = '';
6683:                 totalEl.textContent = '0.00';
6684:                 if (productField) productField.required = true;
6685:                 if (qtyField) qtyField.required = true;
6686:                 return;
6687:             }
6688: 
6689:             const sortValue = sortEl ? sortEl.value : 'name';
6690:             const sortedItems = [...currentInvoiceItems].sort((a, b) => {
6691:                 const productA = products.find(p => p.id == a.productId);
6692:                 const productB = products.find(p => p.id == b.productId);
6693:                 const nameA = (productA ? productA.name : '').toString();
6694:                 const nameB = (productB ? productB.name : '').toString();
6695:                 const priceA = parseFloat(a.unitPrice || 0);
6696:                 const priceB = parseFloat(b.unitPrice || 0);
6697:                 if (sortValue === 'price_desc') return priceB - priceA;
6698:                 if (sortValue === 'price_asc') return priceA - priceB;
6699:                 return nameA.localeCompare(nameB, 'ar');
6700:             });
6701: 
6702:             let total = 0;
6703:             const rowsHtml = sortedItems.map((item, idx) => {
6704:                 const product = products.find(p => p.id == item.productId);
6705:                 const colorInfo = item.selectedColor ? ` - ${item.selectedColor}` : '';
6706:                 const lineTotal = parseFloat(item.netTotal || 0);
6707:                 total += lineTotal;
6708:                 return `
6709:                     <tr>
6710:                         <td>${product ? product.name : 'منتج محذوف'}${colorInfo}</td>
6711:                         <td style="text-align:center;">${item.quantity || 0}</td>
6712:                         <td style="text-align:center;">${parseFloat(item.unitPrice || 0).toFixed(2)}</td>
6713:                         <td style="text-align:center; font-weight:600; color: var(--accent);">${lineTotal.toFixed(2)}</td>
6714:                         <td style="text-align:center;">
6715:                             <button type="button" onclick="removeInvoiceItem(${idx})" class="btn btn-delete" style="padding:6px 10px;">
6716:                                 <i class="fas fa-trash"></i>
6717:                             </button>
6718:                         </td>
6719:                     </tr>
6720:                 `;
6721:             }).join('');
6722: 
6723:             list.innerHTML = `
6724:                 <div style="overflow:auto;">
6725:                     <table style="width:100%; border-collapse: collapse;">
6726:                         <thead>
6727:                             <tr style="background:#f8f9fa;">
6728:                                 <th style="padding:8px; text-align:right; border-bottom:1px solid #eee;">المنتج</th>
6729:                                 <th style="padding:8px; text-align:center; border-bottom:1px solid #eee;">الكمية</th>
6730:                                 <th style="padding:8px; text-align:center; border-bottom:1px solid #eee;">السعر</th>
6731:                                 <th style="padding:8px; text-align:center; border-bottom:1px solid #eee;">الإجمالي</th>
6732:                                 <th style="padding:8px; text-align:center; border-bottom:1px solid #eee;">حذف</th>
6733:                             </tr>
6734:                         </thead>
6735:                         <tbody>
6736:                             ${rowsHtml}
6737:                         </tbody>
6738:                     </table>
6739:                 </div>
6740:             `;
6741: 
6742:             totalEl.textContent = total.toFixed(2);
6743:             area.style.display = 'block';
6744:             if (productField) productField.required = false;
6745:             if (qtyField) qtyField.required = false;
6746:         }
6747: 
6748:         function addInvoiceItem() {
6749:             console.log('=== ADD INVOICE ITEM STARTED ===');
6750:             
6751:             const productId = parseInt(document.getElementById('invoiceProduct').value);
6752:             const quantity = parseInt(document.getElementById('invoiceQuantity').value);
6753:             const platform = document.getElementById('salesPlatform').value;
6754:             const selectedColor = document.getElementById('selectedProductColor').value;
6755:             const priceOption = document.getElementById('selectedPriceOption').value;
6756:             const customPrice = parseFloat(document.getElementById('customUnitPrice').value) || 0;
6757:             
6758:             console.log('Add item fields:', { productId, quantity, platform, priceOption, customPrice });
6759:             
6760:             if (!productId) { showErrorMessage('اختر منتج أولاً'); return; }
6761:             if (!platform) { showErrorMessage('اختر منصة البيع أولاً'); return; }
6762:             if (!priceOption && !customPrice) { showErrorMessage('اختر السعر أو أدخل سعر مخصص'); return; }
6763:             if (!quantity || quantity < 1) { showErrorMessage('اختر كمية صحيحة'); return; }
6764: 
6765:             const product = products.find(p => p.id == productId);
6766:             if (!product) { showErrorMessage('المنتج غير موجود'); return; }
6767: 
6768:             let availableQuantity = 0;
6769:             if (product.colors && product.colors.length > 0) {
6770:                 if (!selectedColor) { showErrorMessage('يرجى اختيار اللون'); return; }
6771:                 const colorData = product.colors.find(c => c.name === selectedColor);
6772:                 availableQuantity = colorData ? colorData.quantity : 0;
6773:             } else {
6774:                 availableQuantity = product.totalQuantity || product.quantity || 0;
6775:             }
6776: 
6777:             const alreadySelected = currentInvoiceItems
6778:                 .filter(i => i.productId == productId && (i.selectedColor || '') === (selectedColor || ''))
6779:                 .reduce((sum, i) => sum + (parseInt(i.quantity) || 0), 0);
6780:             const remaining = availableQuantity - alreadySelected;
6781: 
6782:             if (quantity > remaining) {
6783:                 showErrorMessage(`الكمية المطلوبة غير متوفرة. المتاح: ${remaining} قطعة`);
6784:                 return;
6785:             }
6786: 
6787:             // Calculate price - Custom price has highest priority
6788:             let unitPrice = 0;
6789:             if (customPrice > 0) {
6790:                 unitPrice = customPrice;
6791:             } else if (priceOption === 'discount') {
6792:                 if (product.platformDiscounts && product.platformDiscounts.length > 0) {
6793:                     const platformDiscount = product.platformDiscounts.find(d => d.platform === platform);
6794:                     if (platformDiscount && platformDiscount.price > 0) {
6795:                         unitPrice = platformDiscount.price;
6796:                     }
6797:                 }
6798:             } else if (priceOption === 'amazon_orig' && product.amazonPrice) {
6799:                 unitPrice = product.amazonPrice;
6800:             } else if (priceOption === 'jumia_orig' && product.jumiaPrice) {
6801:                 unitPrice = product.jumiaPrice;
6802:             } else if (priceOption === 'noon_orig' && product.noonPrice) {
6803:                 unitPrice = product.noonPrice;
6804:             } else if (priceOption === 'other_orig' && product.otherPrice) {
6805:                 unitPrice = product.otherPrice;
6806:             } else {
6807:                 // Default to platform specific price if set, otherwise sellingPrice
6808:                 if (platform === 'amazon' && product.amazonPrice > 0) unitPrice = product.amazonPrice;
6809:                 else if (platform === 'noon' && product.noonPrice > 0) unitPrice = product.noonPrice;
6810:                 else if (platform === 'jumia' && product.jumiaPrice > 0) unitPrice = product.jumiaPrice;
6811:                 else if ((platform === 'tiktok' || platform === 'other') && product.otherPrice > 0) unitPrice = product.otherPrice;
6812:                 else unitPrice = product.sellingPrice || 0;
6813:             }
6814: 
6815:             const grossTotal = unitPrice * quantity;
6816:             let commission = 0;
6817:             let shipping = 0;
6818:             
6819:             // Calculate commission and shipping for marketplace platforms
6820:             if (platform === 'noon') {
6821:                 commission = grossTotal * (product.noonCommission || 0) / 100;
6822:                 shipping = (product.noonShipping || 0) * quantity;
6823:             } else if (platform === 'jumia') {
6824:                 commission = grossTotal * (product.jumiaCommission || 0) / 100;
6825:                 shipping = (product.jumiaShipping || 0) * quantity;
6826:             }
6827: 
6828:             const netTotal = grossTotal - commission - shipping;
6829: 
6830:             const existingIndex = currentInvoiceItems.findIndex(i => i.productId == productId && (i.selectedColor || '') === (selectedColor || ''));
6831:             if (existingIndex >= 0) {
6832:                 const newQty = (parseInt(currentInvoiceItems[existingIndex].quantity) || 0) + quantity;
6833:                 const newGrossTotal = unitPrice * newQty;
6834:                 let newCommission = 0;
6835:                 let newShipping = 0;
6836:                 if (platform === 'noon') {
6837:                     newCommission = newGrossTotal * (product.noonCommission || 0) / 100;
6838:                     newShipping = (product.noonShipping || 0) * newQty;
6839:                 } else if (platform === 'jumia') {
6840:                     newCommission = newGrossTotal * (product.jumiaCommission || 0) / 100;
6841:                     newShipping = (product.jumiaShipping || 0) * newQty;
6842:                 }
6843:                 currentInvoiceItems[existingIndex] = {
6844:                     ...currentInvoiceItems[existingIndex],
6845:                     quantity: newQty,
6846:                     unitPrice: unitPrice,
6847:                     grossTotal: newGrossTotal,
6848:                     commission: newCommission,
6849:                     shipping: newShipping,
6850:                     netTotal: newGrossTotal - newCommission - newShipping
6851:                 };
6852:             } else {
6853:                 currentInvoiceItems.push({
6854:                     productId,
6855:                     selectedColor: selectedColor || '',
6856:                     quantity,
6857:                     unitPrice: unitPrice,
6858:                     grossTotal: grossTotal,
6859:                     commission: commission,
6860:                     shipping: shipping,
6861:                     netTotal: netTotal
6862:                 });
6863:             }
6864: 
6865:             renderInvoiceItems();
6866:             clearSelectedProduct();
6867:         }
6868: 
6869:         function removeInvoiceItem(index) {
6870:             currentInvoiceItems.splice(index, 1);
6871:             renderInvoiceItems();
6872:         }

6874:         function renderExpenseItems() {
6875:             const area = document.getElementById('expenseItemsArea');
6876:             const list = document.getElementById('expenseItemsList');
6877:             const totalEl = document.getElementById('expenseItemsTotal');
6878:             const typeField = document.getElementById('expenseType');
6879:             const customField = document.getElementById('expenseTypeCustom');
6880:             const amountField = document.getElementById('expenseAmount');
6881:             const descField = document.getElementById('expenseDescription');
6882:             const dateField = document.getElementById('expenseDate');
6883: 
6884:             if (!currentExpenseItems || currentExpenseItems.length === 0) {
6885:                 area.style.display = 'none';
6886:                 list.innerHTML = '';
6887:                 totalEl.textContent = '0.00';
6888:                 if (typeField) typeField.required = true;
6889:                 if (customField) customField.required = false;
6890:                 if (amountField) amountField.required = true;
6891:                 if (descField) descField.required = true;
6892:                 if (dateField) dateField.required = true;
6893:                 return;
6894:             }
6895: 
6896:             let total = 0;
6897:             const rowsHtml = currentExpenseItems.map((item, idx) => {
6898:                 const lineTotal = parseFloat(item.amount || 0);
6899:                 total += lineTotal;
6900:                 return `
6901:                     <tr>
6902:                         <td>${item.type}</td>
6903:                         <td style="text-align:center;">${item.description || '-'}</td>
6904:                         <td style="text-align:center;">${new Date(item.date).toLocaleDateString('ar-EG')}</td>
6905:                         <td style="text-align:center; font-weight:600; color: var(--danger);">${lineTotal.toFixed(2)}</td>
6906:                         <td style="text-align:center;">
6907:                             <button type="button" onclick="removeExpenseItem(${idx})" class="btn btn-delete" style="padding:6px 10px;">
6908:                                 <i class="fas fa-trash"></i>
6909:                             </button>
6910:                         </td>
6911:                     </tr>
6912:                 `;
6913:             }).join('');
6914: 
6915:             list.innerHTML = `
6916:                 <div style="overflow:auto;">
6917:                     <table style="width:100%; border-collapse: collapse;">
6918:                         <thead>
6919:                             <tr style="background:#f8f9fa;">
6920:                                 <th style="padding:8px; text-align:right; border-bottom:1px solid #eee;">النوع</th>
6921:                                 <th style="padding:8px; text-align:center; border-bottom:1px solid #eee;">الوصف</th>
6922:                                 <th style="padding:8px; text-align:center; border-bottom:1px solid #eee;">التاريخ</th>
6923:                                 <th style="padding:8px; text-align:center; border-bottom:1px solid #eee;">المبلغ</th>
6924:                                 <th style="padding:8px; text-align:center; border-bottom:1px solid #eee;">حذف</th>
6925:                             </tr>
6926:                         </thead>
6927:                         <tbody>
6928:                             ${rowsHtml}
6929:                         </tbody>
6930:                     </table>
6931:                 </div>
6932:             `;
6933: 
6934:             totalEl.textContent = total.toFixed(2);
6935:             area.style.display = 'block';
6936:             if (typeField) typeField.required = false;
6937:             if (customField) customField.required = false;
6938:             if (amountField) amountField.required = false;
6939:             if (descField) descField.required = false;
6940:             if (dateField) dateField.required = false;
6941:         }

7089:         function loadInvoices(page) {
7090:             if (page) invoicesPage = page;
7091:             const tbody = document.getElementById('invoicesTableBody');
7092:             if (invoices.length === 0) {
7093:                 tbody.innerHTML = '<tr><td colspan="8" style="text-align: center; padding: 50px; color: #666;"><i class="fas fa-file-invoice" style="font-size: 3rem; margin-bottom: 15px; opacity: 0.3;"></i><br>لا توجد فواتير حالياً</td></tr>';
7094:                 renderPagination(invoices.length, invoicesPage, 'invoicesPagination', 'loadInvoices');
7095:                 return;
7096:             }
7097: 
7098:             // Calculate pagination
7099:             const startIndex = (invoicesPage - 1) * itemsPerPage;
7100:             const endIndex = startIndex + itemsPerPage;
7101:             const paginatedInvoices = invoices.slice(startIndex, endIndex);
7102:             
7103:             tbody.innerHTML = paginatedInvoices.map(invoice => {
7104:                 const items = normalizeInvoiceItems(invoice);
7105:                 const firstItem = items[0];
7106:                 const product = firstItem ? products.find(p => p.id == firstItem.productId) : null;
7107:                 const displayTotal = invoice.netTotal || invoice.total || 0;
7108:                 const colorInfo = firstItem && firstItem.selectedColor ? ` - ${firstItem.selectedColor}` : '';
7109:                 const extraCount = items.length > 1 ? ` + ${items.length - 1} منتج` : '';
7110:                 // حالة التوصيل
7111:                 const deliveryStatus = invoice.deliveryStatus || 'pending';
7112:                 let statusBadge = '';
7113:                 let statusColor = '';
7114:                 
7115:                 if (deliveryStatus === 'delivered') {
7116:                     statusBadge = '<span style="background: #28a745; color: white; padding: 5px 10px; border-radius: 20px; font-size: 0.9rem;">✓ تم التوصيل</span>';
7117:                     statusColor = 'var(--accent)';
7118:                 } else if (deliveryStatus === 'not_delivered') {
7119:                     statusBadge = '<span style="background: #dc3545; color: white; padding: 5px 10px; border-radius: 20px; font-size: 0.9rem;">✗ لم يتم</span>';
7120:                     statusColor = 'var(--danger)';
7121:                 } else {
7122:                     statusBadge = '<span style="background: #ffc107; color: #000; padding: 5px 10px; border-radius: 20px; font-size: 0.9rem;">⏳ قيد الانتظار</span>';
7123:                     statusColor = 'var(--warning)';
7124:                 }
7125: 
7126:                 return `
7127:                     <tr>
7128:                         <td style="padding: 15px; text-align: center;">
7129:                             <input type="checkbox" class="invoice-checkbox" value="${invoice.id}" style="transform: scale(1.2);">
7130:                         </td>
7131:                         <td style="padding: 15px;"><strong>#${invoice.id}</strong></td>
7132:                         <td style="padding: 15px;">${invoice.customerName}</td>
7133:                         <td style="padding: 15px;">${product ? product.name + colorInfo + extraCount : 'منتج محذوف'}</td>
7134:                         <td style="padding: 15px;"><small>${getShippingCompanyName(invoice.shippingCompany)}</small></td>
7135:                         <td style="padding: 15px; font-weight: bold; color: var(--accent);">${displayTotal.toFixed(2)} جنيه</td>
7136:                         <td style="padding: 15px; text-align: center;">${statusBadge}</td>
7137:                         <td style="padding: 15px;">${new Date(invoice.date).toLocaleDateString('ar-EG')}</td>
7138:                         <td style="padding: 15px; text-align: center;">
7139:                             ${deliveryStatus === 'pending' ? `
7140:                                 <button onclick="confirmDelivery(${invoice.id})" class="btn" title="تأكيد التوصيل" style="padding:6px 10px; font-size:0.9rem; margin:0 6px; display:inline-block;">
7141:                                     <i class="fas fa-check"></i>
7142:                                 </button>
7143:                                 <button onclick="markNotDelivered(${invoice.id})" class="btn" title="لم يتم التوصيل" style="padding:6px 10px; font-size:0.9rem; margin:0 6px; display:inline-block; background:#dc3545; color:#fff;">
7144:                                     <i class="fas fa-times"></i>
7145:                                 </button>
7146:                             ` : ''}
7147:                             <button onclick="previewInvoice(${invoice.id})" class="btn btn-print" style="padding:6px 10px; font-size:0.9rem; margin:0 6px; display:inline-block;">
7148:                                 <i class="fas fa-eye"></i>
7149:                             </button>
7150:                             <button onclick="printInvoiceById(${invoice.id})" class="btn btn-primary" title="طباعة" style="padding:6px 10px; font-size:0.9rem; margin:0 6px; display:inline-block;">
7151:                                 <i class="fas fa-print"></i>
7152:                             </button>
7153:                             <button onclick="sendInvoiceToWhatsApp(${invoice.id})" title="إرسال واتساب" style="padding:6px 10px; font-size:0.9rem; margin:0 6px; display:inline-block; background:#25D366; color:#fff; border:none; border-radius:4px; cursor:pointer;">
7154:                                 <i class="fab fa-whatsapp"></i>
7155:                             </button>
7156:                             <button onclick="exportInvoiceAsPDF(${invoice.id})" class="btn" title="حفظ PDF" style="padding:6px 10px; font-size:0.9rem; margin:0 6px; display:inline-block; background:#c82333; color:#fff;">
7157:                                 <i class="fas fa-file-pdf"></i>
7158:                             </button>
7159:                             <button onclick="openInvoiceModal(${invoice.id})" class="btn btn-edit" style="padding:6px 10px; font-size:0.9rem; margin:0 6px; display:inline-block;">
7160:                                 <i class="fas fa-edit"></i>
7161:                             </button>
7162:                             <button onclick="deleteInvoice(${invoice.id})" class="btn btn-delete" style="padding:6px 10px; font-size:0.9rem; margin:0 6px; display:inline-block; background:#dc3545; color:#fff;">
7163:                                 <i class="fas fa-trash"></i>
7164:                             </button>
7165:                         </td>
7166:                     </tr>
7167:                 `;
7168:             }).join('');
7169:             
7170:             // Render pagination
7171:             renderPagination(invoices.length, invoicesPage, 'invoicesPagination', 'loadInvoices');
7172:         }
7173: 
7174:         function deleteInvoice(id) {
7175:             const invoice = invoices.find(i => i.id === id);
7176:             if (!invoice) {
7177:                 showErrorMessage('الفاتورة غير موجودة');
7178:                 return;
7179:             }
7180: 
7181:             const product = products.find(p => p.id == invoice.productId);
7182:             const productName = product ? product.name : 'منتج محذوف';
7183:             const colorInfo = invoice.selectedColor ? ` - ${invoice.selectedColor}` : '';
7184: 
7185:             if (confirm(`هل أنت متأكد من حذف هذه الفاتورة؟\n\nالفاتورة: #${invoice.id}\nالعميل: ${invoice.customerName}\nالمنتج: ${productName}${colorInfo}\nالكمية: ${invoice.quantity}\n\n⚠️ سيتم إرجاع الكمية للمخزون تلقائياً`)) {
7186:                 if (product) {
7187:                     // Return quantity to stock
7188:                     returnQuantityToStock(invoice.productId, invoice.selectedColor, invoice.quantity);
7189:                 }
7190:                 
7191:                 // Store invoice data before deletion for recalculation
7192:                 const invoiceTotal = invoice.total || 0;
7193:                 const wasDelivered = invoice.deliveryStatus === 'delivered';
7194:                 
7195:                 invoices = invoices.filter(i => i.id !== id);
7196:                 saveData();
7197:                 loadInvoices();
7198:                 loadProducts();
7199:                 
7200:                 // If the deleted invoice was delivered, adjust capital
7201:                 if (wasDelivered) {
7202:                     // Calculate the profit that was added when delivery was confirmed
7203:                     const items = invoice.items || [invoice];
7204:                     let invoiceProfit = 0;
7205:                     items.forEach(item => {
7206:                         const unitPrice = item.unitPrice || 0;
7207:                         const quantity = item.quantity || 0;
7208:                         const sellingAmount = unitPrice * quantity;
7209:                         const commission = sellingAmount * (invoice.commissionPercent || 0) / 100;
7210:                         const shipping = (invoice.shipping || 0);
7211:                         const tax = sellingAmount * 0.14;
7212:                         invoiceProfit += (sellingAmount - commission - shipping - tax);
7213:                     });
7214:                     
7215:                     // Subtract the profit from capital
7216:                     capitalAmount -= invoiceProfit;
7217:                     localStorage.setItem('hances_capital', capitalAmount.toString());
7218:                 }
7219:                 
7220:                 updateDashboard();
7221:                 showSuccessMessage('تم حذف الفاتورة وإرجاع الكمية للمخزون بنجاح!');
7222:             }
7223:         }
7224: 
7225:         // Delivery Management (top-level)
7226:         function confirmDelivery(id) {
7227:             const invoice = invoices.find(i => i.id === id);
7228:             if (!invoice) return;
7229: 
7230:             if (confirm('هل تريد تأكيد توصيل هذا الأوردر؟\nسيتم إضافة المبلغ للمدفوعات المعلقة')) {
7231:                 invoice.deliveryStatus = 'delivered';
7232:                 
7233:                 // حساب إجمالي المبلغ من الفاتورة
7234:                 const invoiceTotal = invoice.total || invoice.netTotal || 0;
7235:                 const platform = invoice.platform || 'website';
7236:                 
7237:                 // إضافة للمدفوعات المعلقة للمنصة المناسبة
7238:                 const payment = {
7239:                     id: Date.now().toString(),
7240:                     platform: platform,
7241:                     invoiceNumber: invoice.id,
7242:                     amount: invoiceTotal,
7243:                     deliveryStatus: 'delivered',
7244:                     date: new Date().toISOString(),
7245:                     notes: 'فاتورة من تأكيد التوصيل',
7246:                     status: 'pending',
7247:                     createdAt: new Date().toISOString()
7248:                 };
7249:                 
7250:                 platformPayments.push(payment);
7251:                 savePlatformData();
7252:                 
7253:                 saveData();
7254:                 loadInvoices();
7255:                 loadPlatformPayments();
7256:                 updateDashboard();
7257:                 showSuccessMessage('✓ تم تأكيد التوصيل بنجاح! تم إضافة ' + invoiceTotal.toFixed(2) + ' جنيه للمدفوعات المعلقة');
7258:             }
7259:         }
7260: 
7261:         function markNotDelivered(id) {
7262:             const invoice = invoices.find(i => i.id === id);
7263:             if (!invoice) return;
7264: 
7265:             const product = products.find(p => p.id == invoice.productId);
7266:             if (!product) return;
7267: 
7268:             if (confirm('هل تريد تحديد هذا الأوردر كـ "لم يتم التوصيل"؟\nسيتم إرجاع المنتج للمخزون وإنشاء مرتجع تلقائياً')) {
7269:                 // Return quantity to stock
7270:                 returnQuantityToStock(invoice.productId, invoice.selectedColor, invoice.quantity);
7271: 
7272:                 // Calculate losses for automatic return
7273:                 const perUnitCommission = invoice.quantity ? (invoice.commission || 0) / invoice.quantity : 0;
7274:                 const perUnitShipping = invoice.quantity ? (invoice.shipping || 0) / invoice.quantity : 0;
7275:                 const commissionLoss = perUnitCommission * invoice.quantity;
7276:                 const shippingLoss = perUnitShipping * invoice.quantity;
7277:                 const purchaseLoss = (product.purchasePrice || 0) * invoice.quantity;
7278:                 const totalLoss = purchaseLoss + commissionLoss + shippingLoss;
7279: 
7280:                 // Create automatic return with loss calculation
7281:                 const newReturn = {
7282:                     id: Date.now(),
7283:                     invoiceId: invoice.id,
7284:                     productId: invoice.productId,
7285:                     customerName: invoice.customerName,
7286:                     selectedColor: invoice.selectedColor,
7287:                     quantity: invoice.quantity,
7288:                     amount: (invoice.total || invoice.netTotal || 0),
7289:                     reason: 'لم يتم التوصيل',
7290:                     platform: invoice.platform || '',
7291:                     refunded: false,
7292:                     receivedByShipper: false,
7293:                     commissionLoss,
7294:                     shippingLoss,
7295:                     purchaseLoss,
7296:                     totalLoss,
7297:                     date: new Date().toISOString()
7298:                 };
7299:                 returns.push(newReturn);
7300: 
7301:                 // Update invoice status
7302:                 invoice.deliveryStatus = 'not_delivered';
7303: 
7304:                 saveData();
7305:                 loadInvoices();
7306:                 loadReturns();
7307:                 loadProducts();
7308:                 updateDashboard();
7309:                 showSuccessMessage('✗ تم تحديد الأوردر كـ "لم يتم التوصيل" وتم إرجاع المنتج للمخزون وإنشاء مرتجع تلقائياً');
7310:             }
