7340:         let purchaseItems = [];
7341:         
7342:         function addPurchaseItem() {
7343:             const supplier = document.getElementById('itemSupplier').value || document.getElementById('purchaseSupplier').value;
7344:             const productId = parseInt(document.getElementById('itemProduct').value);
7345:             const color = document.getElementById('itemColor').value;
7346:             const quantity = parseInt(document.getElementById('itemQuantity').value) || 1;
7347:             const price = parseFloat(document.getElementById('itemPrice').value);
7348:             
7349:             if (!supplier || !productId || !color || !quantity || !price) {
7350:                 showErrorMessage('يرجى ملء جميع بيانات المنتج (بما في ذلك اللون والمورد)');
7351:                 return;
7352:             }
7353:             
7354:             const product = products.find(p => p.id === productId);
7355:             if (!product) {
7356:                 showErrorMessage('المنتج غير موجود');
7357:                 return;
7358:             }
7359:             
7360:             // Check if product with same color already exists in items
7361:             const existingIndex = purchaseItems.findIndex(item => item.productId === productId && item.color === color);
7362:             if (existingIndex !== -1) {
7363:                 // Update quantity
7364:                 purchaseItems[existingIndex].quantity += quantity;
7365:                 purchaseItems[existingIndex].total = purchaseItems[existingIndex].quantity * purchaseItems[existingIndex].price;
7366:             } else {
7367:                 // Add new item
7368:                 purchaseItems.push({
7369:                     supplier: supplier,
7370:                     productId: productId,
7371:                     productName: product.name,
7372:                     productCode: product.code,
7373:                     color: color,
7374:                     quantity: quantity,
7375:                     price: price,
7376:                     total: price * quantity
7377:                 });
7378:             }
7379:             
7380:             // Clear inputs
7381:             document.getElementById('itemProduct').value = '';
7382:             document.getElementById('itemColor').innerHTML = '<option value="">-- اختر اللون --</option>';
7383:             document.getElementById('itemQuantity').value = '1';
7384:             document.getElementById('itemPrice').value = '';
7385:             
7386:             renderPurchaseItems();
7387:         }
7388:         
7389:         function loadProductColors(productId) {
7390:             const colorSelect = document.getElementById('itemColor');
7391:             colorSelect.innerHTML = '<option value="">-- اختر اللون --</option>';
7392:             
7393:             if (!productId) return;
7394:             
7395:             // Handle both string and number productId
7396:             const productIdNum = parseInt(productId);
7397:             const product = products.find(p => p.id === productId || p.id === productIdNum);
7398:             
7399:             if (!product) {
7400:                 console.log('Product not found for ID:', productId, 'Available products:', products.map(p => ({id: p.id, name: p.name})));
7401:                 return;
7402:             }
7403:             
7404:             console.log('Found product:', product.name, 'Colors:', product.colors);
7405:             
7406:             // Check if product has colors array
7407:             if (product.colors && Array.isArray(product.colors) && product.colors.length > 0) {
7408:                 product.colors.forEach(color => {
7409:                     const option = document.createElement('option');
7410:                     option.value = color.name || color;
7411:                     option.textContent = color.name || color;
7412:                     if (color.quantity !== undefined && color.quantity !== null) {
7413:                         option.textContent += ' (' + color.quantity + ' متاح)';
7414:                     }
7415:                     colorSelect.appendChild(option);
7416:                 });
7417:             } else if (product.color) {
7418:                 // Old format - single color
7419:                 const option = document.createElement('option');
7420:                 option.value = product.color;
7421:                 option.textContent = product.color;
7422:                 colorSelect.appendChild(option);
7423:             }
7424:         }

7428:             renderPurchaseItems();
7429:         }
7430:         
7431:         function renderPurchaseItems() {
7432:             const tbody = document.getElementById('purchaseItemsList');
7433:             
7434:             if (purchaseItems.length === 0) {
7435:                 tbody.innerHTML = '<tr id="noItemsRow"><td colspan="8" style="padding: 30px; text-align: center; color: #999;"><i class="fas fa-shopping-cart" style="font-size: 2rem; margin-bottom: 10px;"></i><br>لا توجد منتجات في الفاتورة</td></tr>';
7436:                 document.getElementById('purchaseTotal').textContent = '0.00 جنيه';
7437:                 return;
7438:             }
7439:             
7440:             let html = '';
7441:             let total = 0;
7442:             
7443:             purchaseItems.forEach((item, index) => {
7444:                 total += item.total;
7445:                 html += '<tr style="border-bottom: 1px solid #eee;">';
7446:                 html += '<td style="padding: 12px; text-align: center;">' + item.supplier + '</td>';
7447:                 html += '<td style="padding: 12px; text-align: center;">' + item.productName + ' (' + item.productCode + ')</td>';
7448:                 html += '<td style="padding: 12px; text-align: center;">' + (item.color || '-') + '</td>';
7449:                 html += '<td style="padding: 12px; text-align: center;">' + item.quantity + '</td>';
7450:                 html += '<td style="padding: 12px; text-align: center;">' + item.price.toFixed(2) + ' جنيه</td>';
7451:                 html += '<td style="padding: 12px; text-align: center; font-weight: bold;">' + item.total.toFixed(2) + ' جنيه</td>';
7452:                 html += '<td style="padding: 12px; text-align: center;">';
7453:                 html += '<button type="button" onclick="removePurchaseItem(' + index + ')" class="btn btn-delete" style="padding: 6px 12px;">';
7454:                 html += '<i class="fas fa-trash"></i></button>';
7455:                 html += '</td></tr>';
7456:             });
7457:             
7458:             tbody.innerHTML = html;
7459:             document.getElementById('purchaseTotal').textContent = total.toFixed(2) + ' جنيه';
7460:         }

7499:                 });

7505:                 (p.code && p.code.toLowerCase().includes(searchCode)) || 
7506:                 (p.name && p.name.toLowerCase().includes(searchCode))
7507:             );

7610:         function loadPurchases(page) {
7611:             if (page) purchasesPage = page;
7612:             const tbody = document.getElementById('purchasesTableBody');
7613:             
7614:             // Update statistics
7615:             const pendingCount = purchases.filter(p => !p.status || p.status === 'pending').length;
7616:             const approvedCount = purchases.filter(p => p.status === 'approved').length;
7617:             const receivedCount = purchases.filter(p => p.status === 'received').length;
7618:             
7619:             document.getElementById('pendingOrdersCount').textContent = pendingCount;
7620:             document.getElementById('approvedOrdersCount').textContent = approvedCount;
7621:             document.getElementById('receivedOrdersCount').textContent = receivedCount;
7622:             
7623:             if (purchases.length === 0) {
7624:                 tbody.innerHTML = '<tr><td colspan="9" style="text-align: center; padding: 50px; color: #666;"><i class="fas fa-shopping-bag" style="font-size: 3rem; margin-bottom: 15px; opacity: 0.3;"></i><br>لا توجد فواتير مشتريات حالياً</td></tr>';
7625:                 renderPagination(purchases.length, purchasesPage, 'purchasesPagination', 'loadPurchases');
7626:                 return;
7627:             }
7628: 
7629:             // Calculate pagination
7630:             const startIndex = (purchasesPage - 1) * itemsPerPage;
7631:             const endIndex = startIndex + itemsPerPage;
7632:             const paginatedPurchases = purchases.slice(startIndex, endIndex);
7633:             
7634:             tbody.innerHTML = paginatedPurchases.map(purchase => {
7635:                 // Check if new format (has items array)
7636:                 const isNewFormat = purchase.items && Array.isArray(purchase.items);
7637:                 
7638:                 // Status display
7639:                 let statusBadge = '';
7640:                 let statusActions = '';
7641:                 if (!purchase.status || purchase.status === 'pending') {
7642:                     statusBadge = '<span style="background: #ffc107; color: #000; padding: 5px 10px; border-radius: 20px; font-size: 0.8rem;">معلقة</span>';
7643:                     statusActions = `
7644:                         <button onclick="editPurchase(${purchase.id})" class="btn" style="padding: 5px 10px; font-size: 0.8rem; background: #6c757d; color: white; margin-left: 5px;"><i class="fas fa-edit"></i> تعديل</button>
7645:                         <button onclick="approvePurchase(${purchase.id})" class="btn btn-add" style="padding: 5px 10px; font-size: 0.8rem;"><i class="fas fa-check"></i> تأكيد</button>
7646:                         <button onclick="deletePurchase(${purchase.id})" class="btn btn-delete" style="padding: 5px 10px; font-size: 0.8rem;"><i class="fas fa-trash"></i></button>
7647:                     `;
7648:                 } else if (purchase.status === 'approved') {
7649:                     statusBadge = '<span style="background: #28a745; color: white; padding: 5px 10px; border-radius: 20px; font-size: 0.8rem;">مؤكدة</span>';
7650:                     statusActions = `
7651:                         <button onclick="editPurchase(${purchase.id})" class="btn" style="padding: 5px 10px; font-size: 0.8rem; background: #6c757d; color: white; margin-left: 5px;"><i class="fas fa-edit"></i> تعديل</button>
7652:                         <button onclick="receivePurchase(${purchase.id})" class="btn btn-primary" style="padding: 5px 10px; font-size: 0.8rem;"><i class="fas fa-truck"></i> استلام</button>
7653:                         <button onclick="deletePurchase(${purchase.id})" class="btn btn-delete" style="padding: 5px 10px; font-size: 0.8rem;"><i class="fas fa-trash"></i></button>
7654:                     `;
7655:                 } else if (purchase.status === 'received') {
7656:                     statusBadge = '<span style="background: #17a2b8; color: white; padding: 5px 10px; border-radius: 20px; font-size: 0.8rem;">مستلمة</span>';
7657:                     statusActions = `
7658:                         <button onclick="editPurchase(${purchase.id})" class="btn" style="padding: 5px 10px; font-size: 0.8rem; background: #6c757d; color: white; margin-left: 5px;"><i class="fas fa-edit"></i> تعديل</button>
7659:                         <button onclick="deletePurchase(${purchase.id})" class="btn btn-delete" style="padding: 5px 10px; font-size: 0.8rem;"><i class="fas fa-trash"></i></button>
7660:                         <span style="color: #28a745;"><i class="fas fa-check-circle"></i> مكتمل</span>
7661:                     `;
7662:                 }
7663:                 
7664:                 if (isNewFormat) {
7665:                     // New multi-item format
7666:                     const itemsCount = purchase.items.length;
7667:                     const totalQty = purchase.totalQuantity || purchase.items.reduce((sum, item) => sum + item.quantity, 0);
7668:                     const totalAmt = purchase.totalAmount || purchase.items.reduce((sum, item) => sum + item.total, 0);
7669:                     
7670:                     return `
7671:                         <tr>
7672:                             <td style="padding: 15px; text-align: center;">
7673:                                 <input type="checkbox" class="purchase-checkbox" value="${purchase.id}" style="transform: scale(1.2);">
7674:                             </td>
7675:                             <td style="padding: 15px;"><strong>#${purchase.id}</strong></td>
7676:                             <td style="padding: 15px;">${purchase.purchaseSupplier}</td>
7677:                             <td style="padding: 15px;">
7678:                                 <span style="background: #e3f2fd; padding: 5px 10px; border-radius: 5px; font-size: 0.9rem;">${itemsCount} منتج</span>
7679:                             </td>
7680:                             <td style="padding: 15px; font-weight: bold;">${totalQty}</td>
7681:                             <td style="padding: 15px; font-weight: bold; color: var(--accent);">${totalAmt.toFixed(2)} جنيه</td>
7682:                             <td style="padding: 15px;">${new Date(purchase.purchaseDate).toLocaleDateString('ar-EG')}</td>
7683:                             <td style="padding: 15px; text-align: center;">${statusBadge}</td>
7684:                             <td style="padding: 15px; text-align: center;">
7685:                                 ${statusActions}
7686:                             </td>
7687:                         </tr>
7688:                     `;
7689:                 } else {
7690:                     // Old single-item format (backward compatibility)
7691:                     const product = products.find(p => p.id == purchase.productId);
7692:                     const productName = product ? product.name : 'منتج محذوف';
7693:                     const total = (purchase.purchasePrice || 0) * (purchase.purchaseQuantity || 1);
7694:                     
7695:                     return `
7696:                         <tr>
7697:                             <td style="padding: 15px; text-align: center;">
7698:                                 <input type="checkbox" class="purchase-checkbox" value="${purchase.id}" style="transform: scale(1.2);">
7699:                             </td>
7700:                             <td style="padding: 15px;"><strong>#${purchase.id}</strong></td>
7701:                             <td style="padding: 15px;">${purchase.purchaseSupplier}</td>
7702:                             <td style="padding: 15px;">${productName}</td>
7703:                             <td style="padding: 15px; font-weight: bold; color: #ff6b6b;">${parseFloat(purchase.purchasePrice || 0).toFixed(2)} جنيه</td>
7704:                             <td style="padding: 15px; text-align: center;"><span style="background: #f0f0f0; padding: 5px 10px; border-radius: 5px;">${purchase.purchaseQuantity}</span></td>
7705:                             <td style="padding: 15px; font-weight: bold; color: var(--danger);">${total.toFixed(2)} جنيه</td>
7706:                             <td style="padding: 15px;">${new Date(purchase.purchaseDate).toLocaleDateString('ar-EG')}</td>
7707:                             <td style="padding: 15px; text-align: center;">${statusBadge}</td>
7708:                             <td style="padding: 15px; text-align: center;">
7709:                                 ${statusActions}
7710:                             </td>
7711:                         </tr>
7712:                     `;
7713:                 }
7714:             }).join('');
7715:             
7716:             // Render pagination
7717:             renderPagination(purchases.length, purchasesPage, 'purchasesPagination', 'loadPurchases');
7718:         }
7719:         
7720:         function createPurchaseOrdersForLowStock() {
7721:             const lowStockProducts = products.filter(p => {
7722:                 const min = p.minStock || 5;
7723:                 return p.totalQuantity > 0 && p.totalQuantity <= min && p.supplier;
7724:             });
7725:             
7726:             if (lowStockProducts.length === 0) {
7727:                 showErrorMessage('لا توجد منتجات منخفضة المخزون تحتاج لطلبات شراء');
7728:                 return;
7729:             }
7730:             
7731:             // Group products by supplier
7732:             const supplierOrders = {};
7733:             lowStockProducts.forEach(product => {
7734:                 const supplier = product.supplier;
7735:                 if (!supplierOrders[supplier]) {
7736:                     supplierOrders[supplier] = [];
7737:                 }
7738:                 supplierOrders[supplier].push(product);
7739:             });
7740:             
7741:             // Create purchase orders for each supplier
7742:             Object.keys(supplierOrders).forEach(supplier => {
7743:                 const products = supplierOrders[supplier];
7744:                 products.forEach(product => {
7745:                     const orderQuantity = (product.minStock || 5) - product.totalQuantity + 10; // Order enough to reach min + 10 extra
7746:                     
7747:                     const newPurchase = {
7748:                         id: Date.now() + Math.floor(Math.random() * 1000),
7749:                         purchaseSupplier: supplier,
7750:                         purchaseSupplierAddress: '',
7751:                         productId: product.id,
7752:                         purchasePrice: product.purchasePrice || 0,
7753:                         purchaseQuantity: orderQuantity,
7754:                         purchaseDate: new Date().toISOString().split('T')[0],
7755:                         purchaseNotes: 'طلب شراء تلقائي للمخزون المنخفض',
7756:                         status: 'pending',
7757:                         autoGenerated: true,
7758:                         createdAt: new Date().toISOString()
7759:                     };
7760:                     
7761:                     purchases.push(newPurchase);
7762:                 });
7763:             });
7764:             
7765:             saveData();
7766:             loadPurchases();
7767:             showSuccessMessage(`تم إنشاء ${lowStockProducts.length} طلب شراء للمنتجات منخفضة المخزون`);
7768:         }
7769:         
7770:         function approvePurchase(id) {
7771:             // Handle both string and number types
7772:             const approveId = typeof id === 'string' ? parseInt(id) : id;
7773:             const purchase = purchases.find(p => p.id == approveId || p.id === approveId);
7774:             if (purchase) {
7775:                 purchase.status = 'approved';
7776:                 saveData();
7777:                 loadPurchases();
7778:                 showSuccessMessage('تم تأكيد طلب الشراء');
7779:             }
7780:         }
7781:         
7782:         // Expose approvePurchase to global scope
7783:         window.approvePurchase = approvePurchase;
7784:         
7785:         function receivePurchase(id) {
7786:             // Handle both string and number types
7787:             const receiveId = typeof id === 'string' ? parseInt(id) : id;
7788:             const purchase = purchases.find(p => p.id == receiveId || p.id === receiveId);
7789:             if (purchase) {
7790:                 purchase.status = 'received';
7791:                 
7792:                 // Update product stock - handle both old and new format
7793:                 if (purchase.items && Array.isArray(purchase.items)) {
7794:                     // New format: items array
7795:                     purchase.items.forEach(item => {
7796:                         const product = products.find(p => p.id == item.productId);
7797:                         if (product) {
7798:                             product.totalQuantity = (product.totalQuantity || 0) + item.quantity;
7799:                         }
7800:                     });
7801:                 } else {
7802:                     // Old format: single product
7803:                     const product = products.find(p => p.id == purchase.productId);
7804:                     if (product) {
7805:                         product.totalQuantity = (product.totalQuantity || 0) + purchase.purchaseQuantity;
7806:                     }
7807:                 }
7808:                 
7809:                 saveData();
7810:                 loadPurchases();
7811:                 loadProducts();
7812:                 loadInventorySection();
7813:                 showSuccessMessage('تم استلام طلب الشراء وتحديث المخزون');
7814:             }
7815:         }
7816:         
7817:         // Expose receivePurchase to global scope
7818:         window.receivePurchase = receivePurchase;
7819: 
7820:         function savePurchase() {
7821:             if (purchaseItems.length === 0) {
7822:                 showErrorMessage('يرجى إضافة منتج واحد على الأقل للفاتورة');
7823:                 return;
7824:             }
7825:             
7826:             const address = document.getElementById('purchaseSupplierAddress').value;
7827:             const notes = document.getElementById('purchaseNotes').value;
7828:             const purchaseDate = document.getElementById('purchaseDate').value;
7829:             
7830:             // Group items by supplier
7831:             const itemsBySupplier = {};
7832:             purchaseItems.forEach(item => {
7833:                 const supplier = item.supplier || 'مورد غير محدد';
7834:                 if (!itemsBySupplier[supplier]) {
7835:                     itemsBySupplier[supplier] = [];
7836:                 }
7837:                 itemsBySupplier[supplier].push(item);
7838:             });
7839:             
7840:             // Create a separate purchase for each supplier
7841:             let totalDeducted = 0;
7842:             Object.keys(itemsBySupplier).forEach((supplier, index) => {
7843:                 const supplierItems = itemsBySupplier[supplier];
7844:                 const supplierTotal = supplierItems.reduce((sum, item) => sum + item.total, 0);
7845:                 const supplierQuantity = supplierItems.reduce((sum, item) => sum + item.quantity, 0);
7846:                 
7847:                 const purchase = {
7848:                     id: Date.now() + index,
7849:                     purchaseSupplier: supplier,
7850:                     purchaseSupplierAddress: address,
7851:                     items: supplierItems,
7852:                     totalQuantity: supplierQuantity,
7853:                     totalAmount: supplierTotal,
7854:                     purchaseDate: purchaseDate,
7855:                     notes: notes,
7856:                     status: 'pending',
7857:                     createdAt: new Date().toISOString()
7858:                 };
7859:                 
7860:                 purchases.push(purchase);
7861:                 totalDeducted += supplierTotal;
7862:             });
7863:             
7864:             // Deduct total from capital
7865:             capitalAmount -= totalDeducted;
7866:             localStorage.setItem('hances_capital', capitalAmount.toString());
7867:             
7868:             saveData();
7869:             loadPurchases();
7870:             updateDashboard();
7871:             closeModal('purchaseModal');
7872:             showSuccessMessage('تم إضافة فواتير الشراء لكل مورد بنجاح!');
7873:         }
7874: 
7875:         function editPurchase(id) {
7876:             console.log('editPurchase called with id:', id, 'type:', typeof id);
7877:             
7878:             // Ensure id is a number for comparison
7879:             const searchId = typeof id === 'string' ? parseInt(id) : id;
7880:             console.log('Searching with id:', searchId);
7881:             
7882:             const purchase = purchases.find(p => p.id == searchId || p.id === searchId);
7883:             console.log('Found purchase:', purchase);
7884:             if (!purchase) {
7885:                 console.log('Purchase not found for id:', searchId);
7886:                 console.log('Available purchase IDs:', purchases.map(p => p.id));
7887:                 showErrorMessage('لم يتم العثور على الفاتورة');
7888:                 return;
7889:             }
7890:             
7891:             // Clear existing items
7892:             purchaseItems = [];
7893:             console.log('Cleared purchaseItems');
7894:             
7895:             loadPurchaseSupplierOptions();
7896:             loadItemProductOptions();
7897:             
7898:             // Also populate itemSupplier dropdown
7899:             const itemSupplierSelect = document.getElementById('itemSupplier');
7900:             if (itemSupplierSelect) {
7901:                 itemSupplierSelect.innerHTML = '<option value="">-- اختر المورد --</option>';
7902:                 suppliers.forEach(supplier => {
7903:                     itemSupplierSelect.innerHTML += `<option value="${supplier}">${supplier}</option>`;
7904:                 });
7905:             }
7906:             
7907:             console.log('Loaded options');
7908:             
7909:             // Check if new format (has items array)
7910:             if (purchase.items && Array.isArray(purchase.items)) {
7911:                 // New multi-item format
7912:                 console.log('New format detected, items:', purchase.items);
7913:                 purchaseItems = [...purchase.items];
7914:                 renderPurchaseItems();
7915:             } else {
7916:                 // Old single-item format (backward compatibility)
7917:                 console.log('Old format detected');
7918:                 const product = products.find(p => p.id == purchase.productId);
7919:                 console.log('Found product:', product);
7920:                 if (product) {
7921:                     purchaseItems.push({
7922:                         supplier: purchase.purchaseSupplier,
7923:                         productId: purchase.productId,
7924:                         productName: product.name,
7925:                         productCode: product.code,
7926:                         color: product.color || '',
7927:                         quantity: purchase.purchaseQuantity,
7928:                         price: purchase.purchasePrice,
7929:                         total: (purchase.purchasePrice || 0) * (purchase.purchaseQuantity || 1)
7930:                     });
7931:                 }
7932:                 renderPurchaseItems();
7933:             }
7934:             
7935:             document.getElementById('purchaseSupplier').value = purchase.purchaseSupplier;
7936:             document.getElementById('purchaseSupplierAddress').value = purchase.purchaseSupplierAddress || '';
7937:             document.getElementById('purchaseDate').value = purchase.purchaseDate;
7938:             document.getElementById('purchaseNotes').value = purchase.notes || purchase.purchaseNotes || '';
7939:             
7940:             document.querySelector('#purchaseModal .modal-header h3').innerHTML = '<i class="fas fa-edit"></i> تعديل فاتورة الشراء (#' + purchase.id + ')';
7941:             document.getElementById('purchaseModal').style.display = 'block';
7942:             console.log('Modal should be visible now');
7943:             
7944:             const form = document.getElementById('purchaseForm');
7945:             form.onsubmit = (e) => {
7946:                 e.preventDefault();
7947:                 
7948:                 if (purchaseItems.length === 0) {
7949:                     showErrorMessage('يرجى إضافة منتجات للفاتورة');
7950:                     return;
7951:                 }
7952:                 
7953:                 const supplier = document.getElementById('purchaseSupplier').value;
7954:                 if (!supplier) {
7955:                     showErrorMessage('يرجى اختيار المورد');
7956:                     return;
7957:                 }
7958:                 
7959:                 const totalAmount = purchaseItems.reduce((sum, item) => sum + item.total, 0);
7960:                 const totalQuantity = purchaseItems.reduce((sum, item) => sum + item.quantity, 0);
7961:                 
7962:                 // Use searchId for comparison to handle both string and number types
7963:                 const updateId = typeof id === 'string' ? parseInt(id) : id;
7964:                 const index = purchases.findIndex(p => p.id == updateId || p.id === updateId);
7965:                 if (index !== -1) {
7966:                     purchases[index] = {
7967:                         ...purchases[index],
7968:                         purchaseSupplier: supplier,
7969:                         purchaseSupplierAddress: document.getElementById('purchaseSupplierAddress').value,
7970:                         purchaseDate: document.getElementById('purchaseDate').value,
7971:                         notes: document.getElementById('purchaseNotes').value,
7972:                         items: purchaseItems,
7973:                         totalAmount: totalAmount,
7974:                         totalQuantity: totalQuantity,
7975:                         updatedAt: new Date().toISOString()
7976:                     };
7977:                     saveData();
7978:                     loadPurchases();
7979:                     closeModal('purchaseModal');
7980:                     showSuccessMessage('تم تحديث فاتورة الشراء بنجاح!');
7981:                 } else {
7982:                     showErrorMessage('لم يتم العثور على الفاتورة للتحديث');
7983:                 }
7984:             };
7985:         }

7990:         function deletePurchase(id) {
7991:             if (confirm('هل أنت متأكد من حذف هذه الفاتورة؟')) {
7992:                 // Handle both string and number types
7993:                 const deleteId = typeof id === 'string' ? parseInt(id) : id;
7994:                 const purchase = purchases.find(p => p.id == deleteId || p.id === deleteId);
7995:                 if (purchase && purchase.totalAmount) {
7996:                     // Restore capital
7997:                     capitalAmount += purchase.totalAmount;
7998:                     localStorage.setItem('hances_capital', capitalAmount.toString());
7999:                 }
8000:                 purchases = purchases.filter(p => p.id != deleteId && p.id !== deleteId);
8001:                 saveData();
8002:                 loadPurchases();
8003:                 updateDashboard();
8004:                 showSuccessMessage('تم حذف فاتورة الشراء بنجاح!');
8005:             }
8006:         }

8009:         window.deletePurchase = deletePurchase;

8011:         function exportPurchasesToExcel() {
8012:             if (purchases.length === 0) {
8013:                 alert('لا توجد بيانات لتصديرها');
8014:                 return;
8015:             }
8016: 
8017:             const data = purchases.map(p => {
8018:                 const product = products.find(pr => pr.id == p.productId);
8019:                 const productName = product ? product.name : 'منتج محذوف';
8020:                 const productCode = product ? product.code : '-';
8021:                 return {
8022:                     'رقم الفاتورة': p.id,
8023:                     'المورد': p.purchaseSupplier,
8024:                     'العنوان': p.purchaseSupplierAddress,
8025:                     'المنتج': productName,
8026:                     'الرمز': productCode,
8027:                     'سعر الشراء': p.purchasePrice,
8028:                     'الكمية': p.purchaseQuantity,
8029:                     'الإجمالي': p.purchasePrice * p.purchaseQuantity,
8030:                     'التاريخ': new Date(p.purchaseDate).toLocaleDateString('ar-EG'),
8031:                     'ملاحظات': p.purchaseNotes
8032:                 };
8033:             });
8034: 
8035:             const worksheet = XLSX.utils.json_to_sheet(data);
8036:             const workbook = XLSX.utils.book_new();
8037:             XLSX.utils.book_append_sheet(workbook, worksheet, 'المشتريات');
8038:             XLSX.writeFile(workbook, `فواتير_المشتريات_${new Date().toLocaleDateString('ar-EG')}.xlsx`);
8039:         }

8050:         function loadReturnInvoiceOptions() {
8051:             const select = document.getElementById('returnInvoice');
8052:             select.innerHTML = '<option value="">اختر الفاتورة للإرجاع</option>';
8053:             invoices.forEach(invoice => {
8054:                 const items = normalizeInvoiceItems(invoice);
8055:                 const productNames = items.map((item, idx) => {
8056:                     const product = products.find(p => p.id == item.productId);
8057:                     const colorInfo = item.selectedColor ? ` - ${item.selectedColor}` : '';
8058:                     return `${product ? product.name : 'منتج محذوف'}${colorInfo}`;
8059:                 }).join(', ');
8060:                 select.innerHTML += `<option value="${invoice.id}">فاتورة #${invoice.id} - ${invoice.customerName} - ${productNames}</option>`;
8061:             });

8064:         function searchInvoicesForReturn() {
8065:             const q = (document.getElementById('returnSearchInput').value || '').trim().toLowerCase();
8066:             const select = document.getElementById('returnInvoice');
8067:             select.innerHTML = '<option value="">اختر الفاتورة للإرجاع</option>';
8068:             if (!q) {
8069:                 invoices.forEach(invoice => {
8070:                     const items = normalizeInvoiceItems(invoice);
8071:                     const productNames = items.map((item, idx) => {
8072:                         const product = products.find(p => p.id == item.productId);
8073:                         const colorInfo = item.selectedColor ? ` - ${item.selectedColor}` : '';
8074:                         return `${product ? product.name : 'منتج محذوف'}${colorInfo}`;
8075:                     }).join(', ');
8076:                     select.innerHTML += `<option value="${invoice.id}">فاتورة #${invoice.id} - ${invoice.customerName} - ${productNames}</option>`;
8077:                 });
8078:                 return;
8079:             }
8080: 
8081:             const filtered = invoices.filter(inv => (''+inv.id).includes(q) || (inv.customerName || '').toLowerCase().includes(q));
8082:             if (filtered.length === 0) {
8083:                 select.innerHTML += '<option value="">لا توجد فواتير مطابقة</option>';
8084:                 return;
8085:             }
8086:             filtered.forEach(invoice => {
8087:                 const items = normalizeInvoiceItems(invoice);
8088:                 const productNames = items.map((item, idx) => {
8089:                     const product = products.find(p => p.id == item.productId);
8090:                     const colorInfo = item.selectedColor ? ` - ${item.selectedColor}` : '';
8091:                     return `${product ? product.name : 'منتج محذوف'}${colorInfo}`;
8092:                 }).join(', ');
8093:                 select.innerHTML += `<option value="${invoice.id}">فاتورة #${invoice.id} - ${invoice.customerName} - ${productNames}</option>`;
8094:             });
8095:         }

8112:             productSelect.innerHTML = '';

8214: 
8215:         function addReturnItem() {
8216:             const invoiceId = parseInt(document.getElementById('returnInvoice').value);
8217:             const productSelect = document.getElementById('returnProductSelect');
8218:             const selectedOption = productSelect.options[productSelect.selectedIndex];
8219:             const quantityInput = document.getElementById('returnQuantity');
8220:             
8221:             if (!invoiceId) {
8222:                 alert('Please select an invoice first');
8223:                 return;
8224:             }
8225:             
8226:             if (!selectedOption || !selectedOption.value) {
8227:                 alert('Please select a product');
8228:                 return;
8229:             }
8230:             
8231:             const quantity = parseInt(quantityInput.value);
8232:             if (!quantity || quantity < 1) {
8233:                 alert('Please enter a valid quantity');
8234:                 return;
8235:             }
8236:             
8237:             const maxReturnable = parseInt(selectedOption.getAttribute('data-max')) || 0;
8238:             if (quantity > maxReturnable) {
8239:                 alert(`Maximum returnable quantity is ${maxReturnable}`);
8240:                 return;
8241:             }
8242:             
8243:             const productId = selectedOption.value;
8244:             const itemIndex = parseInt(selectedOption.getAttribute('data-index'));
8245:             const invoice = invoices.find(i => i.id === invoiceId);
8246:             const items = normalizeInvoiceItems(invoice);
8247:             const item = items[itemIndex];
8248:             const product = products.find(p => p.id == item.productId);
8249:             
8250:             // Check if already added
8251:             const existingIndex = currentReturnItems.findIndex(r => r.productId == productId && r.itemIndex === itemIndex);
8252:             if (existingIndex !== -1) {
8253:                 alert('This product is already in the return list');
8254:                 return;
8255:             }
8256:             
8257:             const unitPrice = item.unitPrice || item.price || 0;
8258:             
8259:             currentReturnItems.push({
8260:                 productId: productId,
8261:                 itemIndex: itemIndex,
8262:                 quantity: quantity,
8263:                 unitPrice: unitPrice,
8264:                 total: quantity * unitPrice,
8265:                 product: product,
8266:                 selectedColor: item.selectedColor || ''
8267:             });
8268:             
8269:             // Reset quantity input
8270:             quantityInput.value = '';
8271:             productSelect.selectedIndex = 0;
8272:             document.getElementById('returnSelectedProductDisplay').style.display = 'none';
8273:             
8274:             renderReturnItems();
8275:         }
8276: 
8277:         function removeReturnItem(index) {
8278:             currentReturnItems.splice(index, 1);
8279:             renderReturnItems();
8280:         }
8281: 
8282:         function renderReturnItems() {
8283:             const listArea = document.getElementById('returnItemsArea');
8284:             const listContainer = document.getElementById('returnItemsList');
8285:             const totalSpan = document.getElementById('returnItemsTotal');
8286:             
8287:             if (currentReturnItems.length === 0) {
8288:                 listArea.style.display = 'none';
8289:                 return;
8290:             }
8291:             
8292:             listArea.style.display = 'block';
8293:             let total = 0;
8294:             
8295:             listContainer.innerHTML = currentReturnItems.map((item, index) => {
8296:                 total += item.total;
8297:                 const imgHtml = item.product && item.product.image ? `<img src="${item.product.image}" alt="${item.product.name}">` : `<div style="width:80px;height:80px;background:#f0f0f0;border-radius:8px;display:flex;align-items:center;justify-content:center;"><i class="fas fa-image" style="color:#ccc;font-size:1.4rem"></i></div>`;
8298:                 
8299:                 return `
8300:                     <div class="return-item-card">
8301:                         ${imgHtml}
8302:                         <div class="item-details">
8303:                             <h4>${item.product ? item.product.name : 'Deleted Product'}</h4>
8304:                             <div style="color:#666;font-size:0.9rem;">
8305:                                 Qty: ${item.quantity} × EGP ${item.unitPrice.toFixed(2)} = EGP ${item.total.toFixed(2)}
8306:                             </div>
8307:                             ${item.selectedColor ? `<div style="color:#666;font-size:0.9rem;">Color: ${item.selectedColor}</div>` : ''}
8308:                         </div>
8309:                         <button type="button" class="remove-btn" onclick="removeReturnItem(${index})">
8310:                             <i class="fas fa-trash"></i>
8311:                         </button>
8312:                     </div>
8313:                 `;
8314:             }).join('');
8315:             
8316:             totalSpan.textContent = total.toFixed(2);
8317:         }

8319:         function loadReturns(page) {
8320:             if (page) returnsPage = page;
8321:             const tbody = document.getElementById('returnsTableBody');
8322:             if (returns.length === 0) {
8323:                 tbody.innerHTML = '<tr><td colspan="8" style="text-align: center; padding: 80px; color: #666;"><i class="fas fa-undo" style="font-size: 5rem; margin-bottom: 25px; opacity: 0.3;"></i><br><span style="font-size: 1.4rem;">لا توجد مرتجعات حالياً</span></td></tr>';
8324:                 renderPagination(returns.length, returnsPage, 'returnsPagination', 'loadReturns');
8325:                 return;
8326:             }
8327: 
8328:             // Calculate pagination
8329:             const startIndex = (returnsPage - 1) * itemsPerPage;
8330:             const endIndex = startIndex + itemsPerPage;
8331:             const paginatedReturns = returns.slice(startIndex, endIndex);
8332:             
8333:             tbody.innerHTML = paginatedReturns.map(returnItem => {
8334:                 const invoice = invoices.find(i => i.id == returnItem.invoiceId);
8335:                 const product = products.find(p => p.id == returnItem.productId);
8336:                 const colorInfo = returnItem.selectedColor ? ` - ${returnItem.selectedColor}` : '';
8337:                 const refundedBadge = returnItem.refunded ? '<span style="background:#28a745;color:#fff;padding:6px 10px;border-radius:12px;font-weight:600">تم الاسترداد</span>' : '<span style="background:#ffc107;color:#000;padding:6px 10px;border-radius:12px;font-weight:600">قيد الانتظار</span>';
8338:                 const platformLabel = returnItem.platform ? (returnItem.platform === 'noon' ? 'Noon' : returnItem.platform === 'jumia' ? 'Jumia' : 'Web Site') : '-';
8339:                 const shipperBadge = returnItem.receivedByShipper ? '<span style="background:#28a745;color:#fff;padding:6px 10px;border-radius:12px;font-weight:600">✓ تم الاستلام</span>' : '<span style="background:#dc3545;color:#fff;padding:6px 10px;border-radius:12px;font-weight:600">✗ قيد الانتظار</span>';
8340: 
8341:                 return `
8342:                     <tr>
8343:                         <td style="padding: 25px; font-size: 1.1rem;"><strong>#${returnItem.id}</strong></td>
8344:                         <td style="padding: 25px; font-size: 1.1rem;">#${returnItem.invoiceId}</td>
8345:                         <td style="padding: 25px; font-size: 1.1rem;">${returnItem.customerName}</td>
8346:                         <td style="padding: 25px; font-size: 1.1rem;">${returnItem.reason || '-'}</td>
8347:                         <td style="padding: 25px; font-size: 1.1rem;">${product ? product.name + colorInfo : 'منتج محذوف'}</td>
8348:                         <td style="padding: 25px; font-weight: bold; color: var(--danger); font-size: 1.1rem;">${returnItem.totalLoss.toFixed(2)} جنيه</td>
8349:                         <td style="padding: 25px; font-size: 1.1rem;">${new Date(returnItem.date).toLocaleDateString('ar-EG')}</td>
8350:                         <td style="padding: 25px;">${shipperBadge}</td>
8351:                         <td style="padding: 25px; text-align: center; display: flex; gap: 6px; flex-wrap: wrap; justify-content: center;">
8352:                             <button onclick="previewReturnInvoice(${returnItem.id})" class="btn btn-print" style="padding: 6px 10px; font-size: 0.85rem;"><i class="fas fa-eye"></i></button>
8353:                             <button onclick="exportReturnAsPDF(${returnItem.id})" class="btn" style="padding: 6px 10px; font-size: 0.85rem; background:#28a745;color:#fff;"><i class="fas fa-file-pdf"></i></button>
8354:                             <button onclick="toggleReturnShipperReceived(${returnItem.id})" class="btn" style="padding: 6px 10px; font-size: 0.85rem; background:#ffc107;color:#000;"><i class="fas fa-truck"></i></button>
8355:                             <button onclick="toggleReturnRefunded(${returnItem.id})" class="btn" style="padding: 6px 10px; font-size: 0.85rem; background:#17a2b8;color:#fff;"><i class="fas fa-check-circle"></i></button>
8356:                             <button onclick="deleteReturn(${returnItem.id})" class="btn btn-delete" style="padding: 6px 10px; font-size: 0.85rem;"><i class="fas fa-trash"></i></button>
8357:                         </td>
8358:                     </tr>
8359:                 `;
8360:             }).join('');
8361:             
8362:             // Render pagination
8363:             renderPagination(returns.length, returnsPage, 'returnsPagination', 'loadReturns');
8364:         }

8367:             if (confirm('هل أنت متأكد من حذف هذا المرتجع؟')) {
8368:                 returns = returns.filter(r => r.id !== id);
8369:                 saveData();
8370:                 loadReturns();
8371:                 updateDashboard();
8372:                 showSuccessMessage('تم حذف المرتجع بنجاح!');
8373:             }
8374:         }

8376:         function toggleReturnRefunded(id) {
8377:             const idx = returns.findIndex(r => r.id === id);
8378:             if (idx === -1) return;
8379:             returns[idx].refunded = !returns[idx].refunded;
8380:             saveData();
8381:             loadReturns();
8382:             showSuccessMessage(returns[idx].refunded ? 'تم تمييز المرتجع كمسترد' : 'تم إلغاء حالة الاسترداد');
8383:         }
8384: 
8385:         function toggleReturnShipperReceived(id) {
8386:             const idx = returns.findIndex(r => r.id === id);
8387:             if (idx === -1) return;
8388:             returns[idx].receivedByShipper = !returns[idx].receivedByShipper;
8389:             saveData();
8390:             loadReturns();
8391:             showSuccessMessage(returns[idx].receivedByShipper ? 'تم تمييز المرتجع كمستلم من الشركة' : 'تم إلغاء حالة الاستلام من الشركة');
8392:         }

8394:         function previewReturnInvoice(returnId) {
8395:             const ret = returns.find(r => r.id === returnId);
8396:             if (!ret) { showErrorMessage('مرتجع غير موجود'); return; }
8397:             const product = products.find(p => p.id == ret.productId) || {};
8398:             // Build a small invoice-like object to reuse generator
8399:             const fakeInvoice = {
8400:                 id: 'R-' + ret.id,
8401:                 customerName: ret.customerName || '',
8402:                 customerPhone: '',
8403:                 customerAddress: '',
8404:                 selectedColor: ret.selectedColor || '',
8405:                 quantity: ret.quantity || 0,
8406:                 unitPrice: ret.amount && ret.quantity ? (ret.amount / ret.quantity) : 0,
8407:                 grossTotal: ret.amount || 0,
8408:                 commission: ret.commissionLoss || 0,
8409:                 shipping: ret.shippingLoss || 0,
8410:                 platform: ret.platform || '',
8411:                 paymentMethod: '',
8412:                 date: ret.date || new Date().toISOString()
8413:             };
8414: 
8415:             const html = generateInvoiceHTML(fakeInvoice, product, { mode: 'return', returnData: ret });
8416:             try {
8417:                 const w = window.open('', '_blank', 'width=900,height=700');
8418:                 if (!w || w.closed) throw new Error('popup-blocked');
8419:                 w.document.write(html);
8420:                 w.document.close();
8421:             } catch (e) {
8422:                 showInvoiceModal(html);
8423:             }
8424:         }

8426:         function exportReturnAsPDF(returnId) {
8427:             const ret = returns.find(r => r.id === returnId);
8428:             if (!ret) { showErrorMessage('مرتجع غير موجود'); return; }
8429:             const product = products.find(p => p.id == ret.productId) || {};
8430:             const fakeInvoice = {
8431:                 id: 'R-' + ret.id,
8432:                 customerName: ret.customerName || '',
8433:                 customerPhone: '',
8434:                 customerAddress: '',
8435:                 selectedColor: ret.selectedColor || '',
8436:                 quantity: ret.quantity || 0,
8437:                 unitPrice: ret.amount && ret.quantity ? (ret.amount / ret.quantity) : 0,
8438:                 grossTotal: ret.amount || 0,
8439:                 commission: ret.commissionLoss || 0,
8440:                 shipping: ret.shippingLoss || 0,
8441:                 platform: ret.platform || '',
8442:                 paymentMethod: '',
8443:                 date: ret.date || new Date().toISOString()
8444:             };
8445: 
8446:             const html = generateInvoiceHTML(fakeInvoice, product, { mode: 'return', returnData: ret });
8447:             const opt = { margin:0.4, filename: `return_${ret.id}.pdf`, html2canvas: { scale:2 } };
8448:             const element = document.createElement('div'); element.innerHTML = html; element.style.display = 'block'; document.body.appendChild(element);
8449:             html2pdf().from(element).set(opt).save().then(()=>{ element.remove(); });
8450:         }

8468:         function loadExpenses() {
8469:             console.log('Loading expenses...');
8470:             const tbody = document.getElementById('expensesTableBody');
8471:             
8472:             console.log('Expenses array:', expenses);
8473:             console.log('Expenses count:', expenses.length);
8474:             
8475:             if (expenses.length === 0) {
8476:                 tbody.innerHTML = '<tr><td colspan="4" style="text-align: center; padding: 80px; color: #666;"><i class="fas fa-money-bill-wave" style="font-size: 5rem; margin-bottom: 25px; opacity: 0.3;"></i><br><span style="font-size: 1.4rem;">لا توجد مصروفات حالياً</span></td></tr>';
8477:                 return;
8478:             }
8479: 
8480:             const todayKey = new Date().toLocaleDateString('ar-EG');
8481:             const isToday = (exp) => {
8482:                 const d = new Date(exp.date);
8483:                 return d.toLocaleDateString('ar-EG') === todayKey;
8484:             };
8485: 
8486:             const todays = expenses.filter(isToday);
8487:             const others = expenses.filter(exp => !isToday(exp));
8488: 
8489:             const renderRow = (expense) => `
8490:                 <tr style="${isToday(expense) ? 'background:rgba(40,167,69,0.06);' : ''}">
8491:                     <td style="padding: 25px; font-size: 1.1rem;"><strong>${expense.type}</strong></td>
8492:                     <td style="padding: 25px; font-weight: bold; color: var(--danger); font-size: 1.1rem;">${expense.amount.toFixed(2)} جنيه</td>
8493:                     <td style="padding: 25px; font-size: 1.1rem;">${new Date(expense.date).toLocaleDateString('ar-EG')}</td>
8494:                     <td style="padding: 25px; text-align: center;">
8495:                         ${!expense.confirmed ? 
8496:                             `<button onclick="confirmExpense(${expense.id})" class="btn" style="background: #28a745; color: white; margin-left: 5px;" title="تأكيد وخصم من رأس المال">
8497:                                 <i class="fas fa-check"></i> تأكيد
8498:                             </button>` : 
8499:                             '<span style="color: #28a745; font-weight: bold;"><i class="fas fa-check-circle"></i> مُؤكد</span>'
8500:                         }
8501:                         <button onclick="editExpense(${expense.id})" class="btn btn-edit" title="تعديل">
8502:                             <i class="fas fa-edit"></i>
8503:                         </button>
8504:                         <button onclick="deleteExpense(${expense.id})" class="btn btn-delete">
8505:                             <i class="fas fa-trash"></i> حذف
8506:                         </button>
8507:                     </td>
8508:                 </tr>
8509:             `;
8510: 
8511:             let html = '';
8512:             if (todays.length > 0) {
8513:                 html += `
8514:                     <tr style="background: linear-gradient(90deg, rgba(40,167,69,0.15), rgba(255,255,255,0)); border: 1px solid rgba(40,167,69,0.25);">
8515:                         <td colspan="4" style="padding: 18px; font-weight: 800; color: #1e7e34; text-align: center; letter-spacing: 0.5px;">
8516:                             <span style="display:inline-flex;align-items:center;gap:8px;">
8517:                                 <i class="fas fa-sun" style="color:#f0ad4e;"></i>
8518:                                 مصاريف اليوم
8519:                                 <span style="font-weight:600;color:#666;">(${todayKey})</span>
8520:                             </span>
8521:                         </td>
8522:                     </tr>
8523:                 `;
8524:                 html += todays.map(renderRow).join('');
8525:             }
8526:             
8527:             // Show others expenses even if no today's expenses
8528:             if (others.length > 0) {
8529:                 if (todays.length > 0) {
8530:                     html += `
8531:                         <tr style="background: linear-gradient(90deg, rgba(108,117,125,0.1), rgba(255,255,255,0)); border: 1px solid rgba(108,117,125,0.25);">
8532:                             <td colspan="4" style="padding: 18px; font-weight: 800; color: #6c757d; text-align: center; letter-spacing: 0.5px;">
8533:                                 <span style="display:inline-flex;align-items:center;gap:8px;">
8534:                                     <i class="fas fa-history" style="color:#6c757d;"></i>
8535:                                     مصاريف سابقة
8536:                                 </span>
8537:                             </td>
8538:                         </tr>
8539:                     `;
8540:                 }
8541:                 html += others.map(renderRow).join('');
8542:             }
8543: 
8544:             tbody.innerHTML = html;
8545:         }

8547:         function deleteExpense(id) {
8548:             if (confirm('هل أنت متأكد من حذف هذا المصروف؟')) {
8549:                 const expense = expenses.find(e => e.id === id);
8550:                 
8551:                 // If expense was confirmed, restore capital
8552:                 if (expense && expense.confirmed) {
8553:                     capitalAmount += expense.amount;
8554:                     localStorage.setItem('hances_capital', capitalAmount.toString());
8555:                 }
8556:                 
8557:                 expenses = expenses.filter(e => e.id !== id);
8558:                 saveData();
8559:                 loadExpenses();
8560:                 updateDashboard();
8561:                 showSuccessMessage('تم حذف المصروف بنجاح!');
8562:             }
8563:         }
8564:         
8565:         // Confirm expense - deduct from capital only
8566:         function confirmExpense(id) {
8567:             const expense = expenses.find(e => e.id === id);
8568:             if (!expense) return;
8569:             
8570:             if (expense.confirmed) {
8571:                 showErrorMessage('هذا المصروف مُؤكد بالفعل');
8572:                 return;
8573:             }
8574:             
8575:             if (confirm('هل تريد تأكيد هذا المصروف؟\nسيتم خصم المبلغ من رأس المال')) {
8576:                 expense.confirmed = true;
8577:                 expense.confirmedDate = new Date().toISOString();
8578:                 
8579:                 // Deduct from capital only
8580:                 capitalAmount -= expense.amount;
8581:                 localStorage.setItem('hances_capital', capitalAmount.toString());
8582:                 
8583:                 saveData();
8584:                 loadExpenses();
8585:                 updateDashboard();
8586:                 showSuccessMessage('تم تأكيد المصروف وخصم من رأس المال!');
8587:             }
8588:         }
8589: 
8590:         // Edit expense - open modal with expense data
8591:         function editExpense(id) {
8592:             const expense = expenses.find(e => e.id === id);
8593:             if (!expense) return;
8594:             
8595:             // Open modal
8596:             document.getElementById('expenseType').value = expense.type || '';
8597:             document.getElementById('expenseAmount').value = expense.amount || '';
8598:             document.getElementById('expenseDescription').value = expense.description || '';
8599:             document.getElementById('expenseDate').value = expense.date ? expense.date.split('T')[0] : '';
8600:             
8601:             // Store editing id
8602:             document.getElementById('expenseForm').dataset.editingId = id;
8603:             
8604:             // Change button text
8605:             document.querySelector('#expenseModal .modal-header h3').innerHTML = '<i class="fas fa-edit"></i> تعديل مصروف';
8606:             
8607:             // Open modal
8608:             document.getElementById('expenseModal').style.display = 'block';
8609:         }
8610: 
8611:         // Dashboard Functions
8612:         function updateDashboard() {
8613:             // حساب المبيعات من جميع الفواتير المُسلّمة
8614:             const deliveredInvoices = invoices.filter(invoice => invoice.deliveryStatus === 'delivered');
8615:             
8616:             // إجمالي المبيعات = مجموع سعر البيع بدون خصم أي شيء
8617:             const totalRevenue = deliveredInvoices.reduce((sum, invoice) => {
8618:                 const items = normalizeInvoiceItems(invoice);
8619:                 return sum + items.reduce((itemSum, item) => itemSum + (item.unitPrice || 0) * (item.quantity || 0), 0);
8620:             }, 0);
8621:             
8622:             // Count all invoices as orders
8623:             const totalOrders = invoices.length;
8624:             const totalProductsCount = products.length;
8625:             // Only count confirmed expenses in total expenses
8626:             const confirmedExpenses = expenses.filter(e => e.confirmed);
8627:             const totalExpensesAmount = confirmedExpenses.reduce((sum, expense) => sum + expense.amount, 0);
8628:             
8629:             // Show ALL expenses in dashboard (not just confirmed ones)
8630:             const totalExpensesDisplay = expenses.reduce((sum, expense) => sum + expense.amount, 0);
8631:             
8632:             // إجمالي الربح = المبيعات - العمولة - الشحن - الضريبة
8633:             let grossProfit = 0;
8634:             deliveredInvoices.forEach(invoice => {
8635:                 const items = normalizeInvoiceItems(invoice);
8636:                 items.forEach(item => {
8637:                     const product = products.find(p => p.id == item.productId);
8638:                     if (!product) return;
8639:                     
8640:                     const unitPrice = item.unitPrice || 0;
8641:                     const quantity = item.quantity || 0;
8642:                     const sellingAmount = unitPrice * quantity;
8643:                     
8644:                     // خصم العمولة والشحن والضريبة
8645:                     const commission = sellingAmount * (invoice.commissionPercent || 0) / 100;
8646:                     const shipping = (invoice.shipping || 0);
8647:                     const tax = sellingAmount * 0.14; // ضريبة 14%
8648:                     
8649:                     // الربح = سعر البيع - العمولة - الشحن - الضريبة
8650:                     grossProfit += (sellingAmount - commission - shipping - tax);
8651:                 });
8652:             });
8653:             
8654:             // استخراج خسائر المرتجعات
8655:             const totalReturnLosses = returns.reduce((sum, ret) => {
8656:                 return sum + (ret.totalLoss || 0);
8657:             }, 0);
8658:             
8659:             // صافي الربح = إجمالي الربح - المصروفات - خسائر المرتجعات
8660:             const netProfit = grossProfit - totalReturnLosses - totalExpensesAmount;
8661:             
8662:             // تحديث الواجهة
8663:             document.getElementById('totalRevenue').textContent = totalRevenue.toFixed(2) + ' جنيه';
8664:             document.getElementById('totalOrders').textContent = totalOrders;
8665:             document.getElementById('totalProducts').textContent = totalProductsCount;
8666:             document.getElementById('totalExpenses').textContent = totalExpensesDisplay.toFixed(2) + ' جنيه';
8667:             document.getElementById('grossProfit').textContent = grossProfit.toFixed(2) + ' جنيه';
8668:             
8669:             // تحديث صافي الربح مع اللون
8670:             const netProfitElement = document.getElementById('netProfit');
8671:             netProfitElement.textContent = netProfit.toFixed(2) + ' جنيه';
8672: 
8673:             let totalUnitsSoldDash = 0;
8674:             deliveredInvoices.forEach(inv => {
8675:                 const items = normalizeInvoiceItems(inv);
8676:                 items.forEach(it => { totalUnitsSoldDash += (it.quantity || 0); });
8677:             });
8678:             const avgProfitVal = totalUnitsSoldDash > 0 ? (netProfit / totalUnitsSoldDash) : 0;
8679:             const avgElem = document.getElementById('avgProfitPerUnit');
8680:             if (avgElem) avgElem.textContent = avgProfitVal.toFixed(2) + ' جنيه';
8681:             netProfitElement.style.color = netProfit >= 0 ? '#28a745' : '#dc3545';
8682:             
8683:             // حساب الزكاة 10% من صافي الربح
8684:             const positiveNetProfit = netProfit > 0 ? netProfit : 0;
8685:             const ZainetProfit = positiveNetProfit * 0.10;
8686:             const netAfterZakat = positiveNetProfit - ZainetProfit;
8687:             
8688:             document.getElementById('zakatAmount').textContent = ZainetProfit.toFixed(2) + ' جنيه';
8689:             document.getElementById('netAfterZakat').textContent = netAfterZakat.toFixed(2) + ' جنيه';
8690:             
8691:             // Calculate profit percentage
8692:             const profitPercentage = totalRevenue > 0 ? ((netProfit / totalRevenue) * 100).toFixed(1) : 0;
8693:             document.getElementById('profitPercentage').textContent = profitPercentage + '%';
8694:             
8695:             // تحديث لون نسبة الربح حسب النتيجة
8696:             const profitPercentageElement = document.getElementById('profitPercentage');
8697:             profitPercentageElement.parentElement.querySelector('small').innerHTML = netProfit >= 0 ? '<i class="fas fa-arrow-up"></i> ممتاز' : '<i class="fas fa-arrow-down"></i> خسارة';
8698:             
8699:             // تحديث رأس المال في الداشبورد
8700:             if (document.getElementById('dashboardCapital')) {
8701:                 const currentCapital = capitalAmount || 0;
8702:                 document.getElementById('dashboardCapital').textContent = currentCapital.toFixed(2) + ' جنيه';
8703:             }
8704:             document.getElementById('capital').textContent = capitalAmount.toFixed(2) + ' جنيه';
8705: 
8706:             // Update Products section stats
8707:             document.getElementById('totalProductsStats').textContent = totalProductsCount;
8708:             document.getElementById('totalCollectionsStats2').textContent = collections.length;
8709:             const lowStockProducts = products.filter(p => {
8710:                 const qty = p.totalQuantity || p.quantity || p.stock || 0;
8711:                 const min = p.minStock || 5;
8712:                 return qty > 0 && qty <= min;
8713:             }).length;
8714:             document.getElementById('lowStockProducts').textContent = lowStockProducts;
8715:             const outOfStockProducts = products.filter(p => {
8716:                 const qty = p.totalQuantity || p.quantity || p.stock || 0;
8717:                 return qty === 0;
8718:             }).length;
8719:             document.getElementById('outOfStockProducts').textContent = outOfStockProducts;
8720: 
8721:             // Update Collections section stats
8722:             document.getElementById('totalCollectionsStats').textContent = collections.length;
8723:             document.getElementById('totalProductsInCollections').textContent = totalProductsCount;
8724:             document.getElementById('totalCollectionSales').textContent = totalRevenue.toFixed(2) + ' جنيه';
8725: 
8726:             // Update Invoices section stats
8727:             document.getElementById('totalInvoicesStats').textContent = invoices.length;
8728:             document.getElementById('totalInvoiceAmount').textContent = totalRevenue.toFixed(2) + ' جنيه';
8729:             document.getElementById('totalInvoiceProfit').textContent = grossProfit.toFixed(2) + ' جنيه';
8730:             document.getElementById('invoiceProfitPercentage').textContent = profitPercentage + '%';
8731: 
8732:             // Update Returns section stats
8733:             document.getElementById('totalReturns').textContent = returns.length;
8734:             document.getElementById('totalReturnsAmount').textContent = totalReturnLosses.toFixed(2) + ' جنيه';
8735:             document.getElementById('receivedReturns').textContent = returns.filter(r => r.received).length;
8736: 
8737:             // Update Expenses section stats
8738:             document.getElementById('totalExpensesStats').textContent = totalExpensesDisplay.toFixed(2) + ' جنيه';
8739:             const today = new Date().toISOString().split('T')[0];
8740:             const todayExpenses = expenses.filter(e => e.date === today).reduce((sum, e) => sum + (e.amount || 0), 0);
8741:             document.getElementById('todayExpenses').textContent = todayExpenses.toFixed(2) + ' جنيه';
8742:             const currentMonth = new Date().toISOString().slice(0, 7);
8743:             const monthExpenses = expenses.filter(e => e.date && e.date.startsWith(currentMonth)).reduce((sum, e) => sum + (e.amount || 0), 0);
8744:             document.getElementById('monthExpenses').textContent = monthExpenses.toFixed(2) + ' جنيه';
8745: 
8746:             updateBestSellingProducts();
8747:         }
8748: 
8749:         function updateBestSellingProducts() {
8750:             const productSales = {};
8751:             
8752:             // استخدام الفواتير المُسلّمة فقط
8753:             const deliveredInvoices = invoices.filter(invoice => invoice.deliveryStatus === 'delivered');
8754:             deliveredInvoices.forEach(invoice => {
8755:                 const items = normalizeInvoiceItems(invoice);
8756:                 items.forEach(item => {
8757:                     const productId = item.productId;
8758:                     if (!productSales[productId]) {
8759:                         productSales[productId] = { quantity: 0, revenue: 0 };
8760:                     }
8761:                     productSales[productId].quantity += item.quantity || 0;
8762:                     productSales[productId].revenue += item.netTotal || 0;
8763:                 });
8764:             });
8765: 
8766:             const sortedProducts = Object.entries(productSales)
8767:                 .map(([productId, sales]) => {
8768:                     const product = products.find(p => p.id == productId);
8769:                     return { product, sales, productId };
8770:                 })
8771:                 .filter(item => item.product)
8772:                 .sort((a, b) => b.sales.quantity - a.sales.quantity)
8773:                 .slice(0, 6);
8774: 
8775:             const container = document.getElementById('bestSellingProducts');
8776:             
8777:             if (sortedProducts.length === 0) {
8778:                 container.innerHTML = `
8779:                     <div style="text-align: center; color: #666; padding: 40px;">
8780:                         <i class="fas fa-chart-bar" style="font-size: 4rem; margin-bottom: 20px; opacity: 0.3;"></i>
8781:                         <p>لا توجد مبيعات بعد</p>
8782:                     </div>
8783:                 `;
8784:                 return;
8785:             }
8786: 
8787:             container.innerHTML = sortedProducts.map((item, index) => {
8788:                 const img = item.product.image || item.product.imageUrl || 'https://cdn-icons-png.flaticon.com/512/679/679922.png';
8789:                 const totalCost = (item.product.purchasePrice || 0) * item.sales.quantity;
8790:                 const totalProfit = item.sales.revenue - totalCost;
8791:                 const avgProfit = item.sales.quantity > 0 ? (totalProfit / item.sales.quantity) : 0;
8792: 
8793:                 return `
8794:                 <div style="background: linear-gradient(135deg, #ffffff, #f8f9fa); padding: 20px; border-radius: 15px; text-align: center; border: 2px solid ${index === 0 ? '#ffd700' : index === 1 ? '#c0c0c0' : index === 2 ? '#cd7f32' : '#e9ecef'}; box-shadow: 0 4px 15px rgba(0,0,0,0.05);">
8795:                     <div style="font-size: 2.2rem; margin-bottom: 10px;">
8796:                         ${index === 0 ? '🥇' : index === 1 ? '🥈' : index === 2 ? '🥉' : '🏆'}
8797:                     </div>
8798:                     <img src="${img}" alt="${item.product.name}" style="width: 85px; height: 85px; object-fit: cover; border-radius: 12px; margin-bottom: 12px; border: 2px solid #ddd; box-shadow: 0 4px 10px rgba(0,0,0,0.1);" onerror="this.src='https://cdn-icons-png.flaticon.com/512/679/679922.png'">
8799:                     <h4 style="color: var(--primary); margin-bottom: 10px; font-size: 1.1rem;">${item.product.name}</h4>
8800:                     <p style="color: #444; margin-bottom: 6px; font-weight: bold;"><i class="fas fa-shopping-cart" style="color:#2196f3;"></i> مبيعات: ${item.sales.quantity} قطعة</p>
8801:                     <p style="color: #2e7d32; font-weight: bold; margin-bottom: 6px; font-size: 1rem;"><i class="fas fa-tag"></i> إجمالي المباع (اتباعت بكام): ${item.sales.revenue.toFixed(2)} ج.م</p>
8802:                     <p style="color: #00838f; font-weight: bold; font-size: 0.95rem; background: #e0f7fa; padding: 6px; border-radius: 8px;"><i class="fas fa-coins"></i> متوسط الربح: ${avgProfit.toFixed(2)} ج.م/قطعة</p>
8803:                 </div>
8804:                 `;
8805:             }).join('');
8806:         }
8807:         // Invoice Preview and Print Functions
8808:         function previewInvoice(invoiceId) {
8809:             const invoice = invoices.find(i => i.id === invoiceId);
8810:             if (!invoice) {
8811:                 showErrorMessage('الفاتورة غير موجودة');
8812:                 return;
8813:             }
8814: 
8815:             const product = products.find(p => p.id == invoice.productId);
8816:             if (!product) {
8817:                 showErrorMessage('بيانات المنتج غير متوفرة');
8818:                 return;
8819:             }
8820: 
8821:             const invoiceHTML = generateInvoiceHTML(invoice, product);
8822:             
8823:             try {
8824:                 const printWindow = window.open('', '_blank', 'width=800,height=600');
8825:                 if (!printWindow || printWindow.closed) throw new Error('popup-blocked');
8826:                 printWindow.document.write(invoiceHTML);
8827:                 printWindow.document.close();
8828:             } catch (e) {
8829:                 // Fallback to in-page modal preview
8830:                 showInvoiceModal(invoiceHTML);
8831:             }
8832:         }
8833: 
8834:         function printInvoicePDF(invoiceId) {
8835:             const invoice = invoices.find(i => i.id === invoiceId);
8836:             if (!invoice) {
8837:                 showErrorMessage('الفاتورة غير موجودة');
8838:                 return;
8839:             }
8840: 
8841:             const product = products.find(p => p.id == invoice.productId);
8842:             if (!product) {
8843:                 showErrorMessage('بيانات المنتج غير متوفرة');
8844:                 return;
8845:             }
8846: 
8847:             const invoiceHTML = generateInvoiceHTML(invoice, product);
8848:             
8849:             try {
8850:                 const printWindow = window.open('', '_blank', 'width=800,height=600');
8851:                 if (!printWindow || printWindow.closed) throw new Error('popup-blocked');
8852:                 printWindow.document.write(invoiceHTML);
8853:                 printWindow.document.close();
8854:                 // Auto print after loading
8855:                 printWindow.onload = function() {
8856:                     printWindow.print();
8857:                 };
8858:             } catch (e) {
8859:                 showInvoiceModal(invoiceHTML, { autoPrint: true });
8860:             }
8861:         }
8862: 
8863:         function exportInvoiceAsPDF(invoiceId) {
8864:             const invoice = invoices.find(i => i.id === invoiceId);
8865:             if (!invoice) {
8866:                 showErrorMessage('الفاتورة غير موجودة');
8867:                 return;
8868:             }
8869: 
8870:             const product = products.find(p => p.id == invoice.productId);
8871:             if (!product) {
8872:                 showErrorMessage('بيانات المنتج غير متوفرة');
8873:                 return;
8874:             }
8875: 
8876:             const invoiceHTML = generateInvoiceHTML(invoice, product);
8877:             try {
8878:                 const printWindow = window.open('', '_blank', 'width=900,height=800');
8879:                 if (!printWindow || printWindow.closed) throw new Error('popup-blocked');
8880:                 printWindow.document.write(invoiceHTML);
8881:                 printWindow.document.close();
8882: 
8883:                 printWindow.onload = function() {
8884:                     try {
8885:                         if (printWindow.html2pdf) {
8886:                             printWindow.html2pdf().from(printWindow.document.body).save();
8887:                         } else {
8888:                             printWindow.print();
8889:                         }
8890:                     } catch (err) {
8891:                         printWindow.print();
8892:                     }
8893:                 };
8894:             } catch (e) {
8895:                 showInvoiceModal(invoiceHTML, { savePdf: true });
8896:             }
8897:         }

8899:         // Modal fallback to show invoice HTML inside current page
8900:         function showInvoiceModal(htmlContent, options = {}) {
8901:             // remove existing if present
8902:             const existing = document.getElementById('invoicePreviewModal');
8903:             if (existing) existing.remove();
8904: 
8905:             const modal = document.createElement('div');
8906:             modal.id = 'invoicePreviewModal';
8907:             modal.style.cssText = 'position:fixed;z-index:3000;left:0;top:0;width:100%;height:100%;background:rgba(0,0,0,0.7);display:flex;align-items:center;justify-content:center;padding:20px;';
8908: 
8909:             const inner = document.createElement('div');
8910:             inner.style.cssText = 'width:100%;max-width:1000px;height:90%;background:white;border-radius:14px;overflow:auto;position:relative;padding:10px;';
8911: 
8912:             const closeBtn = document.createElement('button');
8913:             closeBtn.textContent = 'إغلاق';
8914:             closeBtn.style.cssText = 'position:absolute;left:12px;top:12px;z-index:5;padding:8px 12px;background:#6b8273;color:white;border:none;border-radius:8px;cursor:pointer;';
8915:             closeBtn.onclick = () => modal.remove();
8916: 
8917:             const actions = document.createElement('div');
8918:             actions.style.cssText = 'position:absolute;right:12px;top:12px;z-index:5;display:flex;gap:8px;';
8919: 
8920:             const printBtn = document.createElement('button');
8921:             printBtn.innerHTML = 'طباعة';
8922:             printBtn.style.cssText = 'padding:8px 12px;background:#17a2b8;color:white;border:none;border-radius:8px;cursor:pointer;';
8923:             printBtn.onclick = () => {
8924:                 const w = window.open('','_blank');
8925:                 if (w) {
8926:                     w.document.write(htmlContent);
8927:                     w.document.close();
8928:                     w.onload = () => w.print();
8929:                 } else alert('منع النوافذ المنبثقة، استخدم حفظ PDF بدلاً من ذلك');
8930:             };
8931: 
8932:             const savePdfBtn = document.createElement('button');
8933:             savePdfBtn.innerHTML = 'حفظ PDF';
8934:             savePdfBtn.style.cssText = 'padding:8px 12px;background:#c82333;color:white;border:none;border-radius:8px;cursor:pointer;';
8935:             savePdfBtn.onclick = () => {
8936:                 try {
8937:                     const opt = { margin:0.5, filename: 'invoice_'+Date.now()+'.pdf', html2canvas:{scale:2} };
8938:                     html2pdf().from(htmlContent).set(opt).save();
8939:                 } catch (err) {
8940:                     alert('تعذر حفظ PDF هنا، يرجى السماح للنوافذ المنبثقة أو تجربة المتصفح الآخر');
8941:                 }
8942:             };
8943: 
8944:             actions.appendChild(printBtn);
8945:             actions.appendChild(savePdfBtn);
8946: 
8947:             inner.appendChild(closeBtn);
8948:             inner.appendChild(actions);
8949: 
8950:             const contentWrapper = document.createElement('div');
8951:             contentWrapper.style.cssText = 'padding:20px;';
8952:             contentWrapper.innerHTML = htmlContent;
8953: 
8954:             inner.appendChild(contentWrapper);
8955:             modal.appendChild(inner);
8956:             document.body.appendChild(modal);
8957: 
8958:             if (options.autoPrint) printBtn.click();
8959:             if (options.savePdf) savePdfBtn.click();
8960:         }
