8962:         function generateInvoiceHTML(invoice, product, opts) {
8963:             function esc(s){ if (s === undefined || s === null) return ''; return String(s).replace(/&/g,'&amp;').replace(/</g,'&lt;').replace(/>/g,'&gt;').replace(/"/g,'&quot;').replace(/'/g,'&#39;'); }
8964: 
8965:             var invoiceDate = new Date(invoice.date).toLocaleDateString('en-US', { year: 'numeric', month: 'long', day: 'numeric' });
8966:             var grossTotal = parseFloat(invoice.grossTotal || invoice.total || 0);
8967:             var commission = parseFloat(invoice.commission || 0);
8968:             var shipping = parseFloat(invoice.shipping || 0);
8969:             var isReturn = opts && opts.mode === 'return';
8970:             var returnData = isReturn ? (opts.returnData || {}) : null;
8971:             // For returns we want to display refund amount but also list losses
8972:             if (isReturn) {
8973:                 // use return amounts if available
8974:                 grossTotal = parseFloat(returnData.amount || grossTotal || 0);
8975:                 commission = parseFloat(returnData.commissionLoss || 0);
8976:                 shipping = parseFloat(returnData.shippingLoss || 0);
8977:             }
8978:             // If platform is 'noon' we specifically subtract commission and shipping
8979:             var applyPlatformDeductions = (invoice.platform === 'noon');
8980:             var commissionAmount = applyPlatformDeductions ? commission : (invoice.platform ? commission : commission);
8981:             var shippingAmount = applyPlatformDeductions ? shipping : shipping;
8982:             var netTotal = grossTotal - (commissionAmount || 0) - (shippingAmount || 0);
8983: 
8984:             var logoSrc = localStorage.getItem('hances_store_logo') || '';
8985: 
8986:             var html = [];
8987:             html.push('<!doctype html><html><head><meta charset="utf-8"><title>HANCES Invoice #' + esc(invoice.id) + '</title>');
8988:             html.push('<meta name="viewport" content="width=device-width,initial-scale=1">');
8989:             html.push('<style>body{font-family:Inter,Arial,Helvetica,sans-serif;color:#222;background:#fff;padding:20px} .inv{max-width:840px;margin:0 auto;background:#fff;border-radius:12px;box-shadow:0 10px 30px rgba(0,0,0,0.06);overflow:hidden} .inv-header{display:flex;align-items:center;gap:18px;padding:24px;background:linear-gradient(135deg,#f8fafc,#eef2f5);position:relative} .inv-header .decor{position:absolute;right:12px;top:-18px;opacity:0.12;width:200px;height:200px} .logo{width:90px;height:90px;object-fit:contain;border-radius:10px;background:#fff;padding:8px;border:1px solid #eee} .inv-title{font-family:Playfair Display,serif;font-size:1.6rem;font-weight:700;color:#233} .info{padding:20px;display:flex;justify-content:space-between;gap:20px} .section{padding:20px} table{width:100%;border-collapse:collapse;margin-top:10px} th,td{padding:12px;border-bottom:1px solid #f1f1f1;text-align:left} .product-img{width:120px;height:120px;object-fit:cover;border-radius:10px;border:1px solid #eee} .totals{padding:20px;text-align:right} .totals div{margin:6px 0} .badge{display:inline-block;padding:6px 12px;border-radius:20px;background:#f0f4f8;color:#223;font-weight:600} .contact{display:flex;gap:20px;justify-content:center;padding:18px 24px;background:linear-gradient(90deg,#f7fbff,#ffffff);border-top:1px solid #eef3f6;font-size:0.95rem;color:#334} .contact .item{display:flex;flex-direction:column;align-items:center} .contact .label{font-weight:600;color:#222;margin-bottom:6px} .footer-decor{height:40px;background:linear-gradient(90deg, rgba(52,152,219,0.06), rgba(39,174,96,0.04));margin-top:20px}</style>');
8990:             html.push('</head><body>');
8991: 
8992:             html.push('<div class="inv">');
8993:             html.push('<div class="inv-header">');
8994:             if (logoSrc) {
8995:                 html.push('<img src="' + esc(logoSrc) + '" class="logo" onerror="this.style.display=\'none\'">');
8996:             }
8997:             if (isReturn) {
8998:                 html.push('<div style="margin-left:12px;color:#c82333;font-weight:800;">');
8999:                 html.push('<span style="background:#fdecea;color:#c82333;padding:8px 14px;border-radius:8px;border:1px solid #f5c6cb;">Return Invoice</span>');
9000:                 html.push('</div>');
9001:             }
9002:             // decorative SVG (subtle)
9003:             html.push('<svg class="decor" viewBox="0 0 200 200" xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg">');
9004:             html.push('<defs>');
9005:             html.push('<linearGradient id="gBag" x1="0" x2="1"><stop offset="0" stop-color="#ffd6c6"/><stop offset="1" stop-color="#ffd1f0"/></linearGradient>');
9006:             html.push('<linearGradient id="gAcc" x1="0" x2="1"><stop offset="0" stop-color="#d6f0ff"/><stop offset="1" stop-color="#e6ffd9"/></linearGradient>');
9007:             html.push('</defs>');
9008:             html.push('<g transform="translate(10,10) scale(0.9)">');
9009:             // shopping bag
9010:             html.push('<rect x="20" y="50" width="80" height="60" rx="10" fill="url(#gBag)" stroke="#ffffff" stroke-opacity="0.25"/>');
9011:             html.push('<path d="M30 50 C30 32,90 32,90 50" stroke="#ffffff" stroke-width="6" stroke-linecap="round" fill="none" stroke-opacity="0.28"/>');
9012:             // small tag accessory
9013:             html.push('<rect x="110" y="60" width="36" height="22" rx="4" fill="url(#gAcc)" transform="rotate(18 128 71)" stroke="#ffffff" stroke-opacity="0.22"/>');
9014:             html.push('<circle cx="118" cy="70" r="3" fill="#ffffff" fill-opacity="0.6"/>');
9015:             // necklace/chain hint
9016:             html.push('<path d="M60 120 C70 110,110 110,120 120" stroke="#ffffff" stroke-width="4" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-opacity="0.22" fill="none"/>');
9017:             html.push('</g>');
9018:             html.push('</svg>');
9019:             html.push('<div><div class="inv-title">HANCES Invoice</div><div style="margin-top:6px;color:#556">#' + esc(invoice.id) + ' — ' + esc(invoiceDate) + '</div></div></div>');
9020: 
9021:             html.push('<div class="info">');
9022:             html.push('<div class="section"><strong>Customer</strong><br>' + esc(invoice.customerName || '-') + '<br>' + esc(invoice.customerPhone || '-') + '<br>' + esc(invoice.customerAddress || '-') + '</div>');
9023:             html.push('<div class="section"><strong>Payment</strong><br>' + esc(invoice.paymentMethod || '-') + '<br><span class="badge">' + esc(invoice.platform || '-') + '</span></div>');
9024:             html.push('</div>');
9025: 
9026:             html.push('<div class="section">');
9027:             html.push('<table><thead><tr><th>Product</th><th>Code</th><th>Variant</th><th>Qty</th><th>Unit</th><th>Subtotal</th></tr></thead><tbody>');
9028: 
9029:             var itemsList = (opts && opts.mode === 'return' && returnData) ? [{
9030:                 productId: returnData.productId || invoice.productId,
9031:                 selectedColor: returnData.selectedColor || invoice.selectedColor || '',
9032:                 quantity: returnData.quantity || invoice.quantity || 0,
9033:                 unitPrice: returnData.unitPrice || invoice.unitPrice || 0
9034:             }] : normalizeInvoiceItems(invoice);
9035: 
9036:             if (!itemsList || itemsList.length === 0) {
9037:                 itemsList = [{
9038:                     productId: invoice.productId,
9039:                     selectedColor: invoice.selectedColor || '',
9040:                     quantity: invoice.quantity || 0,
9041:                     unitPrice: invoice.unitPrice || 0
9042:                 }];
9043:             }
9044: 
9045:             itemsList.forEach(function(item) {
9046:                 var p = products.find(function(pp){ return pp.id == item.productId; }) || (product && product.id == item.productId ? product : null);
9047:                 var qty = parseFloat(item.quantity || 0);
9048:                 var unit = parseFloat(item.unitPrice || 0);
9049:                 var subtotal = unit * qty;
9050: 
9051:                 html.push('<tr>');
9052:                 if (p && p.image) {
9053:                     html.push('<td style="vertical-align:middle"><div style="display:flex;gap:12px;align-items:center"><img src="' + esc(p.image) + '" class="product-img">' + '<div>' + esc(p.name || '-') + '<br><small style="color:#777">' + esc(p.collection || '') + '</small></div></div></td>');
9054:                 } else {
9055:                     html.push('<td>' + esc(p ? p.name : 'منتج محذوف') + '</td>');
9056:                 }
9057:                 html.push('<td>' + esc(p ? (p.code || '-') : '-') + '</td>');
9058:                 html.push('<td>' + esc(item.selectedColor || '-') + '</td>');
9059:                 html.push('<td>' + esc(qty || 0) + '</td>');
9060:                 html.push('<td>EGP ' + unit.toFixed(2) + '</td>');
9061:                 html.push('<td>EGP ' + subtotal.toFixed(2) + '</td>');
9062:                 html.push('</tr>');
9063:             });
9064: 
9065:             html.push('</tbody></table></div>');
9066: 
9067:             html.push('<div class="totals">');
9068:             if (!isReturn) {
9069:                 html.push('<div>Gross: <strong>EGP ' + grossTotal.toFixed(2) + '</strong></div>');
9070:                 // Only show commission and net for noon/jumia platforms
9071:                 if ((invoice.platform === 'noon' || invoice.platform === 'jumia') && commissionAmount > 0) {
9072:                     html.push('<div>Commission: <strong>- EGP ' + commissionAmount.toFixed(2) + '</strong></div>');
9073:                 }
9074:                 if ((invoice.platform === 'noon' || invoice.platform === 'jumia') && shippingAmount > 0) {
9075:                     html.push('<div>Shipping: <strong>- EGP ' + shippingAmount.toFixed(2) + '</strong></div>');
9076:                 }
9077:                 // Only show Net Payable for noon/jumia platforms
9078:                 if (invoice.platform === 'noon' || invoice.platform === 'jumia') {
9079:                     html.push('<div style="margin-top:10px;font-size:1.15rem">Net Payable: <strong>EGP ' + netTotal.toFixed(2) + '</strong></div>');
9080:                 }
9081:             } else {
9082:                 // For returns show refund amount and losses
9083:                 html.push('<div>Refund Amount: <strong>EGP ' + grossTotal.toFixed(2) + '</strong></div>');
9084:                 if (commission && commission > 0) html.push('<div>Commission Loss: <strong>EGP ' + commission.toFixed(2) + '</strong></div>');
9085:                 if (shipping && shipping > 0) html.push('<div>Shipping Loss: <strong>EGP ' + shipping.toFixed(2) + '</strong></div>');
9086:                 if (returnData && returnData.purchaseLoss) html.push('<div>Purchase Loss: <strong>EGP ' + parseFloat(returnData.purchaseLoss || 0).toFixed(2) + '</strong></div>');
9087:                 html.push('<div style="margin-top:10px;font-size:1.15rem;color:#c82333">Total Loss: <strong>EGP ' + parseFloat(returnData.totalLoss || 0).toFixed(2) + '</strong></div>');
9088:                 if (returnData && returnData.reason) html.push('<div style="margin-top:8px;font-size:1rem">Reason: <strong>' + esc(returnData.reason) + '</strong></div>');
9089:             }
9090:             html.push('</div>');
9091: 
9092:             // Contact info block
9093:             html.push('<div class="contact">');
9094:             html.push('<div class="item"><div class="label">Phone</div><div>+201550801034</div></div>');
9095:             html.push('<div class="item"><div class="label">Email</div><div>info@hances.store</div></div>');

9106:         function deductQuantityFromStock(productId, selectedColor, quantity) {
9107:             const product = products.find(p => p.id == productId);
9108:             if (!product) return;
9109: 
9110:             if (product.colors && product.colors.length > 0) {
9111:                 if (selectedColor) {
9112:                     const colorData = product.colors.find(c => c.name === selectedColor);
9113:                     if (colorData) {
9114:                         colorData.quantity -= quantity;
9115:                     }
9116:                 } else if (product.colors.length === 1) {
9117:                     product.colors[0].quantity -= quantity;
9118:                 }
9119:                 // Update total quantity
9120:                 product.totalQuantity = product.colors.reduce((sum, color) => sum + color.quantity, 0);
9121:             } else {
9122:                 // Old format
9123:                 product.quantity = (product.quantity || 0) - quantity;
9124:             }
9125:         }
9126: 
9127:         function returnQuantityToStock(productId, selectedColor, quantity) {
9128:             const product = products.find(p => p.id == productId);
9129:             if (!product) {
9130:                 console.log('Product not found:', productId);
9131:                 return;
9132:             }
9133:             
9134:             console.log('Returning to stock:', product.name, 'color:', selectedColor, 'quantity:', quantity);
9135: 
9136:             if (product.colors && product.colors.length > 0) {
9137:                 if (selectedColor) {
9138:                     // Try to find color with case-insensitive matching
9139:                     const colorData = product.colors.find(c => c.name === selectedColor || c.name.toLowerCase() === selectedColor.toLowerCase());
9140:                     if (colorData) {
9141:                         console.log('Found color:', colorData.name, 'current quantity:', colorData.quantity);
9142:                         colorData.quantity += quantity;
9143:                         console.log('Updated quantity:', colorData.quantity);
9144:                     } else {
9145:                         console.log('Color not found, available colors:', product.colors.map(c => c.name));
9146:                     }
9147:                 } else if (product.colors.length === 1) {
9148:                     product.colors[0].quantity += quantity;
9149:                 }
9150:                 // Update total quantity
9151:                 product.totalQuantity = product.colors.reduce((sum, color) => sum + color.quantity, 0);
9152:             } else {
9153:                 // Old format
9154:                 product.quantity = (product.quantity || 0) + quantity;
9155:             }
9156:             
9157:             // Save changes to localStorage
9158:             saveData();
9159:             loadProducts();
9160:         }

9188:         function updateNotifications() {
9189:             const notificationsList = document.getElementById('notificationsList');
9190:             const notificationBadge = document.getElementById('notificationBadge');
9191:             
9192:             if (!notificationsList || !notificationBadge) return;
9193:             
9194:             // Get low stock products
9195:             const lowStockProducts = products.filter(p => {
9196:                 const qty = p.totalQuantity || p.quantity || p.stock || 0;
9197:                 const min = p.minStock || 5;
9198:                 return qty > 0 && qty <= min;
9199:             });
9200:             
9201:             const outOfStockProducts = products.filter(p => {
9202:                 const qty = p.totalQuantity || p.quantity || p.stock || 0;
9203:                 return qty === 0;
9204:             });
9205:             
9206:             // Get pending debts
9207:             const pendingDebts = debts ? debts.filter(d => !d.paid || d.paid < d.amount).length : 0;
9208:             
9209:             // Get pending purchases
9210:             const pendingPurchases = purchases ? purchases.filter(p => !p.status || p.status === 'pending').length : 0;
9211:             
9212:             const totalNotifications = lowStockProducts.length + outOfStockProducts.length + pendingDebts + pendingPurchases;
9213:             
9214:             // Update badge
9215:             if (totalNotifications > 0) {
9216:                 notificationBadge.style.display = 'block';
9217:                 notificationBadge.textContent = totalNotifications;
9218:             } else {
9219:                 notificationBadge.style.display = 'none';
9220:             }
9221:             
9222:             // Build notifications HTML
9223:             let html = '';
9224:             
9225:             // Out of stock products
9226:             if (outOfStockProducts.length > 0) {
9227:                 html += `<div style="padding: 8px; border-bottom: 1px solid #ffcccc; background: #fff5f5;">
9228:                     <div style="color: #dc3545; font-weight: bold; font-size: 0.85rem;">
9229:                         <i class="fas fa-times-circle"></i> نفد من المخزون (${outOfStockProducts.length})
9230:                     </div>`;
9231:                 outOfStockProducts.slice(0, 3).forEach(p => {
9232:                     html += `<div style="font-size: 0.8rem; color: #666; padding: 2px 0;">• ${p.name}</div>`;
9233:                 });
9234:                 if (outOfStockProducts.length > 3) {
9235:                     html += `<div style="font-size: 0.75rem; color: #999;">و${outOfStockProducts.length - 3} أخرى...</div>`;
9236:                 }
9237:                 html += `</div>`;
9238:             }
9239:             
9240:             // Low stock products
9241:             if (lowStockProducts.length > 0) {
9242:                 html += `<div style="padding: 8px; border-bottom: 1px solid #fff3cd; background: #fffaf0;">
9243:                     <div style="color: #e65100; font-weight: bold; font-size: 0.85rem;">
9244:                         <i class="fas fa-exclamation-triangle"></i> مخزون منخفض (${lowStockProducts.length})
9245:                     </div>`;
9246:                 lowStockProducts.slice(0, 3).forEach(p => {
9247:                     const qty = p.totalQuantity || p.quantity || 0;
9248:                     html += `<div style="font-size: 0.8rem; color: #666; padding: 2px 0;">• ${p.name} (${qty})</div>`;
9249:                 });
9250:                 if (lowStockProducts.length > 3) {
9251:                     html += `<div style="font-size: 0.75rem; color: #999;">و${lowStockProducts.length - 3} أخرى...</div>`;
9252:                 }
9253:                 html += `</div>`;
9254:             }
9255:             
9256:             // Pending debts
9257:             if (pendingDebts > 0) {
9258:                 html += `<div style="padding: 8px; border-bottom: 1px solid #f8d7da; background: #fff5f5;">
9259:                     <div style="color: #dc3545; font-weight: bold; font-size: 0.85rem;">
9260:                         <i class="fas fa-money-bill-wave"></i> ديون مستحقة (${pendingDebts})
9261:                     </div>
9262:                 </div>`;
9263:             }
9264:             
9265:             // Pending purchases
9266:             if (pendingPurchases > 0) {
9267:                 html += `<div style="padding: 8px; border-bottom: 1px solid #cce5ff; background: #f0f8ff;">
9268:                     <div style="color: #004085; font-weight: bold; font-size: 0.85rem;">
9269:                         <i class="fas fa-shopping-cart"></i> مشتريات معلقة (${pendingPurchases})
9270:                     </div>
9271:                 </div>`;
9272:             }
9273:             
9274:             if (html === '') {
9275:                 html = '<div style="padding: 15px; text-align: center; color: #28a745;"><i class="fas fa-check-circle"></i> لا توجد تنبيهات</div>';
9276:             }
9277:             
9278:             notificationsList.innerHTML = html;
9279:         }

9499:         // Import Products from Excel
9500:         function importProductsFromExcel(event) {
9501:             const file = event.target.files[0];
9502:             if (!file) return;
9503:             
9504:             const reader = new FileReader();
9505:             reader.onload = function(e) {
9506:                 try {
9507:                     const data = new Uint8Array(e.target.result);
9508:                     const workbook = XLSX.read(data, {type: 'array'});
9509:                     const firstSheet = workbook.Sheets[workbook.SheetNames[0]];
9510:                     const jsonData = XLSX.utils.sheet_to_json(firstSheet, {header: 1});
9511:                     
9512:                     if (jsonData.length < 2) {
9513:                         showErrorMessage('الملف فارغ أو لا يحتوي على بيانات');
9514:                         return;
9515:                     }
9516:                     
9517:                     const headers = jsonData[0].map(h => h.toString().trim());
9518:                     const importedProducts = [];
9519:                     let importedCount = 0;
9520:                     
9521:                     for (let i = 1; i < jsonData.length; i++) {
9522:                         const row = jsonData[i];
9523:                         if (!row || row.length === 0) continue;
9524:                         
9525:                         const product = {
9526:                             id: Date.now() + Math.random(),
9527:                             name: getValueByHeader(headers, row, ['اسم المنتج', 'name', 'Name']) || '',
9528:                             code: getValueByHeader(headers, row, ['الكود', 'code', 'Code']) || generateProductCode(),
9529:                             color: getValueByHeader(headers, row, ['اللون', 'color', 'Color']) || '',
9530:                             colorCode: getValueByHeader(headers, row, ['كود اللون', 'colorCode']) || '',
9531:                             collection: getValueByHeader(headers, row, ['الكوليكشن', 'collection', 'Collection']) || 'عام',
9532:                             supplier: getValueByHeader(headers, row, ['التاجر', 'supplier', 'Supplier']) || '',
9533:                             purchasePrice: parseFloat(getValueByHeader(headers, row, ['سعر الشراء', 'purchasePrice', 'سعر التكلفة'])) || 0,
9534:                             sellingPrice: parseFloat(getValueByHeader(headers, row, ['سعر البيع', 'sellingPrice', 'سعر']) || 0),
9535:                             quantity: parseInt(getValueByHeader(headers, row, ['الكمية', 'quantity', 'العدد'])) || 0,
9536:                             totalQuantity: parseInt(getValueByHeader(headers, row, ['الكمية', 'quantity', 'العدد'])) || 0,
9537:                             minStock: 5,
9538:                             date: new Date().toISOString()
9539:                         };
9540:                         
9541:                         if (product.name) {
9542:                             importedProducts.push(product);
9543:                             importedCount++;
9544:                         }
9545:                     }
9546:                     
9547:                     if (importedCount > 0) {
9548:                         products = [...products, ...importedProducts];
9549:                         saveData();
9550:                         loadProducts();
9551:                         updateDashboard();
9552:                         showSuccessMessage('تم استيراد ' + importedCount + ' منتج بنجاح!');
9553:                     } else {
9554:                         showErrorMessage('لم يتم العثور على منتجات صالحة للاستيراد');
9555:                     }
9556:                     
9557:                 } catch (error) {
9558:                     console.error('Import error:', error);
9559:                     showErrorMessage('حدث خطأ أثناء استيراد الملف: ' + error.message);
9560:                 }
9561:             };
9562:             
9563:             reader.readAsArrayBuffer(file);
9564:             event.target.value = '';
9565:         }
9566:         
9567:         function getValueByHeader(headers, row, possibleHeaders) {
9568:             for (let h of possibleHeaders) {
9569:                 const index = headers.findIndex(header => header === h || header.toLowerCase() === h.toLowerCase());
9570:                 if (index !== -1 && row[index] !== undefined) {
9571:                     return row[index];
9572:                 }
9573:             }
9574:             return null;
9575:         }
9576:         
9577:         function generateProductCode() {
9578:             return 'PRD' + Date.now().toString().slice(-6);
9579:         }

9798:                 // Image compression function - more aggressive compression
9799:                 function compressImage(file, maxWidth = 400, quality = 0.4) {
9800:                     return new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
9801:                         const reader = new FileReader();
9802:                         reader.readAsDataURL(file);
9803:                         reader.onload = function(event) {
9804:                             const img = new Image();
9805:                             img.src = event.target.result;
9806:                             img.onload = function() {
9807:                                 const canvas = document.createElement('canvas');
9808:                                 let width = img.width;
9809:                                 let height = img.height;
9810:                                 
9811:                                 // Resize if needed - more aggressive
9812:                                 if (width > maxWidth) {
9813:                                     height = (height * maxWidth) / width;
9814:                                     width = maxWidth;
9815:                                 }
9816:                                 
9817:                                 canvas.width = width;
9818:                                 canvas.height = height;
9819:                                 const ctx = canvas.getContext('2d');
9820:                                 
9821:                                 // Draw with smoothing for better quality
9822:                                 ctx.imageSmoothingEnabled = true;
9823:                                 ctx.imageSmoothingQuality = 'medium';
9824:                                 ctx.drawImage(img, 0, 0, width, height);
9825:                                 
9826:                                 // Compress and get base64 - use JPEG with lower quality
9827:                                 const compressedData = canvas.toDataURL('image/jpeg', quality);
9828:                                 console.log('Original size:', file.size, 'Compressed size:', compressedData.length);
9829:                                 resolve(compressedData);
9830:                             };
9831:                             img.onerror = reject;
9832:                         };
9833:                         reader.onerror = reject;
9834:                     });
9835:                 }
9836: 
9837:                 if (imageFile) {
9838:                     // Compress image before saving
9839:                     compressImage(imageFile).then(function(compressedImage) {
9840:                         productData.image = compressedImage;
9841:                         saveProduct(productData);
9842:                     }).catch(function(err) {
9843:                         console.error('Error compressing image:', err);
9844:                         // If compression fails, try original
9845:                         const reader = new FileReader();
9846:                         reader.onload = function(e) {
9847:                             productData.image = e.target.result;
9848:                             saveProduct(productData);
9849:                         };
9850:                         reader.readAsDataURL(imageFile);
9851:                     });
9852:                 } else {
9853:                     if (editingProductId) {
9854:                         const existingProduct = products.find(p => p.id === editingProductId);
9855:                         productData.image = existingProduct ? existingProduct.image : null;
9856:                     }
9857:                     saveProduct(productData);
9858:                 }
9859:             });
9860: 
9861:             function saveProduct(productData) {
9862:                 console.log('Saving product...', productData);
9863:                 
9864:                 try {
9865:                     if (editingProductId) {
9866:                         const index = products.findIndex(p => p.id === editingProductId);
9867:                         products[index] = { ...products[index], ...productData };
9868:                     } else {
9869:                         productData.id = Date.now();
9870:                         products.push(productData);
9871:                     }
9872: 
9873:                     console.log('Products array length:', products.length);
9874:                     
9875:                     // Try to save to localStorage
9876:                     try {
9877:                         localStorage.setItem('hances_products', JSON.stringify(products));
9878:                         console.log('Saved to localStorage successfully');
9879:                     } catch (e) {
9880:                         console.error('localStorage full:', e);
9881:                         showErrorMessage('التخزين المحلي ممتلئ! جرب مسح بعض البيانات.');
9882:                         // Keep in memory at least
9883:                     }
9884:                     
9885:                     // Show success message
9886:                     if (editingProductId) {
9887:                         showSuccessMessage('تم تعديل المنتج بنجاح!');
9888:                     } else {
9889:                         showSuccessMessage('تم إضافة المنتج بنجاح!');
9890:                     }
9891:                     
9892:                     console.log('Data saved');
9893:                     
9894:                     loadProducts();
9895:                     loadCollectionOptions();
9896:                     closeModal('productModal');
9897:                     
9898:                 } catch (e) {
9899:                     console.error('Error saving product:', e);
9900:                     showErrorMessage('حدث خطأ: ' + e.message);
9901:                 }
9902:             }

9946:                 if (!customerName || !customerPhone || !customerAddress || !platform || !paymentMethod || !deliveryStatus) {
9947:                     let missingFields = [];

9962:                 const totalShipping = currentInvoiceItems.reduce((sum, item) => sum + (parseFloat(item.shipping) || 0), 0);

9986:                     totalCommission: totalCommission,

10191:             // Save Expense with Capital Deduction (Confirmation Button)
10192:             function saveExpenseWithConfirmation() {
10193:                 console.log('Saving expense with confirmation...');
10194:                 
10195:                 const type = document.getElementById('expenseType').value;
10196:                 const amount = parseFloat(document.getElementById('expenseAmount').value);
10197:                 const description = document.getElementById('expenseDescription').value.trim();
10198:                 const date = document.getElementById('expenseDate').value;
10199:                 const customType = document.getElementById('expenseTypeCustom').value.trim();
10200:                 const finalType = type === 'custom' ? customType : type;
10201: 
10202:                 const hasItems = currentExpenseItems && currentExpenseItems.length > 0;
10203:                 if (!hasItems && (!finalType || !amount || !description || !date)) {
10204:                     showErrorMessage('يرجى ملء جميع الحقول المطلوبة');
10205:                     return;
10206:                 }
10207: 
10208:                 let itemsToSave = [];
10209:                 let totalAmount = 0;
10210:                 
10211:                 if (hasItems) {
10212:                     itemsToSave = currentExpenseItems.map(i => ({ ...i }));
10213:                     totalAmount = itemsToSave.reduce((sum, item) => sum + parseFloat(item.amount || 0), 0);
10214:                 } else {
10215:                     itemsToSave = [{
10216:                         type: finalType,
10217:                         amount,
10218:                         description,
10219:                         date: new Date(date).toISOString()
10220:                     }];
10221:                     totalAmount = amount;
10222:                 }
10223: 
10224:                 // Save expenses - not confirmed yet
10225:                 itemsToSave.forEach(item => {
10226:                     expenses.push({
10227:                         id: Date.now() + Math.floor(Math.random() * 1000),
10228:                         type: item.type,
10229:                         amount: parseFloat(item.amount || 0),
10230:                         description: item.description,
10231:                         date: item.date,
10232:                         confirmed: false
10233:                     });
10234:                 });
10235: 
10236:                 console.log('Expense saved without deduction. Amount:', totalAmount);
10237: 
10238:                 console.log('Expense saved and deducted from capital. Amount:', totalAmount, 'New capital:', capitalAmount);
10239:                 
10240:                 saveData();
10241:                 loadExpenses();
10242:                 updateDashboard();
10243:                 currentExpenseItems = [];
10244:                 renderExpenseItems();
10245:                 closeModal('expenseModal');
10246:                 showSuccessMessage('تم إضافة المصروفات وتم خصم ' + totalAmount.toFixed(2) + ' جنيه من رأس المال!');
10247:             }
10248: 
10249:             // Return Form Handler
10250:             document.getElementById('returnForm').addEventListener('submit', function(e) {
10251:                 e.preventDefault();
10252:                 
10253:                 const invoiceId = parseInt(document.getElementById('returnInvoice').value);
10254:                 let reason = document.getElementById('returnReason').value;
10255:                 const reasonTextInput = document.getElementById('returnReasonText');
10256:                 if (reason === 'other' && reasonTextInput) {
10257:                     const txt = (reasonTextInput.value || '').trim();
10258:                     if (!txt) { alert('Please enter the return reason'); return; }
10259:                     reason = txt;
10260:                 }
10261:                 const platform = document.getElementById('returnPlatform') ? document.getElementById('returnPlatform').value : '';
10262:                 const shippingCompanyId = document.getElementById('returnShippingCompany') ? document.getElementById('returnShippingCompany').value : '';
10263:                 const refunded = document.getElementById('returnRefunded') ? !!document.getElementById('returnRefunded').checked : false;
10264:                 
10265:                 if (!invoiceId) {
10266:                     alert('Please select an invoice');
10267:                     return;
10268:                 }
10269:                 
10270:                 if (currentReturnItems.length === 0) {
10271:                     alert('Please add at least one product to return');
10272:                     return;
10273:                 }
10274:                 
10275:                 if (!reason) {
10276:                     alert('Please select a return reason');
10277:                     return;
10278:                 }
10279:                 
10280:                 const invoice = invoices.find(i => i.id === invoiceId);
10281:                 if (!invoice) { alert('Invoice not found'); return; }
10282:                 
10283:                 // Calculate totals
10284:                 let totalRefund = 0;
10285:                 let totalPurchaseLoss = 0;
10286:                 let totalCommissionLoss = 0;
10287:                 let totalShippingLoss = 0;
10288:                 
10289:                 // Calculate per-unit values from original invoice
10290:                 const invoiceItems = normalizeInvoiceItems(invoice);
10291:                 const perUnitCommission = invoice.quantity ? (invoice.commission || 0) / invoice.quantity : 0;
10292:                 const perUnitShipping = invoice.quantity ? (invoice.shipping || 0) / invoice.quantity : 0;
10293:                 
10294:                 currentReturnItems.forEach(item => {
10295:                     totalRefund += item.total;
10296:                     totalPurchaseLoss += (item.product ? (item.product.purchasePrice || 0) : 0) * item.quantity;
10297:                     totalCommissionLoss += perUnitCommission * item.quantity;
10298:                     totalShippingLoss += perUnitShipping * item.quantity;
10299:                 });
10300:                 
10301:                 if (!confirm(`Confirm return creation:\n\nInvoice: #${invoice.id}\nCustomer: ${invoice.customerName}\nProducts: ${currentReturnItems.length}\nTotal Refund: EGP ${totalRefund.toFixed(2)}\n\n✅ Quantity will be returned to stock automatically`)) return;
10302:                 
10303:                 // Create return records for each item
10304:                 const returnRecords = currentReturnItems.map(item => {
10305:                     return {
10306:                         id: Date.now() + Math.random(),
10307:                         invoiceId,
10308:                         productId: item.productId,
10309:                         itemIndex: item.itemIndex,
10310:                         customerName: invoice.customerName,
10311:                         selectedColor: item.selectedColor,
10312:                         quantity: item.quantity,
10313:                         amount: item.total,
10314:                         reason,
10315:                         customerNote: document.getElementById('returnCustomerNote').value || '',
10316:                         platform,
10317:                         shippingCompanyId,
10318:                         refunded,
10319:                         receivedByShipper: false,
10320:                         commissionLoss: perUnitCommission * item.quantity,
10321:                         shippingLoss: perUnitShipping * item.quantity,
10322:                         purchaseLoss: (item.product ? (item.product.purchasePrice || 0) : 0) * item.quantity,
10323:                         totalLoss: (perUnitCommission + perUnitShipping + (item.product ? (item.product.purchasePrice || 0) : 0)) * item.quantity,
10324:                         date: new Date().toISOString()
10325:                     };
10326:                 });
10327:                 
10328:                 // Return quantities to stock and save returns
10329:                 currentReturnItems.forEach(item => {
10330:                     if (item.product) {
10331:                         returnQuantityToStock(item.productId, item.selectedColor, item.quantity);
10332:                     }
10333:                 });
10334:                 
10335:                 returns.push(...returnRecords);
10336:                 saveData();
10337:                 loadReturns();
10338:                 loadProducts();
10339:                 updateDashboard();
10340:                 closeModal('returnModal');
10341:                 
10342:                 // Reset return items
10343:                 currentReturnItems = [];
10344:                 renderReturnItems();
10345:                 
10346:                 alert('Return created successfully! Quantities returned to stock.');
10347:             });
10348:             
10349:             console.log('✅ HANCES PRO System Loaded Successfully!');
10350:         });

10378:         // Chart initialization functions
10379:         function initDailySalesChart() {
10380:             const ctx = document.getElementById('dailySalesChart');
10381:             if (!ctx) return;
10382:             
10383:             // Get last 7 days data
10384:             const labels = [];
10385:             const data = [];
10386:             for (let i = 6; i >= 0; i--) {
10387:                 const date = new Date();
10388:                 date.setDate(date.getDate() - i);
10389:                 const dateStr = date.toLocaleDateString('ar-EG');
10390:                 labels.push(dateStr);
10391:                 
10392:                 const dayTotal = invoices
10393:                     .filter(inv => inv.date && new Date(inv.date).toLocaleDateString('ar-EG') === dateStr)
10394:                     .reduce((sum, inv) => sum + (inv.total || 0), 0);
10395:                 data.push(dayTotal);
10396:             }
10397:             
10398:             new Chart(ctx, {
10399:                 type: 'line',
10400:                 data: {
10401:                     labels: labels,
10402:                     datasets: [{
10403:                         label: 'المبيعات (ج.م)',
10404:                         data: data,
10405:                         borderColor: '#28a745',
10406:                         backgroundColor: 'rgba(40, 167, 69, 0.1)',
10407:                         fill: true,
10408:                         tension: 0.4
10409:                     }]
10410:                 },
10411:                 options: {
10412:                     responsive: true,
10413:                     plugins: {
10414:                         legend: { display: false }
10415:                     },
10416:                     scales: {
10417:                         y: { beginAtZero: true }
10418:                     }
10419:                 }
10420:             });
10421:         }
10422:         
10423:         function initCollectionSalesChart() {
10424:             const ctx = document.getElementById('collectionSalesChart');
10425:             if (!ctx) return;
10426:             
10427:             const collectionData = {};
10428:             invoices.forEach(inv => {
10429:                 if (inv.items) {
10430:                     inv.items.forEach(item => {
10431:                         const product = products.find(p => p.id === item.productId);
10432:                         const collection = product ? product.collection : 'أخرى';
10433:                         collectionData[collection] = (collectionData[collection] || 0) + item.total;
10434:                     });
10435:                 }
10436:             });
10437:             
10438:             const labels = Object.keys(collectionData);
10439:             const data = Object.values(collectionData);
10440:             
10441:             new Chart(ctx, {
10442:                 type: 'doughnut',
10443:                 data: {
10444:                     labels: labels,
10445:                     datasets: [{
10446:                         data: data,
10447:                         backgroundColor: [
10448:                             '#28a745', '#17a2b8', '#ffc107', '#dc3545', '#6b8273', '#ff5722', '#9c27b0'
10449:                         ]
10450:                     }]
10451:                 },
10452:                 options: {
10453:                     responsive: true,
10454:                     plugins: {
10455:                         legend: { position: 'bottom' }
10456:                     }
10457:                 }
10458:             });
10459:         }
10460:         
10461:         function initWeeklySalesChart() {
10462:             const ctx = document.getElementById('weeklySalesChart');
10463:             if (!ctx) return;
10464:             
10465:             const weekData = {};
10466:             for (let i = 3; i >= 0; i--) {
10467:                 const weekStart = new Date();
10468:                 weekStart.setDate(weekStart.getDate() - (i * 7) - weekStart.getDay());
10469:                 const weekEnd = new Date(weekStart);
10470:                 weekEnd.setDate(weekEnd.getDate() + 6);
10471:                 const weekKey = `${weekStart.toLocaleDateString('ar-EG')} - ${weekEnd.toLocaleDateString('ar-EG')}`;
10472:                 
10473:                 weekData[weekKey] = invoices
10474:                     .filter(inv => {
10475:                         if (!inv.date) return false;
10476:                         const invDate = new Date(inv.date);
10477:                         return invDate >= weekStart && invDate <= weekEnd;
10478:                     })
10479:                     .reduce((sum, inv) => sum + (inv.total || 0), 0);
10480:             }
10481:             
10482:             new Chart(ctx, {
10483:                 type: 'bar',
10484:                 data: {
10485:                     labels: Object.keys(weekData),
10486:                     datasets: [{
10487:                         label: 'المبيعات (ج.م)',
10488:                         data: Object.values(weekData),
10489:                         backgroundColor: '#17a2b8'
10490:                     }]
10491:                 },
10492:                 options: {
10493:                     responsive: true,
10494:                     plugins: {
10495:                         legend: { display: false }
10496:                     },
10497:                     scales: {
10498:                         y: { beginAtZero: true }
10499:                     }
10500:                 }
10501:             });
10502:         }
10503:         
10504:         function initTopProductsChart() {
10505:             const ctx = document.getElementById('topProductsChart');
10506:             if (!ctx) return;
10507:             
10508:             const productSales = {};
10509:             invoices.forEach(inv => {
10510:                 if (inv.items) {
10511:                     inv.items.forEach(item => {
10512:                         const product = products.find(p => p.id === item.productId);
10513:                         const productName = product ? product.name : `منتج ${item.productId}`;
10514:                         productSales[productName] = (productSales[productName] || 0) + item.quantity;
10515:                     });
10516:                 }
10517:             });
10518:             
10519:             const sortedProducts = Object.entries(productSales)
10520:                 .sort((a, b) => b[1] - a[1])
10521:                 .slice(0, 5);
10522:             
10523:             new Chart(ctx, {
10524:                 type: 'bar',
10525:                 data: {
10526:                     labels: sortedProducts.map(p => p[0]),
10527:                     datasets: [{
10528:                         label: 'الكمية المباعة',
10529:                         data: sortedProducts.map(p => p[1]),
10530:                         backgroundColor: '#6b8273'
10531:                     }]
10532:                 },
10533:                 options: {
10534:                     responsive: true,
10535:                     indexAxis: 'y',
10536:                     plugins: {
10537:                         legend: { display: false }
10538:                     }
10539:                 }
10540:             });
10541:         }
10542:         
10543:         function initMonthlyExpensesChart() {
10544:             const ctx = document.getElementById('monthlyExpensesChart');
10545:             if (!ctx) return;
10546:             
10547:             const monthNames = [];
10548:             const monthData = [];
10549:             for (let i = 5; i >= 0; i--) {
10550:                 const date = new Date();
10551:                 date.setMonth(date.getMonth() - i);
10552:                 const monthName = date.toLocaleDateString('ar-EG', { month: 'long', year: 'numeric' });
10553:                 monthNames.push(monthName);
10554:                 
10555:                 const monthTotal = expenses
10556:                     .filter(exp => {
10557:                         if (!exp.date) return false;
10558:                         const expDate = new Date(exp.date);
10559:                         return expDate.getMonth() === date.getMonth() && expDate.getFullYear() === date.getFullYear();
10560:                     })
10561:                     .reduce((sum, exp) => sum + (exp.amount || 0), 0);
10562:                 monthData.push(monthTotal);
10563:             }
10564:             
10565:             new Chart(ctx, {
10566:                 type: 'line',
10567:                 data: {
10568:                     labels: monthNames,
10569:                     datasets: [{
10570:                         label: 'المصروفات (ج.م)',
10571:                         data: monthData,
10572:                         borderColor: '#dc3545',
10573:                         backgroundColor: 'rgba(220, 53, 69, 0.1)',
10574:                         fill: true,
10575:                         tension: 0.4
10576:                     }]
10577:                 },
10578:                 options: {
10579:                     responsive: true,
10580:                     plugins: {
10581:                         legend: { display: false }
10582:                     },
10583:                     scales: {
10584:                         y: { beginAtZero: true }
10585:                     }
10586:                 }
10587:             });
10588:         }

10590:         function generateSalesReport() {
10591:             if (invoices.length === 0) {
10592:                 showErrorMessage('لا توجد مبيعات لإنشاء التقرير');
10593:                 return;
10594:             }
10595:             
10596:             const totalSales = invoices.reduce((sum, invoice) => sum + invoice.total, 0);
10597:             const reportData = {
10598:                 totalInvoices: invoices.length,
10599:                 totalRevenue: totalSales,
10600:                 averageOrderValue: totalSales / invoices.length,
10601:                 generatedAt: new Date().toISOString()
10602:             };
10603:             
10604:             const dataStr = JSON.stringify(reportData, null, 2);
10605:             const dataBlob = new Blob([dataStr], {type: 'application/json'});
10606:             const url = URL.createObjectURL(dataBlob);
10607:             const link = document.createElement('a');
10608:             link.href = url;
10609:             link.download = `sales_report_${new Date().toISOString().split('T')[0]}.json`;
10610:             link.click();
10611:             
10612:             showSuccessMessage('تم تحميل تقرير المبيعات بنجاح!');
10613:         }
10614: 
10615:         function generateInventoryReport() {
10616:             if (products.length === 0) {
10617:                 showErrorMessage('لا توجد منتجات لإنشاء التقرير');
10618:                 return;
10619:             }
10620:             
10621:             const inventoryData = products.map(product => ({
10622:                 name: product.name,
10623:                 code: product.code,
10624:                 collection: product.collection,
10625:                 totalQuantity: product.totalQuantity || product.quantity || 0,
10626:                 colors: product.colors || [],
10627:                 sellingPrice: product.sellingPrice,
10628:                 purchasePrice: product.purchasePrice
10629:             }));
10630:             
10631:             const dataStr = JSON.stringify(inventoryData, null, 2);
10632:             const dataBlob = new Blob([dataStr], {type: 'application/json'});
10633:             const url = URL.createObjectURL(dataBlob);
10634:             const link = document.createElement('a');
10635:             link.href = url;
10636:             link.download = `inventory_report_${new Date().toISOString().split('T')[0]}.json`;
10637:             link.click();
10638:             
10639:             showSuccessMessage('تم تحميل تقرير المخزون بنجاح!');
10640:         }

10675:         // Upload logo as base64
10676:         function uploadLogo(input) {
10677:             const file = input.files[0];
10678:             if (!file) return;
10679:             
10680:             const reader = new FileReader();
10681:             reader.onload = function(e) {
10682:                 const base64 = e.target.result;
10683:                 document.getElementById('storeLogo').value = base64;
10684:                 document.getElementById('logoPreview').innerHTML = '<img src="' + base64 + '" style="max-width: 150px; max-height: 80px; border-radius: 8px; border: 2px solid #eee;">';
10685:             };
10686:             reader.readAsDataURL(file);
10687:         }

10691:             const totalInvoicesCount = invoices.length;
10692:             const totalSalesAmount = invoices.reduce((sum, invoice) => sum + invoice.total, 0);
10693:             const lastUpdateDate = new Date().toLocaleDateString('ar-EG');
10694:             
10695:             document.getElementById('totalProductsCount').textContent = totalProductsCount;
10696:             document.getElementById('totalInvoicesCount').textContent = totalInvoicesCount;
10697:             document.getElementById('totalSalesAmount').textContent = totalSalesAmount.toFixed(2) + ' جنيه';
10698:             document.getElementById('lastUpdateDate').textContent = lastUpdateDate;
10699:         }

10701:         function saveStoreSettings() {
10702:             const storeName = document.getElementById('storeName').value.trim();
10703:             const storePhone = document.getElementById('storePhone').value.trim();
10704:             const storeEmail = document.getElementById('storeEmail').value.trim();
10705:             const storeWebsite = document.getElementById('storeWebsite').value.trim();
10706:             const storeLogo = document.getElementById('storeLogo').value.trim();
10707:             
10708:             if (!storeName || !storePhone || !storeEmail || !storeWebsite) {
10709:                 showErrorMessage('يرجى ملء جميع الحقول');
10710:                 return;
10711:             }
10712:             
10713:             localStorage.setItem('hances_store_name', storeName);
10714:             localStorage.setItem('hances_store_phone', storePhone);
10715:             localStorage.setItem('hances_store_email', storeEmail);
10716:             localStorage.setItem('hances_store_website', storeWebsite);
10717:             localStorage.setItem('hances_store_logo', storeLogo);
10718:             
10719:             showSuccessMessage('تم حفظ إعدادات المتجر بنجاح!');
10720:         }

10745:         function importAllData(event) {
10746:             const file = event.target.files[0];
10747:             if (!file) return;
10748:             
10749:             const reader = new FileReader();
10750:             reader.onload = function(e) {
10751:                 try {
10752:                     const importedData = JSON.parse(e.target.result);
10753:                     
10754:                     if (confirm('هل أنت متأكد من استيراد البيانات؟ سيتم استبدال جميع البيانات الحالية!')) {
10755:                         products = importedData.products || [];
10756:                         invoices = importedData.invoices || [];
10757:                         expenses = importedData.expenses || [];
10758:                         returns = importedData.returns || [];
10759:                         collections = importedData.collections || [];
10760:                         suppliers = importedData.suppliers || [];
10761:                         expenseTypes = importedData.expenseTypes || [];
10762:                         
10763:                         saveData();
10764:                         showSuccessMessage('تم استيراد البيانات بنجاح!');
10765:                         
10766:                         // Refresh current section
10767:                         updateDashboard();
10768:                     }
10769:                 } catch (error) {
10770:                     showErrorMessage('خطأ في قراءة الملف. تأكد من صحة الملف.');
10771:                 }
10772:             };
10773:             reader.readAsText(file);
10774:         }

10798:         function changePassword() {
10799:             const currentPassword = document.getElementById('currentPassword').value;
10800:             const newPassword = document.getElementById('newPassword').value;
10801:             const confirmPassword = document.getElementById('confirmPassword').value;
10802:             
10803:             // Get stored password (default is 'admin123')
10804:             const storedPassword = localStorage.getItem('hances_system_password') || 'admin123';
10805:             
10806:             if (!currentPassword || !newPassword || !confirmPassword) {
10807:                 showErrorMessage('يرجى ملء جميع حقول كلمة المرور');
10808:                 return;
10809:             }
10810:             
10811:             if (currentPassword !== storedPassword) {
10812:                 showErrorMessage('كلمة المرور الحالية غير صحيحة');
10813:                 return;
10814:             }
10815:             
10816:             if (newPassword.length < 6) {
10817:                 showErrorMessage('كلمة المرور الجديدة يجب أن تكون 6 أحرف على الأقل');
10818:                 return;
10819:             }
10820:             
10821:             if (newPassword !== confirmPassword) {
10822:                 showErrorMessage('كلمة المرور الجديدة وتأكيدها غير متطابقين');
10823:                 return;
10824:             }
10825:             
10826:             localStorage.setItem('hances_system_password', newPassword);
10827:             
10828:             // Clear form
10829:             document.getElementById('currentPassword').value = '';
10830:             document.getElementById('newPassword').value = '';
10831:             document.getElementById('confirmPassword').value = '';
10832:             
10833:             showSuccessMessage('تم تغيير كلمة المرور بنجاح!');
10834:         }

10855:         async function restoreFromIndexedDB() {
10856:             try {
10857:                 const result = await restoreFromIndexedDBBackup();
10858:                 
10859:                 if (result.success) {
10860:                     if (confirm('هل أنت متأكد من استعادة البيانات من النسخ التلقائي؟\nسيتم استبدال جميع البيانات الحالية!')) {
10861:                         const data = result.data;
10862:                         
10863:                         // Restore all data
10864:                         products = data.products || [];
10865:                         invoices = data.invoices || [];
10866:                         expenses = data.expenses || [];
10867:                         collections = data.collections || [];
10868:                         suppliers = data.suppliers || [];
10869:                         expenseTypes = data.expenseTypes || [];
10870:                         customers = data.customers || [];
10871:                         capitalAmount = data.capitalAmount || 0;
10872:                         
10873:                         saveData();
10874:                         showSuccessMessage('تم استعادة البيانات بنجاح!');
10875:                         updateDashboard();
10876:                     }
10877:                 } else {
10878:                     showErrorMessage(result.message);
10879:                 }
10880:             } catch (error) {
10881:                 showErrorMessage('حدث خطأ أثناء استعادة البيانات!');
10882:             }
10883:         }

10899:                 totalInvoices: invoices.length,
10900:                 totalRevenue: invoices.reduce((sum, inv) => sum + inv.total, 0),
10901:                 totalExpenses: expenses.reduce((sum, exp) => sum + exp.amount, 0),
10902:                 totalReturns: returns.length,
10903:                 reportDate: new Date().toISOString(),
10904:                 products: products,
10905:                 invoices: invoices,
10906:                 expenses: expenses,

10921:         function generateMonthlyReport() {
10922:             const currentMonth = new Date().getMonth();
10923:             const currentYear = new Date().getFullYear();
10924:             
10925:             const monthlyInvoices = invoices.filter(inv => {
10926:                 const invDate = new Date(inv.date);
10927:                 return invDate.getMonth() === currentMonth && invDate.getFullYear() === currentYear;
10928:             });
10929:             
10930:             const monthlyExpenses = expenses.filter(exp => {
10931:                 const expDate = new Date(exp.date);
10932:                 return expDate.getMonth() === currentMonth && expDate.getFullYear() === currentYear;
10933:             });
10934:             
10935:             const reportData = {
10936:                 month: currentMonth + 1,
10937:                 year: currentYear,
10938:                 totalInvoices: monthlyInvoices.length,
10939:                 totalRevenue: monthlyInvoices.reduce((sum, inv) => sum + inv.total, 0),
10940:                 totalExpenses: monthlyExpenses.reduce((sum, exp) => sum + exp.amount, 0),
10941:                 invoices: monthlyInvoices,
10942:                 expenses: monthlyExpenses,
10943:                 reportDate: new Date().toISOString()
10944:             };
10945:             
10946:             const reportStr = JSON.stringify(reportData, null, 2);
10947:             const reportBlob = new Blob([reportStr], {type: 'application/json'});
10948:             const url = URL.createObjectURL(reportBlob);
10949:             const link = document.createElement('a');
10950:             link.href = url;
10951:             link.download = `hances_monthly_report_${currentYear}_${(currentMonth + 1).toString().padStart(2, '0')}.json`;
10952:             link.click();
10953:             
10954:             showSuccessMessage('تم إنشاء التقرير الشهري وتحميله بنجاح!');
10955:         }

10957:         function generateSalesReport() {
10958:             const salesData = invoices.map(invoice => {
10959:                 const product = products.find(p => p.id == invoice.productId);
10960:                 return {
10961:                     invoiceId: invoice.id,
10962:                     customerName: invoice.customerName,
10963:                     productName: product ? product.name : 'منتج محذوف',
10964:                     productCode: product ? product.code : 'N/A',
10965:                     quantity: invoice.quantity,
10966:                     unitPrice: invoice.unitPrice,
10967:                     total: invoice.total,
10968:                     platform: invoice.platform,
10969:                     date: invoice.date
10970:                 };
10971:             });
10972:             
10973:             const reportData = {
10974:                 reportType: 'Sales Report',
10975:                 totalSales: salesData.length,
10976:                 totalRevenue: salesData.reduce((sum, sale) => sum + sale.total, 0),
10977:                 sales: salesData,
10978:                 reportDate: new Date().toISOString()
10979:             };
10980:             
10981:             const reportStr = JSON.stringify(reportData, null, 2);
10982:             const reportBlob = new Blob([reportStr], {type: 'application/json'});
10983:             const url = URL.createObjectURL(reportBlob);
10984:             const link = document.createElement('a');
10985:             link.href = url;
10986:             link.download = `hances_sales_report_${new Date().toISOString().split('T')[0]}.json`;
10987:             link.click();
10988:             
10989:             showSuccessMessage('تم إنشاء تقرير المبيعات وتحميله بنجاح!');
10990:         }

10992:         function generateInventoryReport() {
10993:             const inventoryData = products.map(product => {
10994:                 const totalQuantity = product.totalQuantity || product.quantity || 0;
10995:                 const soldQuantity = invoices
10996:                     .filter(inv => inv.productId == product.id)
10997:                     .reduce((sum, inv) => sum + inv.quantity, 0);
10998:                 
10999:                 return {
11000:                     productId: product.id,
11001:                     productName: product.name,
11002:                     productCode: product.code,
11003:                     collection: product.collection,
11004:                     currentStock: totalQuantity,
11005:                     soldQuantity: soldQuantity,
11006:                     purchasePrice: product.purchasePrice,
11007:                     sellingPrice: product.sellingPrice,
11008:                     colors: product.colors || []
11009:                 };
11010:             });
11011:             
11012:             const reportData = {
11013:                 reportType: 'Inventory Report',
11014:                 totalProducts: inventoryData.length,
11015:                 totalStock: inventoryData.reduce((sum, item) => sum + item.currentStock, 0),
11016:                 inventory: inventoryData,
11017:                 reportDate: new Date().toISOString()
11018:             };
11019:             
11020:             const reportStr = JSON.stringify(reportData, null, 2);
11021:             const reportBlob = new Blob([reportStr], {type: 'application/json'});
11022:             const url = URL.createObjectURL(reportBlob);
11023:             const link = document.createElement('a');
11024:             link.href = url;
11025:             link.download = `hances_inventory_report_${new Date().toISOString().split('T')[0]}.json`;
11026:             link.click();
11027:             
11028:             showSuccessMessage('تم إنشاء تقرير المخزون وتحميله بنجاح!');
11029:         }

11109:         function attemptLogin() {
11110:             const enteredPassword = document.getElementById('loginPassword').value;
11111:             const storedPassword = localStorage.getItem('hances_system_password') || 'admin123';
11112:             
11113:             if (enteredPassword === storedPassword) {
11114:                 sessionStorage.setItem('hances_logged_in', 'true');
11115:                 document.getElementById('loginModal').remove();
11116:                 showSuccessMessage('مرحباً بك في نظام HANCES!');
11117:             } else {
11118:                 document.getElementById('loginPassword').value = '';
11119:                 document.getElementById('loginPassword').style.borderColor = '#dc3545';
11120:                 document.getElementById('loginPassword').placeholder = 'كلمة مرور خاطئة - حاول مرة أخرى';
11121:                 setTimeout(() => {
11122:                     document.getElementById('loginPassword').style.borderColor = '#e9ecef';
11123:                     document.getElementById('loginPassword').placeholder = 'كلمة المرور';
11124:                 }, 2000);
11125:             }
11126:         }

11128:         // ========== REPORT GENERATION FUNCTIONS ==========
11129:         function generateSalesReportFull() {
11130:             const deliveredInvoices = invoices.filter(inv => inv.deliveryStatus === 'delivered');
11131:             
11132:             if (deliveredInvoices.length === 0) {
11133:                 alert('لا توجد مبيعات بعد');
11134:                 return;
11135:             }
11136: 
11137:             // Calculate sales by product
11138:             const productSales = {};
11139:             deliveredInvoices.forEach(invoice => {
11140:                 const items = normalizeInvoiceItems(invoice);
11141:                 items.forEach(item => {
11142:                     const product = products.find(p => p.id == item.productId);
11143:                     if (!productSales[item.productId]) {
11144:                         productSales[item.productId] = {
11145:                             productName: product?.name || 'منتج غير معروف',
11146:                             quantity: 0,
11147:                             revenue: 0,
11148:                             count: 0
11149:                         };
11150:                     }
11151:                     productSales[item.productId].quantity += item.quantity || 0;
11152:                     productSales[item.productId].revenue += item.netTotal || 0;
11153:                     productSales[item.productId].count += 1;
11154:                 });
11155:             });
11156: 
11157:             const reportData = Object.entries(productSales).map(([_, data]) => ({
11158:                 ...data,
11159:                 averagePerOrder: data.count > 0 ? (data.revenue / data.count).toFixed(2) : 0
11160:             }));
11161: 
11162:             const totalRevenue = reportData.reduce((sum, item) => sum + item.revenue, 0);
11163:             const totalQuantity = reportData.reduce((sum, item) => sum + item.quantity, 0);
11164: 
11165:             // Display Modal
11166:             const modal = document.createElement('div');
11167:             modal.className = 'modal';
11168:             modal.id = 'salesReportModal';
11169:             modal.innerHTML = `
11170:                 <div class="modal-content" style="max-width: 900px; max-height: 85vh; overflow-y: auto;">
11171:                     <div class="modal-header">
11172:                         <h3><i class="fas fa-shopping-cart"></i> تقرير المبيعات الشامل</h3>
11173:                         <button class="close-btn" onclick="document.getElementById('salesReportModal').remove()">&times;</button>
11174:                     </div>
11175:                     
11176:                     <div style="padding: 20px; background: #f8f9fa; margin: 20px 0; border-radius: 10px;">
11177:                         <div style="display: grid; grid-template-columns: repeat(3, 1fr); gap: 20px; margin-bottom: 15px;">
11178:                             <div style="background: white; padding: 15px; border-radius: 8px; text-align: center; border-left: 4px solid #28a745;">
11179:                                 <p style="color: #666; margin: 0 0 8px 0; font-size: 0.9rem;">إجمالي الإيرادات</p>
11180:                                 <p style="color: #28a745; font-size: 1.8rem; font-weight: bold; margin: 0;">${totalRevenue.toFixed(2)} ج.م</p>
11181:                             </div>
11182:                             <div style="background: white; padding: 15px; border-radius: 8px; text-align: center; border-left: 4px solid #17a2b8;">
11183:                                 <p style="color: #666; margin: 0 0 8px 0; font-size: 0.9rem;">إجمالي الكميات</p>
11184:                                 <p style="color: #17a2b8; font-size: 1.8rem; font-weight: bold; margin: 0;">${totalQuantity}</p>
11185:                             </div>
11186:                             <div style="background: white; padding: 15px; border-radius: 8px; text-align: center; border-left: 4px solid #ffc107;">
11187:                                 <p style="color: #666; margin: 0 0 8px 0; font-size: 0.9rem;">عدد الطلبات</p>
11188:                                 <p style="color: #ffc107; font-size: 1.8rem; font-weight: bold; margin: 0;">${deliveredInvoices.length}</p>
11189:                             </div>
11190:                         </div>
11191:                     </div>
11192: 
11193:                     <table style="width: 100%; border-collapse: collapse; margin-bottom: 20px;">
11194:                         <thead>
11195:                             <tr style="background: #f8f9fa; border-bottom: 2px solid #dee2e6;">
11196:                                 <th style="padding: 12px; text-align: right; border: 1px solid #dee2e6;">المنتج</th>
11197:                                 <th style="padding: 12px; text-align: center; border: 1px solid #dee2e6;">الكمية</th>
11198:                                 <th style="padding: 12px; text-align: center; border: 1px solid #dee2e6;">الإيرادات</th>
11199:                                 <th style="padding: 12px; text-align: center; border: 1px solid #dee2e6;">عدد الطلبات</th>
11200:                                 <th style="padding: 12px; text-align: center; border: 1px solid #dee2e6;">متوسط الطلب</th>
11201:                             </tr>
11202:                         </thead>
11203:                         <tbody>
11204:                             ${reportData.map(item => `
11205:                                 <tr style="border-bottom: 1px solid #dee2e6;">
11206:                                     <td style="padding: 12px; border: 1px solid #dee2e6;">${item.productName}</td>
11207:                                     <td style="padding: 12px; text-align: center; border: 1px solid #dee2e6;">${item.quantity}</td>
11208:                                     <td style="padding: 12px; text-align: center; border: 1px solid #dee2e6; color: #28a745; font-weight: bold;">${item.revenue.toFixed(2)}</td>
11209:                                     <td style="padding: 12px; text-align: center; border: 1px solid #dee2e6;">${item.count}</td>
11210:                                     <td style="padding: 12px; text-align: center; border: 1px solid #dee2e6;">${item.averagePerOrder}</td>
11211:                                 </tr>
11212:                             `).join('')}
11213:                         </tbody>
11214:                     </table>

11216:                     <div style="display: flex; gap: 10px; justify-content: center; margin-top: 20px;">
11217:                         <button class="btn btn-primary" onclick="exportSalesReportExcel()">
11218:                             <i class="fas fa-file-excel"></i> تصدير Excel

11220:                         <button class="btn btn-primary" onclick="exportSalesReportPDF()">
11221:                             <i class="fas fa-file-pdf"></i> تصدير PDF

11229:         function generateReturnsReportFull() {
11230:             if (returns.length === 0) {
11231:                 alert('لا توجد مرتجعات بعد');
11232:                 return;
11233:             }
11234: 
11235:             const returnsByPlatform = {};
11236:             const returnsByReason = {};
11237:             let totalLoss = 0;
11238:             let totalRefunded = 0;
11239: 
11240:             returns.forEach(ret => {
11241:                 // Group by platform
11242:                 if (!returnsByPlatform[ret.platform || 'غير محدد']) {
11243:                     returnsByPlatform[ret.platform || 'غير محدد'] = {
11244:                         count: 0,
11245:                         loss: 0,
11246:                         refunded: 0
11247:                     };
11248:                 }
11249:                 returnsByPlatform[ret.platform || 'غير محدد'].count += 1;
11250:                 returnsByPlatform[ret.platform || 'غير محدد'].loss += ret.totalLoss || 0;
11251:                 if (ret.refunded) returnsByPlatform[ret.platform || 'غير محدد'].refunded += 1;
11252: 
11253:                 // Group by reason
11254:                 const reason = ret.reason || 'أخرى';
11255:                 if (!returnsByReason[reason]) {
11256:                     returnsByReason[reason] = {
11257:                         count: 0,
11258:                         loss: 0
11259:                     };
11260:                 }
11261:                 returnsByReason[reason].count += 1;
11262:                 returnsByReason[reason].loss += ret.totalLoss || 0;
11263: 
11264:                 totalLoss += ret.totalLoss || 0;
11265:                 if (ret.refunded) totalRefunded += 1;
11266:             });
11267: 
11268:             const modal = document.createElement('div');
11269:             modal.className = 'modal';
11270:             modal.id = 'returnsReportModal';
11271:             modal.innerHTML = `
11272:                 <div class="modal-content" style="max-width: 900px; max-height: 85vh; overflow-y: auto;">
11273:                     <div class="modal-header">
11274:                         <h3><i class="fas fa-undo"></i> تقرير المرتجعات الشامل</h3>
11275:                         <button class="close-btn" onclick="document.getElementById('returnsReportModal').remove()">&times;</button>
11276:                     </div>
11277:                     
11278:                     <div style="padding: 20px; background: #f8f9fa; margin: 20px 0; border-radius: 10px;">
11279:                         <div style="display: grid; grid-template-columns: repeat(4, 1fr); gap: 20px; margin-bottom: 15px;">
11280:                             <div style="background: white; padding: 15px; border-radius: 8px; text-align: center; border-left: 4px solid #dc3545;">
11281:                                 <p style="color: #666; margin: 0 0 8px 0; font-size: 0.9rem;">إجمالي المرتجعات</p>
11282:                                 <p style="color: #dc3545; font-size: 1.8rem; font-weight: bold; margin: 0;">${returns.length}</p>
11283:                             </div>
11284:                             <div style="background: white; padding: 15px; border-radius: 8px; text-align: center; border-left: 4px solid #ff6b6b;">
11285:                                 <p style="color: #666; margin: 0 0 8px 0; font-size: 0.9rem;">إجمالي الخسائر</p>
11286:                                 <p style="color: #ff6b6b; font-size: 1.8rem; font-weight: bold; margin: 0;">${totalLoss.toFixed(2)} ج.م</p>
11287:                             </div>
11288:                             <div style="background: white; padding: 15px; border-radius: 8px; text-align: center; border-left: 4px solid #28a745;">
11289:                                 <p style="color: #666; margin: 0 0 8px 0; font-size: 0.9rem;">المسترجعة</p>
11290:                                 <p style="color: #28a745; font-size: 1.8rem; font-weight: bold; margin: 0;">${totalRefunded}</p>
11291:                             </div>
11292:                             <div style="background: white; padding: 15px; border-radius: 8px; text-align: center; border-left: 4px solid #17a2b8;">
11293:                                 <p style="color: #666; margin: 0 0 8px 0; font-size: 0.9rem;">معدل الاسترجاع</p>
11294:                                 <p style="color: #17a2b8; font-size: 1.8rem; font-weight: bold; margin: 0;">${((totalRefunded / returns.length) * 100).toFixed(1)}%</p>
11295:                             </div>
11296:                         </div>
11297:                     </div>
