11354:         function generateProfitReportDaily() {
11355:             const profitData = {};
11356:             
11357:             // Calculate daily profits
11358:             invoices.forEach(invoice => {
11359:                 if (invoice.deliveryStatus === 'delivered') {
11360:                     const date = invoice.date ? new Date(invoice.date).toLocaleDateString('ar-EG') : new Date().toLocaleDateString('ar-EG');
11361:                     if (!profitData[date]) {
11362:                         profitData[date] = { revenue: 0, returns: 0 };
11363:                     }
11364:                     profitData[date].revenue += getInvoiceRevenue(invoice) || 0;
11365:                 }
11366:             });
11367: 
11368:             returns.forEach(ret => {
11369:                 const date = ret.date || new Date().toLocaleDateString('ar-EG');
11370:                 if (!profitData[date]) {
11371:                     profitData[date] = { revenue: 0, returns: 0 };
11372:                 }
11373:                 profitData[date].returns += ret.totalLoss || 0;
11374:             });
11375: 
11376:             expenses.forEach(exp => {
11377:                 const date = exp.date || new Date().toLocaleDateString('ar-EG');
11378:                 if (!profitData[date]) {
11379:                     profitData[date] = { revenue: 0, returns: 0, expenses: 0 };
11380:                 }
11381:                 if (!profitData[date].expenses) profitData[date].expenses = 0;
11382:                 profitData[date].expenses += exp.amount || 0;
11383:             });
11384: 
11385:             const reportData = Object.entries(profitData)
11386:                 .sort((a, b) => new Date(a[0]) - new Date(b[0]))
11387:                 .map(([date, data]) => ({
11388:                     date,
11389:                     revenue: data.revenue,
11390:                     returns: data.returns,
11391:                     expenses: data.expenses || 0,
11392:                     profit: data.revenue - data.returns - (data.expenses || 0)
11393:                 }));
11394: 
11395:             if (reportData.length === 0) {
11396:                 alert('لا توجد بيانات لعرض التقرير');
11397:                 return;
11398:             }
11399: 
11400:             const totalRevenue = reportData.reduce((sum, item) => sum + item.revenue, 0);
11401:             const totalReturns = reportData.reduce((sum, item) => sum + item.returns, 0);
11402:             const totalExpenses = reportData.reduce((sum, item) => sum + item.expenses, 0);
11403:             const totalProfit = totalRevenue - totalReturns - totalExpenses;
11404: 
11405:             showProfitReportModal('التقرير اليومي', reportData, totalRevenue, totalReturns, totalExpenses, totalProfit, 'daily');
11406:         }
11407: 
11408:         function generateProfitReportWeekly() {
11409:             const profitData = {};
11410:             
11411:             invoices.forEach(invoice => {
11412:                 if (invoice.deliveryStatus === 'delivered') {
11413:                     const date = new Date(invoice.date || new Date());
11414:                     const weekStart = new Date(date);
11415:                     weekStart.setDate(weekStart.getDate() - weekStart.getDay());
11416:                     const weekKey = weekStart.toLocaleDateString('ar-EG');
11417:                     
11418:                     if (!profitData[weekKey]) {
11419:                         profitData[weekKey] = { revenue: 0, returns: 0, expenses: 0 };
11420:                     }
11421:                     profitData[weekKey].revenue += getInvoiceRevenue(invoice) || 0;
11422:                 }
11423:             });
11424: 
11425:             returns.forEach(ret => {
11426:                 const date = new Date(ret.date || new Date());
11427:                 const weekStart = new Date(date);
11428:                 weekStart.setDate(weekStart.getDate() - weekStart.getDay());
11429:                 const weekKey = weekStart.toLocaleDateString('ar-EG');
11430:                 
11431:                 if (!profitData[weekKey]) {
11432:                     profitData[weekKey] = { revenue: 0, returns: 0, expenses: 0 };
11433:                 }
11434:                 profitData[weekKey].returns += ret.totalLoss || 0;
11435:             });
11436: 
11437:             expenses.forEach(exp => {
11438:                 const date = new Date(exp.date || new Date());
11439:                 const weekStart = new Date(date);
11440:                 weekStart.setDate(weekStart.getDate() - weekStart.getDay());
11441:                 const weekKey = weekStart.toLocaleDateString('ar-EG');
11442:                 
11443:                 if (!profitData[weekKey]) {
11444:                     profitData[weekKey] = { revenue: 0, returns: 0, expenses: 0 };
11445:                 }
11446:                 profitData[weekKey].expenses += exp.amount || 0;
11447:             });
11448:

11450:                 .sort((a, b) => new Date(a[0]) - new Date(b[0]))
11451:                 .map(([date, data]) => ({
11452:                     date,
11453:                     revenue: data.revenue,
11454:                     returns: data.returns,
11455:                     expenses: data.expenses || 0,

11457:                 }));

11460:                 alert('لا توجد بيانات لعرض التقرير');
11461:                 return;
11462:             }
11463: 
11464:             const totalRevenue = reportData.reduce((sum, item) => sum + item.revenue, 0);
11465:             const totalReturns = reportData.reduce((sum, item) => sum + item.returns, 0);
11466:             const totalExpenses = reportData.reduce((sum, item) => sum + item.expenses, 0);
11467:             const totalProfit = totalRevenue - totalReturns - totalExpenses;

11490:                 
11491:                 if (!profitData[monthKey]) {
11492:                     profitData[monthKey] = { revenue: 0, returns: 0, expenses: 0 };
11493:                 }
11494:                 profitData[monthKey].returns += ret.totalLoss || 0;
11495:             });
11496:

11500:                 
11501:                 if (!profitData[monthKey]) {

11503:                 }

11592:                     </div>

11598:         function generateInventoryReportFull() {
11599:             if (products.length === 0) {
11600:                 alert('لا توجد منتجات بعد');
11601:                 return;
11602:             }
11603: 
11604:             const inventoryData = products.map(product => {
11605:                 let sold = 0;
11606:                 let returned = 0;
11607:                 
11608:                 invoices.forEach(invoice => {
11609:                     if (invoice.deliveryStatus === 'delivered') {
11610:                         const items = normalizeInvoiceItems(invoice);
11611:                         items.forEach(item => {
11612:                             if (item.productId == product.id) {
11613:                                 sold += item.quantity || 0;
11614:                             }
11615:                         });
11616:                     }
11617:                 });
11618: 
11619:                 returns.forEach(ret => {
11620:                     if (ret.productId == product.id) {
11621:                         returned += ret.quantity || 0;
11622:                     }
11623:                 });
11624: 
11625:                 const stock = (product.quantity || 0) - sold + returned;
11626:                 const value = stock * (product.purchasePrice || 0);
11627: 
11628:                 return {
11629:                     name: product.name,
11630:                     currentStock: product.quantity || 0,
11631:                     sold,
11632:                     returned,
11633:                     finalStock: stock,
11634:                     price: product.purchasePrice || 0,
11635:                     value: value,
11636:                     status: stock < 10 ? 'منخفض' : stock < 20 ? 'متوسط' : 'كافي'
11637:                 };
11638:             });
11639: 
11640:             const totalStock = inventoryData.reduce((sum, item) => sum + item.finalStock, 0);
11641:             const totalValue = inventoryData.reduce((sum, item) => sum + item.value, 0);
11642:             const lowStockItems = inventoryData.filter(item => item.finalStock < 10).length;
11643: 
11644:             const modal = document.createElement('div');
11645:             modal.className = 'modal';

11663:                             </div>
11664:                             <div style="background: white; padding: 15px; border-radius: 8px; text-align: center; border-left: 4px solid #ffc107;">

11670:                                 <p style="color: #dc3545; font-size: 1.8rem; font-weight: bold; margin: 0;">${lowStockItems}</p>
11671:                             </div>
11672:                         </div>

11683:                                 <th style="padding: 12px; text-align: center; border: 1px solid #dee2e6;">السعر</th>
11684:                                 <th style="padding: 12px; text-align: center; border: 1px solid #dee2e6;">القيمة</th>
11685:                                 <th style="padding: 12px; text-align: center; border: 1px solid #dee2e6;">الحالة</th>
11686:                             </tr>

11695:                                     <td style="padding: 12px; text-align: center; border: 1px solid #dee2e6; font-weight: bold; color: ${item.finalStock < 10 ? '#dc3545' : item.finalStock < 20 ? '#ffc107' : '#28a745'};">${item.finalStock}</td>

11699:                                         <span style="padding: 5px 10px; border-radius: 5px; background: ${item.status === 'منخفض' ? '#ffebee' : item.status === 'متوسط' ? '#fff3e0' : '#e8f5e9'}; color: ${item.status === 'منخفض' ? '#dc3545' : item.status === 'متوسط' ? '#ff9800' : '#28a745'}; font-weight: bold; font-size: 0.85rem;">
11700:                                             ${item.status}
11701:                                         </span>

11721:         // ========== EXPORT FUNCTIONS ==========
11722:         function downloadCSV(filename, rows) {
11723:             const escapeCell = (val) => {
11724:                 const s = String(val ?? '');
11725:                 if (s.includes('"') || s.includes(',') || s.includes('\n')) {
11726:                     return `"${s.replace(/"/g, '""')}"`;
11727:                 }
11728:                 return s;
11729:             };
11730:             const csv = rows.map(r => r.map(escapeCell).join(',')).join('\n');
11731:             const blob = new Blob([csv], { type: 'text/csv;charset=utf-8;' });
11732:             const url = URL.createObjectURL(blob);
11733:             const link = document.createElement('a');
11734:             link.href = url;
11735:             link.download = filename;
11736:             link.click();
11737:             URL.revokeObjectURL(url);
11738:         }
11739:         function exportSalesReportExcel() {
11740:             const deliveredInvoices = invoices.filter(inv => inv.deliveryStatus === 'delivered');
11741:             const productSales = {};
11742:             
11743:             deliveredInvoices.forEach(invoice => {
11744:                 const items = normalizeInvoiceItems(invoice);
11745:                 items.forEach(item => {
11746:                     const product = products.find(p => p.id == item.productId);
11747:                     if (!productSales[item.productId]) {
11748:                         productSales[item.productId] = {
11749:                             productName: product?.name || 'منتج غير معروف',
11750:                             quantity: 0,
11751:                             revenue: 0,
11752:                             count: 0
11753:                         };
11754:                     }
11755:                     productSales[item.productId].quantity += item.quantity || 0;
11756:                     productSales[item.productId].revenue += item.netTotal || 0;
11757:                     productSales[item.productId].count += 1;
11758:                 });
11759:             });
11760: 
11761:             const reportData = Object.entries(productSales).map(([_, data]) => ({
11762:                 'المنتج': data.productName,
11763:                 'الكمية': data.quantity,
11764:                 'الإيرادات': data.revenue.toFixed(2),
11765:                 'عدد الطلبات': data.count,
11766:                 'متوسط الطلب': (data.revenue / data.count).toFixed(2)
11767:             }));
11768: 
11769:             try {
11770:                 if (typeof XLSX === 'undefined') throw new Error('XLSX not loaded');
11771:                 const ws = XLSX.utils.json_to_sheet(reportData);
11772:                 const wb = XLSX.utils.book_new();
11773:                 XLSX.utils.book_append_sheet(wb, ws, "تقرير المبيعات");
11774:                 XLSX.writeFile(wb, `تقرير_المبيعات_${new Date().toLocaleDateString('ar-EG')}.xlsx`);
11775:             } catch (e) {
11776:                 const rows = [
11777:                     ['المنتج','الكمية','الإيرادات','عدد الطلبات','متوسط الطلب'],
11778:                     ...reportData.map(r => [r['المنتج'], r['الكمية'], r['الإيرادات'], r['عدد الطلبات'], r['متوسط الطلب']])
11779:                 ];
11780:                 downloadCSV(`تقرير_المبيعات_${new Date().toLocaleDateString('ar-EG')}.csv`, rows);
11781:             }
11782:         }
11783: 
11784:         function exportReturnsReportExcel() {
11785:             const platformReturns = {};
11786:             const reasonReturns = {};
11787: 
11788:             returns.forEach(ret => {
11789:                 if (!platformReturns[ret.platform || 'غير محدد']) {
11790:                     platformReturns[ret.platform || 'غير محدد'] = {
11791:                         'المنصة': ret.platform || 'غير محدد',
11792:                         'العدد': 0,
11793:                         'الخسائر': 0,
11794:                         'المسترجعة': 0
11795:                     };
11796:                 }
11797:                 platformReturns[ret.platform || 'غير محدد']['العدد'] += 1;
11798:                 platformReturns[ret.platform || 'غير محدد']['الخسائر'] += ret.totalLoss || 0;
11799:                 if (ret.refunded) platformReturns[ret.platform || 'غير محدد']['المسترجعة'] += 1;
11800: 
11801:                 const reason = ret.reason || 'أخرى';
11802:                 if (!reasonReturns[reason]) {
11803:                     reasonReturns[reason] = {
11804:                         'السبب': reason,
11805:                         'العدد': 0,
11806:                         'الخسائر': 0
11807:                     };
11808:                 }
11809:                 reasonReturns[reason]['العدد'] += 1;
11810:                 reasonReturns[reason]['الخسائر'] += ret.totalLoss || 0;
11811:             });
11812: 
11813:             const platformData = Object.values(platformReturns).map(item => ({
11814:                 'المنصة': item['المنصة'],
11815:                 'العدد': item['العدد'],
11816:                 'الخسائر': item['الخسائر'].toFixed(2),
11817:                 'المسترجعة': item['المسترجعة']
11818:             }));
11819: 
11820:             const reasonData = Object.values(reasonReturns).map(item => ({
11821:                 'السبب': item['السبب'],
11822:                 'العدد': item['العدد'],
11823:                 'الخسائر': item['الخسائر'].toFixed(2)
11824:             }));
11825: 
11826:             try {
11827:                 if (typeof XLSX === 'undefined') throw new Error('XLSX not loaded');
11828:                 const ws1 = XLSX.utils.json_to_sheet(platformData);
11829:                 const ws2 = XLSX.utils.json_to_sheet(reasonData);
11830:                 const wb = XLSX.utils.book_new();
11831:                 XLSX.utils.book_append_sheet(wb, ws1, "حسب المنصة");
11832:                 XLSX.utils.book_append_sheet(wb, ws2, "حسب السبب");
11833:                 XLSX.writeFile(wb, `تقرير_المرتجعات_${new Date().toLocaleDateString('ar-EG')}.xlsx`);
11834:             } catch (e) {
11835:                 const rows = [
11836:                     ['المنصة','العدد','الخسائر','المسترجعة'],
11837:                     ...platformData.map(r => [r['المنصة'], r['العدد'], r['الخسائر'], r['المسترجعة']])
11838:                 ];
11839:                 downloadCSV(`تقرير_المرتجعات_${new Date().toLocaleDateString('ar-EG')}.csv`, rows);
11840:             }
11841:         }
11842: 
11843:         function exportProfitReportExcel(type) {
11844:             const profitData = {};
11845:             
11846:             invoices.forEach(invoice => {
11847:                 if (invoice.deliveryStatus === 'delivered') {
11848:                     const date = type === 'daily' ? (invoice.date || new Date().toLocaleDateString('ar-EG')) :
11849:                                  type === 'weekly' ? new Date(invoice.date || new Date()).toLocaleDateString('ar-EG', { weekday: 'long' }) :
11850:                                  new Date(invoice.date || new Date()).toLocaleDateString('ar-EG', { month: '2-digit', year: 'numeric' });
11851:                     if (!profitData[date]) {
11852:                         profitData[date] = { revenue: 0, returns: 0, expenses: 0 };
11853:                     }
11854:                     profitData[date].revenue += getInvoiceRevenue(invoice) || 0;
11855:                 }
11856:             });
11857: 
11858:             returns.forEach(ret => {
11859:                 const date = type === 'daily' ? (ret.date || new Date().toLocaleDateString('ar-EG')) :
11860:                              type === 'weekly' ? new Date(ret.date || new Date()).toLocaleDateString('ar-EG', { weekday: 'long' }) :
11861:                              new Date(ret.date || new Date()).toLocaleDateString('ar-EG', { month: '2-digit', year: 'numeric' });
11862:                 if (!profitData[date]) {
11863:                     profitData[date] = { revenue: 0, returns: 0, expenses: 0 };
11864:                 }
11865:                 profitData[date].returns += ret.totalLoss || 0;
11866:             });
11867: 
11868:             expenses.forEach(exp => {
11869:                 const date = type === 'daily' ? (exp.date || new Date().toLocaleDateString('ar-EG')) :
11870:                              type === 'weekly' ? new Date(exp.date || new Date()).toLocaleDateString('ar-EG', { weekday: 'long' }) :
11871:                              new Date(exp.date || new Date()).toLocaleDateString('ar-EG', { month: '2-digit', year: 'numeric' });
11872:                 if (!profitData[date]) {
11873:                     profitData[date] = { revenue: 0, returns: 0, expenses: 0 };
11874:                 }
11875:                 profitData[date].expenses += exp.amount || 0;
11876:             });
11877: 
11878:             const reportData = Object.entries(profitData)
11879:                 .sort((a, b) => new Date(a[0]) - new Date(b[0]))
11880:                 .map(([date, data]) => ({
11881:                     'الفترة': date,
11882:                     'الإيرادات': data.revenue.toFixed(2),
11883:                     'الخسائر': data.returns.toFixed(2),
11884:                     'المصروفات': data.expenses.toFixed(2),
11885:                     'الربح الصافي': (data.revenue - data.returns - data.expenses).toFixed(2)
11886:                 }));
11887: 
11888:             const typeLabel = type === 'daily' ? 'يومي' : type === 'weekly' ? 'أسبوعي' : 'شهري';
11889:             try {
11890:                 if (typeof XLSX === 'undefined') throw new Error('XLSX not loaded');
11891:                 const ws = XLSX.utils.json_to_sheet(reportData);
11892:                 const wb = XLSX.utils.book_new();
11893:                 XLSX.utils.book_append_sheet(wb, ws, `التقرير ${typeLabel}`);
11894:                 XLSX.writeFile(wb, `تقرير_الربح_${typeLabel}_${new Date().toLocaleDateString('ar-EG')}.xlsx`);
11895:             } catch (e) {
11896:                 const rows = [
11897:                     ['الفترة','الإيرادات','الخسائر','المصروفات','الربح الصافي'],
11898:                     ...reportData.map(r => [r['الفترة'], r['الإيرادات'], r['الخسائر'], r['المصروفات'], r['الربح الصافي']])
11899:                 ];
11900:                 downloadCSV(`تقرير_الربح_${typeLabel}_${new Date().toLocaleDateString('ar-EG')}.csv`, rows);
11901:             }
11902:         }
11903: 
11904:         function exportInventoryReportExcel() {
11905:             const inventoryData = products.map(product => {
11906:                 let sold = 0;
11907:                 let returned = 0;
11908:                 
11909:                 invoices.forEach(invoice => {
11910:                     if (invoice.productId == product.id && invoice.deliveryStatus === 'delivered') {
11911:                         sold += invoice.quantity || 0;
11912:                     }
11913:                 });
11914: 
11915:                 returns.forEach(ret => {
11916:                     if (ret.productId == product.id) {
11917:                         returned += ret.quantity || 0;
11918:                     }
11919:                 });
11920: 
11921:                 const finalStock = (product.quantity || 0) - sold + returned;
11922: 
11923:                 return {
11924:                     'المنتج': product.name,
11925:                     'المخزون الحالي': product.quantity || 0,
11926:                     'المباع': sold,
11927:                     'المرتجع': returned,
11928:                     'المخزون النهائي': finalStock,
11929:                     'السعر': product.purchasePrice ? product.purchasePrice.toFixed(2) : 0,
11930:                     'القيمة': (finalStock * (product.purchasePrice || 0)).toFixed(2)
11931:                 };
11932:             });
11933: 
11934:             try {
11935:                 if (typeof XLSX === 'undefined') throw new Error('XLSX not loaded');
11936:                 const ws = XLSX.utils.json_to_sheet(inventoryData);
11937:                 const wb = XLSX.utils.book_new();
11938:                 XLSX.utils.book_append_sheet(wb, ws, "تقرير المخزون");
11939:                 XLSX.writeFile(wb, `تقرير_المخزون_${new Date().toLocaleDateString('ar-EG')}.xlsx`);
11940:             } catch (e) {
11941:                 const rows = [
11942:                     ['المنتج','المخزون الحالي','المباع','المرتجع','المخزون النهائي','السعر','القيمة'],
11943:                     ...inventoryData.map(r => [r['المنتج'], r['المخزون الحالي'], r['المباع'], r['المرتجع'], r['المخزون النهائي'], r['السعر'], r['القيمة']])
11944:                 ];
11945:                 downloadCSV(`تقرير_المخزون_${new Date().toLocaleDateString('ar-EG')}.csv`, rows);
11946:             }
11947:         }

12002:         // ==== DEBTS MANAGEMENT ====

12004:         function loadDebtsSection() {
12005:             updateDebtsStats();
12006:             loadCustomerDebtsTable();
12007:             loadSupplierDebtsTable();
12008:         }
12009: 
12010:         function updateDebtsStats() {
12011:             const totalCustomerDebt = customerDebts.reduce((sum, d) => sum + (d.amount - d.paid), 0);
12012:             const totalSupplierDebt = supplierDebts.reduce((sum, d) => sum + (d.amount - d.paid), 0);
12013:             const totalPaid = [...customerDebts, ...supplierDebts].reduce((sum, d) => sum + d.paid, 0);
12014:             
12015:             document.getElementById('totalCustomerDebts').textContent = totalCustomerDebt.toFixed(2) + ' جنيه';
12016:             document.getElementById('totalSupplierDebts').textContent = totalSupplierDebt.toFixed(2) + ' جنيه';
12017:             document.getElementById('totalPaidDebts').textContent = totalPaid.toFixed(2) + ' جنيه';
12018:         }
12019: 
12020:         function loadCustomerDebtsTable() {
12021:             const tbody = document.getElementById('customerDebtsTableBody');
12022:             if (customerDebts.length === 0) {
12023:                 tbody.innerHTML = '<tr><td colspan="7" style="text-align: center; padding: 50px; color: #666;"><i class="fas fa-users" style="font-size: 3rem; margin-bottom: 15px; opacity: 0.3;"></i><br>لا توجد ديون عملاء</td></tr>';
12024:                 return;
12025:             }
12026:             
12027:             tbody.innerHTML = customerDebts.map(d => `
12028:                 <tr>
12029:                     <td>${d.name}</td>
12030:                     <td>${d.phone || '-'}</td>
12031:                     <td>${d.amount.toFixed(2)}</td>
12032:                     <td>${d.paid.toFixed(2)}</td>
12033:                     <td style="color: #dc3545; font-weight: bold;">${(d.amount - d.paid).toFixed(2)}</td>
12034:                     <td>${d.dueDate || '-'}</td>
12035:                     <td>
12036:                         <button onclick="payDebt(${d.id}, 'customer')" class="btn btn-add" style="padding: 8px 15px; font-size: 0.9rem;">دفع</button>
12037:                         <button onclick="editDebt(${d.id}, 'customer')" class="btn btn-edit" style="padding: 8px 15px; font-size: 0.9rem;">تعديل</button>
12038:                         <button onclick="deleteDebt(${d.id}, 'customer')" class="btn btn-delete" style="padding: 8px 15px; font-size: 0.9rem;">حذف</button>
12039:                     </td>
12040:                 </tr>
12041:             `).join('');
12042:         }
12043: 
12044:         function loadSupplierDebtsTable() {

12046:             if (supplierDebts.length === 0) {

12063:                     </td>
12064:                 </tr>

12075:         function editDebt(id, type) {
12076:             const debts = type === 'customer' ? customerDebts : supplierDebts;
12077:             const debt = debts.find(d => d.id === id);
12078:             if (!debt) return;
12079:             
12080:             document.getElementById('debtType').value = type;
12081:             document.getElementById('debtPersonLabel').textContent = type === 'customer' ? 'اسم العميل' : 'اسم المورد';
12082:             document.getElementById('debtPersonName').value = debt.name;
12083:             document.getElementById('debtPersonPhone').value = debt.phone || '';
12084:             document.getElementById('debtAmount').value = debt.amount;
12085:             document.getElementById('debtPaid').value = debt.paid;
12086:             document.getElementById('debtDueDate').value = debt.dueDate || '';
12087:             document.getElementById('debtNotes').value = debt.notes || '';
12088:             document.getElementById('debtForm').dataset.editingId = id;
12089:             document.getElementById('debtModal').style.display = 'block';
12090:         }
12091: 
12092:         function deleteDebt(id, type) {
12093:             if (!confirm('هل أنت متأكد من حذف هذا الدين؟')) return;
12094:             
12095:             if (type === 'customer') {
12096:                 customerDebts = customerDebts.filter(d => d.id !== id);
12097:             } else {
12098:                 supplierDebts = supplierDebts.filter(d => d.id !== id);
12099:             }
12100:             saveData();
12101:             loadDebtsSection();
12102:         }
12103: 
12104:         document.getElementById('debtForm').addEventListener('submit', function(e) {
12105:             e.preventDefault();
12106:             
12107:             const type = document.getElementById('debtType').value;
12108:             const editingId = this.dataset.editingId;
12109:             
12110:             const debt = {
12111:                 id: editingId ? parseInt(editingId) : Date.now(),
12112:                 name: document.getElementById('debtPersonName').value,
12113:                 phone: document.getElementById('debtPersonPhone').value,
12114:                 amount: parseFloat(document.getElementById('debtAmount').value),
12115:                 paid: parseFloat(document.getElementById('debtPaid').value) || 0,
12116:                 dueDate: document.getElementById('debtDueDate').value,
12117:                 notes: document.getElementById('debtNotes').value,
12118:                 type: type,
12119:                 createdAt: new Date().toISOString()
12120:             };
12121:             
12122:             if (editingId) {
12123:                 if (type === 'customer') {
12124:                     const index = customerDebts.findIndex(d => d.id === parseInt(editingId));
12125:                     if (index !== -1) customerDebts[index] = debt;
12126:                 } else {
12127:                     const index = supplierDebts.findIndex(d => d.id === parseInt(editingId));
12128:                     if (index !== -1) supplierDebts[index] = debt;
12129:                 }
12130:             } else {
12131:                 if (type === 'customer') {
12132:                     customerDebts.push(debt);
12133:                 } else {
12134:                     supplierDebts.push(debt);
12135:                 }
12136:             }
12137:             
12138:             saveData();
12139:             closeModal('debtModal');
12140:             loadDebtsSection();
12141:             this.reset();
12142:             delete this.dataset.editingId;
12143:         });

12145:         function payDebt(id, type) {
12146:             document.getElementById('payDebtId').value = id;
12147:             document.getElementById('payDebtAmount').value = '';
12148:             document.getElementById('payDebtNotes').value = '';
12149:             document.getElementById('payDebtModal').style.display = 'block';
12150:         }
12151: 
12152:         document.getElementById('payDebtForm').addEventListener('submit', function(e) {
12153:             e.preventDefault();
12154:             
12155:             const id = parseInt(document.getElementById('payDebtId').value);
12156:             const amount = parseFloat(document.getElementById('payDebtAmount').value);
12157:             const notes = document.getElementById('payDebtNotes').value;
12158:             const type = document.querySelector('#payDebtForm').dataset.type || 'customer';
12159:             
12160:             // Find and update the debt
12161:             const debts = type === 'customer' ? customerDebts : supplierDebts;
12162:             const debt = debts.find(d => d.id === id);
12163:             
12164:             if (debt) {
12165:                 debt.paid = (debt.paid || 0) + amount;
12166:                 if (notes) {
12167:                     debt.notes = (debt.notes || '') + ' | ' + notes;
12168:                 }
12169:                 saveData();
12170:                 closeModal('payDebtModal');
12171:                 loadDebtsSection();
12172:             }
12173:         });

12312:         document.getElementById('bankTransactionForm').addEventListener('submit', function(e) {
12313:             e.preventDefault();
12314:             
12315:             const bankId = parseInt(document.getElementById('transactionBank').value);
12316:             const bank = bankAccounts.find(a => a.id === bankId);
12317:             const type = document.getElementById('transactionType').value;
12318:             const amount = parseFloat(document.getElementById('transactionAmount').value);
12319:             
12320:             if (!bank) return;
12321:             
12322:             // Update bank balance
12323:             if (type === 'deposit') {
12324:                 bank.balance += amount;
12325:             } else if (type === 'withdrawal') {
12326:                 bank.balance -= amount;
12327:             }
12328:             
12329:             const transaction = {
12330:                 id: Date.now(),
12331:                 bankId: bankId,
12332:                 bankName: bank.name,
12333:                 type: type,
12334:                 amount: amount,
12335:                 notes: document.getElementById('transactionNotes').value,
12336:                 date: new Date().toLocaleDateString('ar-EG'),
12337:                 time: new Date().toLocaleTimeString('ar-EG')
12338:             };
12339:             
12340:             bankTransactions.push(transaction);
12341:             saveData();
12342:             closeModal('bankTransactionModal');
12343:             loadBankAccountsSection();
12344:         });

12360:         
12361:         function renderStoreMoneyTable() {
12362:             const tbody = document.getElementById('storeMoneyTableBody');
12363:             if (storeMoney.length === 0) {
12364:                 tbody.innerHTML = `
12365:                     <tr>
12366:                         <td colspan="6" style="text-align: center; padding: 50px; color: #666;">
12367:                             <i class="fas fa-money-bill-wave" style="font-size: 3rem; margin-bottom: 15px; opacity: 0.3;"></i>
12368:                             <br>لا توجد مستحقات
12369:                         </td>
12370:                     </tr>
12371:                 `;
12372:                 return;
12373:             }
12374:             
12375:             tbody.innerHTML = storeMoney.map(entry => `
12376:                 <tr>
12377:                     <td>${new Date(entry.date).toLocaleDateString('ar-EG')}</td>
12378:                     <td><strong>${entry.debtor}</strong></td>
12379:                     <td style="color: #dc3545; font-weight: bold;">${entry.amount.toFixed(2)} جنيه</td>
12380:                     <td>${entry.dueDate ? new Date(entry.dueDate).toLocaleDateString('ar-EG') : '-'}</td>
12381:                     <td>
12382:                         ${entry.status === 'paid' ? 
12383:                             '<span style="color: #28a745; font-weight: bold;"><i class="fas fa-check-circle"></i> مسددة</span>' :
12384:                             '<span style="color: #dc3545; font-weight: bold;"><i class="fas fa-clock"></i> معلقة</span>'
12385:                         }
12386:                     </td>
12387:                     <td>
12388:                         <div class="product-actions">
12389:                             ${entry.status !== 'paid' ? 
12390:                                 `<button class="btn btn-add" onclick="markStoreMoneyAsPaid(${entry.id})" title="تم التحصيل (إضافة لرأس المال)" style="background: #28a745;">
12391:                                     <i class="fas fa-plus"></i>
12392:                                 </button>
12393:                                 <button class="btn" onclick="deductFromCapitalDirect(${entry.id})" title="خصم من رأس المال" style="background: #dc3545; color: white;">
12394:                                     <i class="fas fa-minus"></i>
12395:                                 </button>
12396:                                 <button class="btn btn-edit" onclick="editStoreMoneyEntry(${entry.id})" title="تعديل">
12397:                                     <i class="fas fa-edit"></i>
12398:                                 </button>` : ''
12399:                             }
12400:                             <button class="btn btn-delete" onclick="deleteStoreMoneyEntry(${entry.id})" title="حذف">
12401:                                 <i class="fas fa-trash"></i>
12402:                             </button>
12403:                         </div>
12404:                     </td>
12405:                 </tr>
12406:             `).join('');
12407:         }
12408:         
12409:         function updateStoreMoneyStats() {
12410:             const pending = storeMoney.filter(e => e.status !== 'paid').reduce((sum, e) => sum + e.amount, 0);
12411:             const paid = storeMoney.filter(e => e.status === 'paid').reduce((sum, e) => sum + e.amount, 0);
12412:             const total = storeMoney.reduce((sum, e) => sum + e.amount, 0);
12413:             
12414:             document.getElementById('storeMoneyPending').textContent = pending.toFixed(2) + ' جنيه';
12415:             document.getElementById('storeMoneyPaid').textContent = paid.toFixed(2) + ' جنيه';
12416:             document.getElementById('storeMoneyTotal').textContent = total.toFixed(2) + ' جنيه';
12417:         }
12418:         
12419:         function openStoreMoneyModal() {
12420:             // Set default due date to today
12421:             document.getElementById('storeMoneyDueDate').valueAsDate = new Date();
12422:             document.getElementById('storeMoneyModal').style.display = 'block';
12423:         }
12424:         
12425:         function closeStoreMoneyModal() {
12426:             document.getElementById('storeMoneyModal').style.display = 'none';
12427:             document.getElementById('storeMoneyForm').reset();
12428:         }
12429:         
12430:         // Quick deduction from capital
12431:         function quickDeductFromCapital() {
12432:             const amount = prompt('أدخل المبلغ المراد خصمه من رأس المال:');
12433:             if (!amount || isNaN(amount) || parseFloat(amount) <= 0) {
12434:                 showErrorMessage('يرجى إدخال مبلغ صحيح');
12435:                 return;
12436:             }
12437:             const deductAmount = parseFloat(amount);
12438:             capitalAmount = parseFloat(localStorage.getItem('hances_capital')) || 0;
12439:             capitalAmount -= deductAmount;
12440:             localStorage.setItem('hances_capital', capitalAmount.toString());
12441:             saveData();
12442:             updateDashboard();
12443:             showSuccessMessage('تم خصم ' + deductAmount + ' من رأس المال');
12444:         }
12445:         
12446:         // Quick addition to capital
12447:         function quickAddToCapital() {
12448:             const amount = prompt('أدخل المبلغ المراد إضافته لرأس المال:');
12449:             if (!amount || isNaN(amount) || parseFloat(amount) <= 0) {
12450:                 showErrorMessage('يرجى إدخال مبلغ صحيح');
12451:                 return;
12452:             }
12453:             const addAmount = parseFloat(amount);
12454:             capitalAmount = parseFloat(localStorage.getItem('hances_capital')) || 0;
12455:             capitalAmount += addAmount;
12456:             localStorage.setItem('hances_capital', capitalAmount.toString());
12457:             saveData();
12458:             updateDashboard();
12459:             showSuccessMessage('تم إضافة ' + addAmount + ' لرأس المال');
12460:         }
12461:         
12462:         function addStoreMoneyEntry(actionType) {
12463:             const debtor = document.getElementById('storeMoneyDebtor').value;
12464:             const amount = parseFloat(document.getElementById('storeMoneyAmount').value);
12465:             const dueDate = document.getElementById('storeMoneyDueDate').value;
12466:             const description = document.getElementById('storeMoneyDescription').value;
12467:             
12468:             if (!debtor || !amount || amount <= 0) {
12469:                 showErrorMessage('يرجى ملء جميع الحقول المطلوبة');
12470:                 return;
12471:             }
12472:             
12473:             const entry = {
12474:                 id: Date.now(),
12475:                 date: new Date().toISOString(),
12476:                 debtor: debtor,
12477:                 amount: amount,
12478:                 dueDate: dueDate,
12479:                 description: description,
12480:                 status: actionType === 'add' ? 'paid' : (actionType === 'save' ? 'pending' : 'pending')
12481:             };
12482:             
12483:             if (actionType === 'deduct') {
12484:                 // Deduct from capital (money paid out)
12485:                 capitalAmount -= amount;
12486:                 localStorage.setItem('hances_capital', capitalAmount.toString());
12487:                 showSuccessMessage('تم إضافة المستحقة وخصم ' + amount + ' من رأس المال بنجاح');
12488:             } else if (actionType === 'add') {
12489:                 // Add to capital (money received)
12490:                 capitalAmount += amount;
12491:                 localStorage.setItem('hances_capital', capitalAmount.toString());
12492:                 showSuccessMessage('تم تحصيل ' + amount + ' جنيه وإضافتها لرأس المال بنجاح');
12493:             } else if (actionType === 'save') {
12494:                 // Just save without modifying capital
12495:                 showSuccessMessage('تم حفظ المستحقة بنجاح');
12496:             }
12497:             
12498:             storeMoney.push(entry);
12499:             localStorage.setItem('hances_store_money', JSON.stringify(storeMoney));
12500:             
12501:             saveData();
12502:             closeStoreMoneyModal();
12503:             loadStoreMoneySection();
12504:             updateDashboard();
12505:         }
12506:         
12507:         function editStoreMoneyEntry(id) {
12508:             const entry = storeMoney.find(e => e.id === id);
12509:             if (!entry) return;
12510:             
12511:             // If entry was pending, restore the amount to capital first
12512:             if (entry.status === 'pending') {
12513:                 capitalAmount += entry.amount;
12514:             }
12515:             
12516:             document.getElementById('storeMoneyDebtor').value = entry.debtor;
12517:             document.getElementById('storeMoneyAmount').value = entry.amount;
12518:             document.getElementById('storeMoneyDueDate').value = entry.dueDate;
12519:             document.getElementById('storeMoneyDescription').value = entry.description || '';
12520:             
12521:             // Update modal for editing
12522:             const modal = document.getElementById('storeMoneyModal');
12523:             const form = document.getElementById('storeMoneyForm');
12524:             const header = modal.querySelector('.modal-header h3');
12525:             
12526:             header.innerHTML = '<i class="fas fa-edit"></i> تعديل المستحقة';
12527:             
12528:             // Remove old submit handler and add new one
12529:             form.onsubmit = function(e) {
12530:                 e.preventDefault();
12531:                 const newAmount = parseFloat(document.getElementById('storeMoneyAmount').value);
12532:                 
12533:                 entry.debtor = document.getElementById('storeMoneyDebtor').value;
12534:                 entry.amount = newAmount;
12535:                 entry.dueDate = document.getElementById('storeMoneyDueDate').value;
12536:                 entry.description = document.getElementById('storeMoneyDescription').value;
12537:                 
12538:                 // Deduct new amount from capital
12539:                 capitalAmount -= newAmount;
12540:                 localStorage.setItem('hances_capital', capitalAmount.toString());
12541:                 
12542:                 localStorage.setItem('hances_store_money', JSON.stringify(storeMoney));
12543:                 
12544:                 // Reset form handler
12545:                 form.onsubmit = function(e) { e.preventDefault(); addStoreMoneyEntry(); };
12546:                 
12547:                 // Reset header
12548:                 header.innerHTML = '<i class="fas fa-money-bill-wave"></i> إضافة مستحقة جديدة';
12549:                 
12550:                 saveData();
12551:                 closeStoreMoneyModal();
12552:                 loadStoreMoneySection();
12553:                 updateDashboard();
12554:                 showSuccessMessage('تم تعديل المستحقة بنجاح');
12555:             };
12556:             
12557:             modal.style.display = 'block';
12558:         }
12559:         
12560:         function markStoreMoneyAsPaid(id) {
12561:             if (!confirm('هل أنت متأكد من تسجيل السداد؟\nسيتم إضافة المبلغ لرأس المال تلقائياً!')) return;
12562:             
12563:             const entry = storeMoney.find(e => e.id === id);
12564:             if (entry) {
12565:                 // Remove from pending total (already deducted, so just change status)
12566:                 entry.status = 'paid';
12567:                 entry.paidDate = new Date().toISOString();
12568:                 
12569:                 // Add to capital (net effect: was deducted, now added back)
12570:                 capitalAmount += entry.amount;
12571:                 localStorage.setItem('hances_capital', capitalAmount.toString());
12572:                 
12573:                 localStorage.setItem('hances_store_money', JSON.stringify(storeMoney));
12574:                 
12575:                 saveData();
12576:                 loadStoreMoneySection();
12577:                 updateDashboard();
12578:                 showSuccessMessage('تم تسجيل السداد وإضافة المبلغ لرأس المال!');
12579:             }
12580:         }
12581:         
12582:         function deductFromCapitalDirect(id) {
12583:             if (!confirm('هل أنت متأكد من خصم هذا المبلغ من رأس المال؟')) return;
12584:             
12585:             const entry = storeMoney.find(e => e.id === id);
12586:             if (entry) {
12587:                 // Mark as paid but with deducted status
12588:                 entry.status = 'paid';
12589:                 entry.paidDate = new Date().toISOString();
12590:                 entry.wasDeducted = true;
12591:                 
12592:                 // Deduct from capital
12593:                 capitalAmount -= entry.amount;
12594:                 localStorage.setItem('hances_capital', capitalAmount.toString());
12595:                 
12596:                 localStorage.setItem('hances_store_money', JSON.stringify(storeMoney));
12597:                 
12598:                 saveData();
12599:                 loadStoreMoneySection();
12600:                 updateDashboard();
12601:                 showSuccessMessage('تم خصم المبلغ من رأس المال!');
12602:             }
12603:         }
12604:         
12605:         function deleteStoreMoneyEntry(id) {
12606:             if (!confirm('هل أنت متأكد من حذف هذه المستحقة؟')) return;
12607:             
12608:             const entry = storeMoney.find(e => e.id === id);
12609:             if (entry && entry.status === 'pending') {
12610:                 // Restore amount to capital
12611:                 capitalAmount += entry.amount;
12612:                 localStorage.setItem('hances_capital', capitalAmount.toString());
12613:             }
12614:             
12615:             storeMoney = storeMoney.filter(e => e.id !== id);
12616:             localStorage.setItem('hances_store_money', JSON.stringify(storeMoney));
12617:             
12618:             saveData();
12619:             loadStoreMoneySection();
12620:             updateDashboard();
12621:             showSuccessMessage('تم حذف المستحقة وإعادة المبلغ لرأس المال بنجاح');
12622:         }

12624:         function loadPosProducts() {
12625:             const grid = document.getElementById('posProductsGrid');
12626:             const searchTerm = document.getElementById('posProductSearch').value.toLowerCase();
12627:             
12628:             const filteredProducts = products.filter(p => 
12629:                 p.name.toLowerCase().includes(searchTerm) ||
12630:                 (p.code && p.code.toLowerCase().includes(searchTerm))
12631:             );
12632:             
12633:             if (filteredProducts.length === 0) {
12634:                 grid.innerHTML = `
12635:                     <div class="stats-card" style="text-align: center; padding: 40px; border-radius: 15px; cursor: default;">
12636:                         <i class="fas fa-box" style="font-size: 3rem; color: var(--primary); margin-bottom: 15px;"></i>
12637:                         <p style="color: #666;">لا توجد منتجات متاحة</p>
12638:                     </div>
12639:                 `;
12640:                 return;
12641:             }
12642:             
12643:             grid.innerHTML = filteredProducts.map(p => `
12644:                 <div class="stats-card" style="text-align: center; padding: 25px; border-radius: 15px; cursor: pointer; transition: all 0.3s ease;" onclick="addToPosCart(${p.id})">
12645:                     ${p.image ? `<img src="${p.image}" style="width: 80px; height: 80px; object-fit: cover; border-radius: 10px; margin-bottom: 15px;">` : `<i class="fas fa-box" style="font-size: 3rem; color: var(--primary); margin-bottom: 15px;"></i>`}
12646:                     <h4 style="color: var(--primary); margin-bottom: 10px;">${p.name}</h4>
12647:                     <p style="font-weight: bold; color: #28a745;">${p.price} جنيه</p>
12648:                     <small style="color: #666;">المخزون: ${p.quantity || 0}</small>
12649:                 </div>
12650:             `).join('');
12651:         }

12663:         function addToPosCart(productId) {
12664:             if (!productId) {
12665:                 // Add custom product
12666:                 const name = prompt('اسم المنتج:');
12667:                 if (!name) return;
12668:                 const price = parseFloat(prompt('سعر المنتج:')) || 0;
12669:                 posCart.push({
12670:                     id: 'custom_' + Date.now(),
12671:                     name: name,
12672:                     price: price,
12673:                     quantity: 1,
12674:                     isCustom: true
12675:                 });
12676:             } else {
12677:                 const product = products.find(p => p.id === productId);
12678:                 if (!product) return;
12679:                 
12680:                 const existingItem = posCart.find(item => item.id === productId);
12681:                 if (existingItem) {
12682:                     existingItem.quantity++;
12683:                 } else {
12684:                     posCart.push({
12685:                         id: productId,
12686:                         name: product.name,
12687:                         price: product.price,
12688:                         quantity: 1,
12689:                         isCustom: false
12690:                     });
12691:                 }
12692:             }
12693:             updatePosCart();
12694:         }
12695: 
12696:         function updatePosCart() {
12697:             const container = document.getElementById('posCartItems');
12698:             
12699:             if (posCart.length === 0) {
12700:                 container.innerHTML = `
12701:                     <div style="text-align: center; padding: 30px; color: #666;">
12702:                         <i class="fas fa-shopping-cart" style="font-size: 3rem; margin-bottom: 15px; opacity: 0.3;"></i>
12703:                         <p>السلة فارغة</p>
12704:                     </div>
12705:                 `;
12706:                 document.getElementById('posSubtotal').textContent = '0.00';
12707:                 document.getElementById('posTotal').textContent = '0.00';
12708:                 return;
12709:             }
12710:             
12711:             container.innerHTML = posCart.map((item, index) => `
12712:                 <div style="display: flex; justify-content: space-between; align-items: center; padding: 15px; background: #f8f9fa; border-radius: 10px; margin-bottom: 10px;">
12713:                     <div style="flex: 1;">
12714:                         <h4 style="margin-bottom: 5px;">${item.name}</h4>
12715:                         <small style="color: #666;">${item.price} × ${item.quantity}</small>
12716:                     </div>
12717:                     <div style="display: flex; align-items: center; gap: 10px;">
12718:                         <button onclick="updatePosQuantity(${index}, -1)" style="padding: 5px 10px; border: 1px solid #ddd; border-radius: 5px; cursor: pointer;">-</button>
12719:                         <span>${item.quantity}</span>
12720:                         <button onclick="updatePosQuantity(${index}, 1)" style="padding: 5px 10px; border: 1px solid #ddd; border-radius: 5px; cursor: pointer;">+</button>
12721:                         <button onclick="removeFromPosCart(${index})" style="padding: 5px 10px; background: #dc3545; color: white; border: none; border-radius: 5px; cursor: pointer;"><i class="fas fa-times"></i></button>
12722:                     </div>
12723:                     <div style="font-weight: bold; color: var(--primary); min-width: 80px; text-align: left;">
12724:                         ${(item.price * item.quantity).toFixed(2)}
12725:                     </div>
12726:                 </div>
12727:             `).join('');
12728:             
12729:             const subtotal = posCart.reduce((sum, item) => sum + (item.price * item.quantity), 0);
12730:             document.getElementById('posSubtotal').textContent = subtotal.toFixed(2);
12731:             document.getElementById('posTotal').textContent = subtotal.toFixed(2);
12732:         }
12733: 
12734:         function updatePosQuantity(index, change) {
12735:             posCart[index].quantity += change;
12736:             if (posCart[index].quantity <= 0) {
12737:                 posCart.splice(index, 1);
12738:             }
12739:             updatePosCart();
12740:         }
12741: 
12742:         function removeFromPosCart(index) {
12743:             posCart.splice(index, 1);
12744:             updatePosCart();
12745:         }

12774:         function completePosSale() {
12775:             if (posCart.length === 0) {
12776:                 alert('السلة فارغة!');
12777:                 return;
12778:             }
12779:             
12780:             const customerId = document.getElementById('posCustomer').value;
12781:             const paymentMethod = document.getElementById('posPaymentMethod').value;
12782:             const paidAmount = parseFloat(document.getElementById('posPaidAmount').value) || 0;
12783:             
12784:             const total = posCart.reduce((sum, item) => sum + (item.price * item.quantity), 0);
12785:             
12786:             if (paidAmount < total) {
12787:                 alert('المبلغ المدفوع أقل من الإجمالي!');
12788:                 return;
12789:             }
12790:             
12791:             const customer = customerId ? customers.find(c => c.id === parseInt(customerId)) : null;
12792:             
12793:             // Create invoice from POS
12794:             const invoice = {
12795:                 id: Date.now(),
12796:                 invoiceNumber: 'POS-' + Date.now(),
12797:                 customerName: customer ? customer.name : 'عميل نقدي',
12798:                 customerPhone: customer ? customer.phone : '',
12799:                 customerAddress: customer ? customer.address : '',
12800:                 items: posCart.map(item => ({
12801:                     productId: item.id,
12802:                     name: item.name,
12803:                     price: item.price,
12804:                     quantity: item.quantity
12805:                 })),
12806:                 subtotal: total,
12807:                 discount: 0,
12808:                 total: total,
12809:                 paidAmount: paidAmount,
12810:                 change: paidAmount - total,
12811:                 paymentMethod: paymentMethod,
12812:                 platform: 'POS',
12813:                 date: new Date().toLocaleDateString('ar-EG'),
12814:                 deliveryStatus: 'delivered'
12815:             };
12816:             
12817:             invoices.push(invoice);
12818:             
12819:             // Update product quantities
12820:             posCart.forEach(item => {
12821:                 if (!item.isCustom) {
12822:                     const product = products.find(p => p.id === item.id);
12823:                     if (product) {
12824:                         product.quantity = (product.quantity || 0) - item.quantity;
12825:                     }
12826:                 }
12827:             });
12828:             
12829:             // Save data
12830:             saveData();
12831:             
12832:             // Clear cart
12833:             posCart = [];
12834:             updatePosCart();
12835:             
12836:             alert('تم إتمام البيع بنجاح! رقم الفاتورة: ' + invoice.invoiceNumber);
12837:         }

12847:         // Auto Backup Functions
12848:         function saveAutoBackupSettings() {
12849:             const enableAutoBackup = document.getElementById('enableAutoBackup').checked;
12850:             const autoBackupPeriod = document.getElementById('autoBackupPeriod').value;
12851:             
12852:             localStorage.setItem('hances_auto_backup_enabled', enableAutoBackup);
12853:             localStorage.setItem('hances_auto_backup_period', autoBackupPeriod);
12854:             
12855:             if (enableAutoBackup) {
12856:                 scheduleAutoBackup(autoBackupPeriod);
12857:                 showSuccessMessage('تم تفعيل النسخ الاحتياطي التلقائي!');
12858:             } else {
12859:                 showSuccessMessage('تم تعطيل النسخ الاحتياطي التلقائي');
12860:             }
12861:         }
12862:         
12863:         function toggleAutoBackup() {
12864:             const enabled = document.getElementById('enableAutoBackup').checked;
12865:             document.getElementById('autoBackupPeriod').disabled = !enabled;
12866:         }
12867:         
12868:         function scheduleAutoBackup(period) {
12869:             if (window.autoBackupInterval) clearInterval(window.autoBackupInterval);
12870:             
12871:             let intervalMs;
12872:             switch(period) {
12873:                 case 'daily': intervalMs = 24 * 60 * 60 * 1000; break;
12874:                 case 'weekly': intervalMs = 7 * 24 * 60 * 60 * 1000; break;
12875:                 case 'monthly': intervalMs = 30 * 24 * 60 * 60 * 1000; break;
12876:                 default: intervalMs = 24 * 60 * 60 * 1000;
12877:             }
12878:             
12879:             window.autoBackupInterval = setInterval(() => { createAutoBackup(); }, intervalMs);
12880:         }
12881:         
12882:         function createAutoBackup() {
12883:             const backupData = {
12884:                 version: '1.0',
12885:                 date: new Date().toISOString(),
12886:                 type: 'auto',
12887:                 products: products,
12888:                 invoices: invoices,
12889:                 purchases: purchases,
12890:                 expenses: expenses,
12891:                 returns: returns,
12892:                 collections: collections,
12893:                 suppliers: suppliers,
12894:                 expenseTypes: expenseTypes,
12895:                 shippingCompanies: shippingCompanies,
12896:                 customerDebts: customerDebts,
12897:                 supplierDebts: supplierDebts,
12898:                 bankAccounts: bankAccounts,
12899:                 bankTransactions: bankTransactions,
12900:                 customers: customers,
12901:                 capitalAmount: capitalAmount
12902:             };
12903:             
12904:             localStorage.setItem('hances_auto_backup', JSON.stringify(backupData));
12905:             localStorage.setItem('hances_last_auto_backup', new Date().toISOString());
12906:             updateLastAutoBackupDisplay();
12907:             console.log('Auto backup created');
12908:         }
12909:         
12910:         function loadAutoBackupSettings() {
12911:             const enabled = localStorage.getItem('hances_auto_backup_enabled') === 'true';
12912:             const period = localStorage.getItem('hances_auto_backup_period') || 'daily';
12913:             
12914:             if (document.getElementById('enableAutoBackup')) {
12915:                 document.getElementById('enableAutoBackup').checked = enabled;
12916:                 document.getElementById('autoBackupPeriod').value = period;
12917:                 document.getElementById('autoBackupPeriod').disabled = !enabled;
12918:                 
12919:                 if (enabled) scheduleAutoBackup(period);
12920:                 updateLastAutoBackupDisplay();
12921:             }
12922:         }
12923:         
12924:         function updateLastAutoBackupDisplay() {
12925:             const display = document.getElementById('lastAutoBackup');
12926:             if (!display) return;
12927:             
12928:             const lastBackup = localStorage.getItem('hances_last_auto_backup');
12929:             if (lastBackup) {
12930:                 const date = new Date(lastBackup);
12931:                 display.innerHTML = date.toLocaleDateString('ar-EG') + ' - ' + date.toLocaleTimeString('ar-EG');
12932:             } else {
12933:                 display.innerHTML = 'لم يتم إجراء نسخة احتياطية بعد';
12934:             }
12935:         }
12936:         
12937:         function loadBackupHistory() {
12938:             const tbody = document.getElementById('backupHistoryTableBody');
12939:             if (backupHistory.length === 0) {
12940:                 tbody.innerHTML = '<tr><td colspan="5" style="text-align: center; padding: 50px; color: #666;"><i class="fas fa-history" style="font-size: 3rem; margin-bottom: 15px; opacity: 0.3;"></i><br>لا توجد نسخ احتياطية سابقة</td></tr>';
12941:                 return;
12942:             }
12943:             
12944:             tbody.innerHTML = backupHistory.map(backup => `
12945:                 <tr>
12946:                     <td>${backup.date}</td>
12947:                     <td>${backup.time}</td>
12948:                     <td>${backup.type}</td>
12949:                     <td>${backup.size}</td>
12950:                     <td>
12951:                         <button onclick="downloadBackup(${backup.id})" class="btn btn-primary" style="padding: 8px 15px; font-size: 0.9rem;"><i class="fas fa-download"></i> تحميل</button>
12952:                         <button onclick="restoreBackupById(${backup.id})" class="btn btn-warning" style="padding: 8px 15px; font-size: 0.9rem;"><i class="fas fa-upload"></i> استعادة</button>
12953:                     </td>
12954:                 </tr>
12955:             `).join('');
12956:         }
12957: 
12958:         function createFullBackup() {
12959:             const backupData = {
12960:                 version: '1.0',
12961:                 date: new Date().toISOString(),
12962:                 products: products,
12963:                 invoices: invoices,
12964:                 purchases: purchases,
12965:                 expenses: expenses,
12966:                 returns: returns,
12967:                 collections: collections,
12968:                 suppliers: suppliers,
12969:                 expenseTypes: expenseTypes,
12970:                 shippingCompanies: shippingCompanies,
12971:                 customerDebts: customerDebts,
12972:                 supplierDebts: supplierDebts,
12973:                 bankAccounts: bankAccounts,
12974:                 bankTransactions: bankTransactions,
12975:                 customers: customers,
12976:                 capitalAmount: capitalAmount
12977:             };
12978:             
12979:             const jsonString = JSON.stringify(backupData, null, 2);
12980:             const blob = new Blob([jsonString], { type: 'application/json' });
12981:             const url = URL.createObjectURL(blob);
12982:             const link = document.createElement('a');
12983:             link.href = url;
12984:             link.download = `hances_backup_${new Date().toISOString().split('T')[0]}.json`;
12985:             link.click();
12986:             URL.revokeObjectURL(url);
12987:             
12988:             // Add to history
12989:             addBackupToHistory('نسخة كاملة', jsonString.length);
12990:             alert('تم إنشاء النسخة الاحتياطية بنجاح!');
12991:         }
12992:

13058:         function addBackupToHistory(type, size) {
13059:             backupHistory.unshift({
13060:                 id: Date.now(),
13061:                 date: new Date().toLocaleDateString('ar-EG'),
13062:                 time: new Date().toLocaleTimeString('ar-EG'),
13063:                 type: type,
13064:                 size: formatFileSize(size)
13065:             });
13066:             // Keep only last 20 backups
13067:             if (backupHistory.length > 20) backupHistory.pop();
13068:             saveData();
13069:             loadBackupHistory();
13070:         }
13071: 
13072:         function formatFileSize(bytes) {
13073:             if (bytes < 1024) return bytes + ' B';
13074:             if (bytes < 1024 * 1024) return (bytes / 1024).toFixed(2) + ' KB';
13075:             return (bytes / (1024 * 1024)).toFixed(2) + ' MB';
13076:         }
13077: 
13078:         function restoreBackup(event) {
13079:             const file = event.target.files[0];
13080:             if (!file) return;
13081:             
13082:             if (!confirm('تحذير: استعادة النسخة الاحتياطية ستستبدل جميع البيانات الحالية. هل تريد المتابعة؟')) {
13083:                 event.target.value = '';
13084:                 return;
13085:             }
13086:             
13087:             const reader = new FileReader();
13088:             reader.onload = function(e) {
13089:                 try {
13090:                     const data = JSON.parse(e.target.result);
13091:                     
13092:                     if (data.products) products = data.products;
13093:                     if (data.invoices) invoices = data.invoices;
13094:                     if (data.purchases) purchases = data.purchases;
13095:                     if (data.expenses) expenses = data.expenses;
13096:                     if (data.returns) returns = data.returns;
13097:                     if (data.collections) collections = data.collections;
13098:                     if (data.suppliers) suppliers = data.suppliers;
13099:                     if (data.expenseTypes) expenseTypes = data.expenseTypes;
13100:                     if (data.shippingCompanies) shippingCompanies = data.shippingCompanies;
13101:                     if (data.customerDebts) customerDebts = data.customerDebts;
13102:                     if (data.supplierDebts) supplierDebts = data.supplierDebts;
13103:                     if (data.bankAccounts) bankAccounts = data.bankAccounts;
13104:                     if (data.bankTransactions) bankTransactions = data.bankTransactions;
13105:                     if (data.customers) customers = data.customers;
13106:                     if (data.capitalAmount !== undefined) capitalAmount = data.capitalAmount;
13107:                     
13108:                     saveData();
13109:                     alert('تم استعادة البيانات بنجاح!');
13110:                     location.reload();
13111:                 } catch (err) {
13112:                     alert('خطأ في قراءة الملف: ' + err.message);
13113:                 }
13114:             };
13115:             reader.readAsText(file);
13116:             event.target.value = '';
13117:         }
13118: 
13119:         function downloadBackup(id) {
13120:             const backup = backupHistory.find(b => b.id === id);
13121:             if (!backup) return;
13122:             
13123:             // Recreate the backup data
13124:             let backupData = {
13125:                 version: '1.0',
13126:                 date: new Date().toISOString(),
13127:                 type: backup.type
13128:             };
13129:             
13130:             if (backup.type === 'نسخة كاملة') {
13131:                 backupData.products = products;
13132:                 backupData.invoices = invoices;
13133:                 backupData.purchases = purchases;
13134:                 backupData.expenses = expenses;
13135:                 backupData.returns = returns;
13136:                 backupData.collections = collections;
13137:                 backupData.suppliers = suppliers;
13138:                 backupData.expenseTypes = expenseTypes;
13139:                 backupData.shippingCompanies = shippingCompanies;
13140:                 backupData.customerDebts = customerDebts;
13141:                 backupData.supplierDebts = supplierDebts;
13142:                 backupData.bankAccounts = bankAccounts;
13143:                 backupData.bankTransactions = bankTransactions;
13144:                 backupData.customers = customers;
13145:                 backupData.capitalAmount = capitalAmount;
13146:             } else if (backup.type === 'المنتجات فقط') {
13147:                 backupData.products = products;
13148:                 backupData.collections = collections;
13149:             } else if (backup.type === 'الفواتير فقط') {
13150:                 backupData.invoices = invoices;
13151:             } else if (backup.type === 'المصروفات فقط') {
13152:                 backupData.expenses = expenses;
13153:                 backupData.expenseTypes = expenseTypes;
13154:             }
13155:             
13156:             const jsonString = JSON.stringify(backupData, null, 2);
13157:             const blob = new Blob([jsonString], { type: 'application/json' });
13158:             const url = URL.createObjectURL(blob);
13159:             const link = document.createElement('a');
13160:             link.href = url;
13161:             link.download = `hances_backup_${backup.date.replace(/\//g, '-')}.json`;
13162:             link.click();
13163:             URL.revokeObjectURL(url);
13164:         }
13165: 
13166:         function restoreBackupById(id) {
13167:             if (!confirm('تحذير: استعادة النسخة الاحتياطية ستستبدل جميع البيانات الحالية. هل تريد المتابعة؟')) return;
13168:             downloadBackup(id);
13169:             
13170:             // Give user time to download, then reload
13171:             setTimeout(() => {
13172:                 location.reload();
13173:             }, 1000);
13174:         }

13190:         function updateInventoryStats() {
13191:             document.getElementById('totalInventoryItems').textContent = products.length;
13192:             document.getElementById('totalCollections').textContent = collections.length;
13193:             
13194:             // Calculate low stock based on each product's minStock setting
13195:             let lowStock = 0;
13196:             let outOfStock = 0;
13197:             products.forEach(p => {
13198:                 const min = p.minStock || 5;
13199:                 const qty = p.totalQuantity || p.quantity || 0;
13200:                 
13201:                 if (qty === 0) {
13202:                     outOfStock++;
13203:                 } else if (qty <= min) {
13204:                     lowStock++;
13205:                 }
13206:             });
13207:             
13208:             document.getElementById('lowStockItems').textContent = lowStock;
13209:             document.getElementById('outOfStockItems').textContent = outOfStock;
13210:         }
13211: 
13212:         function loadInventoryByCollection() {
13213:             const container = document.getElementById('inventoryByCollection');
13214:             
13215:             const collectionStats = collections.map(col => {
13216:                 const colProducts = products.filter(p => p.collectionId == col.id);
13217:                 const totalQuantity = colProducts.reduce((sum, p) => sum + (p.quantity || 0), 0);
13218:                 return { ...col, productCount: colProducts.length, totalQuantity };
13219:             });
13220:             
13221:             container.innerHTML = collectionStats.map(col => `
13222:                 <div class="stats-card" style="text-align: right; padding: 25px;">
13223:                     <h4 style="color: var(--primary); margin-bottom: 15px;"><i class="${col.icon || 'fas fa-box'}"></i> ${col.name}</h4>
13224:                     <p style="margin-bottom: 10px;"><i class="fas fa-box"></i> المنتجات: <strong>${col.productCount}</strong></p>
13225:                     <p><i class="fas fa-layer-group"></i> إجمالي الكمية: <strong>${col.totalQuantity}</strong></p>
13226:                 </div>
13227:             `).join('');
13228:         }
13229: 
13230:         function loadLowStockTable() {
13231:             const tbody = document.getElementById('lowStockTableBody');
13232:             // Filter products that are below their minimum stock level
13233:             const lowStockProducts = products.filter(p => {
13234:                 const min = p.minStock || 5;
13235:                 const qty = p.totalQuantity || p.quantity || 0;
13236:                 return qty > 0 && qty <= min;
13237:             });
13238:             
13239:             if (lowStockProducts.length === 0) {
13240:                 tbody.innerHTML = '<tr><td colspan="5" style="text-align: center; padding: 50px; color: #28a745;"><i class="fas fa-check-circle" style="font-size: 3rem; margin-bottom: 15px;"></i><br>لا توجد منتجات منخفضة المخزون</td></tr>';
13241:                 return;
13242:             }
13243:             
13244:             tbody.innerHTML = lowStockProducts.map(p => {
13245:                 const collection = collections.find(c => c.id == p.collectionId);
13246:                 const min = p.minStock || 5;
13247:                 return `
13248:                     <tr>
13249:                         <td>${p.name}</td>
13250:                         <td>${collection ? collection.name : '-'}</td>
13251:                         <td style="color: #fd7e14; font-weight: bold;">${p.totalQuantity}</td>
13252:                         <td>${min}</td>
13253:                         <td>
13254:                             <button onclick="addToAdjustmentProduct(${p.id})" class="btn btn-add" style="padding: 8px 15px; font-size: 0.9rem;">إضافة مخزون</button>
13255:                         </td>
13256:                     </tr>
13257:                 `;
13258:             }).join('');
13259:         }
13260: 
13261:         function loadInventoryMovementsTable() {
13262:             const tbody = document.getElementById('inventoryMovementsTableBody');
13263:             
13264:             if (inventoryMovements.length === 0) {
13265:                 tbody.innerHTML = '<tr><td colspan="5" style="text-align: center; padding: 50px; color: #666;"><i class="fas fa-history" style="font-size: 3rem; margin-bottom: 15px; opacity: 0.3;"></i><br>لا توجد حركات مخزون</td></tr>';
13266:                 return;
13267:             }
13268:             
13269:             tbody.innerHTML = inventoryMovements.slice(-20).reverse().map(m => `
13270:                 <tr>
13271:                     <td>${m.date}</td>
13272:                     <td>${m.productName}</td>
13273:                     <td style="color: ${m.type === 'add' ? '#28a745' : m.type === 'remove' ? '#dc3545' : '#17a2b8'}">${m.type === 'add' ? 'إضافة' : m.type === 'remove' ? 'خصم' : 'تحديد'}</td>
13274:                     <td style="color: ${m.type === 'add' ? '#28a745' : '#dc3545'}">${m.type === 'add' ? '+' : m.type === 'remove' ? '-' : ''}${m.quantity}</td>
13275:                     <td>${m.reason || '-'}</td>
13276:                 </tr>
13277:             `).join('');
13278:         }

13290:         function openInventoryAdjustmentModal() {

13292:             document.getElementById('inventoryAdjustmentModal').style.display = 'block';
13293:         }
13294: 
13295:         document.getElementById('inventoryAdjustmentForm').addEventListener('submit', function(e) {
13296:             e.preventDefault();
13297:             
13298:             const productId = parseInt(document.getElementById('adjustmentProduct').value);
13299:             const type = document.getElementById('adjustmentType').value;
13300:             const quantity = parseInt(document.getElementById('adjustmentQuantity').value);
13301:             const reason = document.getElementById('adjustmentReason').value;
13302:             
13303:             const product = products.find(p => p.id === productId);
13304:             if (!product) return;
13305:             
13306:             const oldQuantity = product.quantity || 0;
13307:             let newQuantity;
13308:             
13309:             if (type === 'add') {
13310:                 newQuantity = oldQuantity + quantity;
13311:             } else if (type === 'remove') {
13312:                 newQuantity = Math.max(0, oldQuantity - quantity);
13313:             } else {
13314:                 newQuantity = quantity;
13315:             }
13316:             
13317:             product.quantity = newQuantity;
13318:             
13319:             // Add movement record
13320:             inventoryMovements.push({
13321:                 id: Date.now(),
13322:                 productId: productId,
13323:                 productName: product.name,
13324:                 type: type,
13325:                 quantity: quantity,
13326:                 oldQuantity: oldQuantity,
13327:                 newQuantity: newQuantity,
13328:                 reason: reason,
13329:                 date: new Date().toLocaleDateString('ar-EG'),
13330:                 time: new Date().toLocaleTimeString('ar-EG')
13331:             });
13332:             
13333:             saveData();
13334:             closeModal('inventoryAdjustmentModal');
13335:             loadInventorySection();
13336:             loadProducts();
13337:         });

13410:             saveData();

13414:         document.getElementById('customerForm').addEventListener('submit', function(e) {
13415:             e.preventDefault();
13416:             
13417:             const customer = {
13418:                 id: document.getElementById('customerId').value ? parseInt(document.getElementById('customerId').value) : Date.now(),
13419:                 name: document.getElementById('customerName').value,
13420:                 phone: document.getElementById('customerPhone').value,
13421:                 email: document.getElementById('customerEmail').value,
13422:                 address: document.getElementById('customerAddress').value,
13423:                 points: parseInt(document.getElementById('customerPoints').value) || 0,
13424:                 notes: document.getElementById('customerNotes').value,
13425:                 createdAt: new Date().toISOString()
13426:             };
13427:             
13428:             const existingIndex = customers.findIndex(c => c.id === customer.id);
13429:             if (existingIndex !== -1) {
13430:                 // Preserve existing points when updating
13431:                 customer.points = customers[existingIndex].points || 0;
13432:                 customers[existingIndex] = customer;
13433:             } else {
13434:                 customers.push(customer);
13435:             }
13436:             
13437:             saveData();
13438:             closeModal('customerModal');
13439:             loadCustomersSection();
13440:             loadPosCustomerOptions();
13441:             this.reset();
13442:             document.getElementById('customerId').value = '';
13443:         });
