13512:         function deleteSupplier(name) {
13513:             if (!confirm('هل أنت متأكد من حذف هذا المورد؟')) return;
13514:             suppliers = suppliers.filter(s => s !== name);
13515:             supplierDetails = supplierDetails.filter(d => d.name !== name);
13516:             saveData();
13517:             loadSuppliersSection();
13518:             loadSupplierOptions();
13519:             loadPurchaseSupplierOptions();
13520:             loadProductSupplierOptions();
13521:         }
13522: 
13523:         document.getElementById('supplierForm').addEventListener('submit', function(e) {
13524:             e.preventDefault();
13525:             
13526:             const name = document.getElementById('supplierName').value;
13527:             const existingSuppliers = [...suppliers];
13528:             const oldName = document.getElementById('supplierId').value;
13529:             
13530:             const detail = {
13531:                 name: name,
13532:                 phone: document.getElementById('supplierPhone').value,
13533:                 email: document.getElementById('supplierEmail').value,
13534:                 address: document.getElementById('supplierAddress').value,
13535:                 notes: document.getElementById('supplierNotes').value
13536:             };
13537:             
13538:             if (oldName && oldName !== name) {
13539:                 // Update supplier name in suppliers array
13540:                 const supIndex = suppliers.indexOf(oldName);
13541:                 if (supIndex !== -1) suppliers[supIndex] = name;
13542:                 
13543:                 // Update purchases
13544:                 purchases.forEach(p => {
13545:                     if (p.supplier === oldName) p.supplier = name;
13546:                 });
13547:                 
13548:                 // Update supplier details
13549:                 const detailIndex = supplierDetails.findIndex(d => d.name === oldName);
13550:                 if (detailIndex !== -1) supplierDetails[detailIndex] = detail;
13551:                 else supplierDetails.push(detail);
13552:             } else {
13553:                 if (!suppliers.includes(name)) {
13554:                     suppliers.push(name);
13555:                 }
13556:                 const existingDetail = supplierDetails.find(d => d.name === name);
13557:                 if (existingDetail) {
13558:                     Object.assign(existingDetail, detail);
13559:                 } else {
13560:                     supplierDetails.push(detail);
13561:                 }
13562:             }
13563:             
13564:             saveData();
13565:             closeModal('supplierModal');
13566:             loadSuppliersSection();
13567:             loadSupplierOptions();
13568:             loadPurchaseSupplierOptions();
13569:             loadProductSupplierOptions();
13570:             this.reset();
13571:             document.getElementById('supplierId').value = '';
13572:         });

13580:         function loadUsersTable() {
13581:             const tbody = document.getElementById('usersTableBody');
13582:             
13583:             tbody.innerHTML = users.map(u => `
13584:                 <tr>
13585:                     <td>${u.username}</td>
13586:                     <td>${u.email}</td>
13587:                     <td><span style="padding: 5px 10px; border-radius: 5px; background: ${u.role === 'admin' ? '#dc3545' : u.role === 'manager' ? '#fd7e14' : '#28a745'}; color: white;">${u.role === 'admin' ? 'مدير عام' : u.role === 'manager' ? 'مدير' : 'مستخدم عادي'}</span></td>
13588:                     <td>${new Date(u.createdAt).toLocaleDateString('ar-EG')}</td>
13589:                     <td><span style="color: ${u.status === 'active' ? '#28a745' : '#dc3545'};">${u.status === 'active' ? 'نشط' : 'غير نشط'}</span></td>
13590:                     <td>
13591:                         ${u.role !== 'admin' ? `
13592:                             <button onclick="editUser(${u.id})" class="btn btn-edit" style="padding: 8px 15px; font-size: 0.9rem;">تعديل</button>
13593:                             <button onclick="toggleUserStatus(${u.id})" class="btn ${u.status === 'active' ? 'btn-warning' : 'btn-add'}" style="padding: 8px 15px; font-size: 0.9rem;">${u.status === 'active' ? 'تعطيل' : 'تفعيل'}</button>
13594:                             <button onclick="deleteUser(${u.id})" class="btn btn-delete" style="padding: 8px 15px; font-size: 0.9rem;">حذف</button>
13595:                         ` : '-'}
13596:                     </td>
13597:                 </tr>
13598:             `).join('');
13599:         }

13601:         function openUserModal(userId = null) {
13602:             document.getElementById('userForm').reset();
13603:             if (userId) {
13604:                 const user = users.find(u => u.id === userId);
13605:                 if (user) {
13606:                     document.getElementById('userId').value = user.id;
13607:                     document.getElementById('userName').value = user.username;
13608:                     document.getElementById('userEmail').value = user.email;
13609:                     document.getElementById('userPassword').value = '';
13610:                     document.getElementById('userRole').value = user.role;
13611:                     document.getElementById('userStatus').value = user.status;
13612:                 }
13613:             }
13614:             document.getElementById('userModal').style.display = 'block';
13615:         }

13618:             openUserModal(id);
13619:         }

13621:         function toggleUserStatus(id) {
13622:             const user = users.find(u => u.id === id);
13623:             if (user) {
13624:                 user.status = user.status === 'active' ? 'inactive' : 'active';
13625:                 saveData();
13626:                 loadUsersTable();
13627:             }
13628:         }
13629: 
13630:         function deleteUser(id) {
13631:             if (!confirm('هل أنت متأكد من حذف هذا المستخدم؟')) return;
13632:             users = users.filter(u => u.id !== id);
13633:             saveData();
13634:             loadUsersTable();
13635:         }
13636: 
13637:         document.getElementById('userForm').addEventListener('submit', function(e) {
13638:             e.preventDefault();
13639:             
13640:             const id = document.getElementById('userId').value ? parseInt(document.getElementById('userId').value) : Date.now();
13641:             const user = {
13642:                 id: id,
13643:                 username: document.getElementById('userName').value,
13644:                 email: document.getElementById('userEmail').value,
13645:                 role: document.getElementById('userRole').value,
13646:                 status: document.getElementById('userStatus').value,
13647:                 createdAt: new Date().toISOString()
13648:             };
13649:             
13650:             const password = document.getElementById('userPassword').value;
13651:             if (password) {
13652:                 user.password = password;
13653:             }
13654:             
13655:             const existingIndex = users.findIndex(u => u.id === id);
13656:             if (existingIndex !== -1) {
13657:                 user.createdAt = users[existingIndex].createdAt;
13658:                 users[existingIndex] = user;
13659:             } else {
13660:                 if (!password) {
13661:                     alert('كلمة المرور مطلوبة للمستخدم الجديد');
13662:                     return;
13663:                 }
13664:                 users.push(user);
13665:             }
13666:             
13667:             saveData();
13668:             closeModal('userModal');
13669:             loadUsersSection();
13670:             this.reset();
13671:             document.getElementById('userId').value = '';
13672:         });

13701:             const invoice = invoices.find(inv => inv.id === invoiceId);
13702:             if (!invoice) return;
13703:             
13704:             currentPrintInvoice = invoice;
13705:

13707:             const storePhone = document.getElementById('storePhone').value || '+201550801034';
13708:             const storeEmail = document.getElementById('storeEmail').value || 'info@hances.store';
13709:

13712:             const borderColor = localStorage.getItem('hances_invoice_border') || '#6b8273';
13713:

13715:                 <tr>
13716:                     <td style="border: 1px solid #ddd; padding: 10px; text-align: center;">${index + 1}</td>

13720:                     <td style="border: 1px solid #ddd; padding: 10px; text-align: left;">${(item.netTotal || (item.price * item.quantity)).toFixed(2)}</td>
13721:                 </tr>
13722:             `).join('');
13723:             
13724:             const content = `
13725:                 <div style="text-align: center; margin-bottom: 30px;">

13727:                     <p style="color: #666; margin: 5px 0;">${storePhone} | ${storeEmail}</p>

13731:                     <h2 style="color: #333; margin-bottom: 15px;">فاتورة رقم: ${invoice.invoiceNumber}</h2>
13732:                     <div style="display: grid; grid-template-columns: 1fr 1fr; gap: 20px;">
13733:                         <div>
13734:                             <p><strong>العميل:</strong> ${invoice.customerName}</p>
13735:                             <p><strong>الهاتف:</strong> ${invoice.customerPhone || '-'}</p>
13736:                             <p><strong>العنوان:</strong> ${invoice.customerAddress || '-'}</p>
13737:                         </div>
13738:                         <div style="text-align: left;">
13739:                             <p><strong>التاريخ:</strong> ${invoice.date}</p>
13740:                             <p><strong>المنصة:</strong> ${invoice.platform || '-'}</p>
13741:                             <p><strong>حالة التوصيل:</strong> ${invoice.deliveryStatus === 'delivered' ? 'تم التوصيل' : invoice.deliveryStatus || '-'}</p>
13742:                         </div>
13743:                     </div>
13744:                 </div>
13745:                 
13746:                 <table style="width: 100%; border-collapse: collapse; margin-bottom: 20px;">
13747:                     <thead>
13748:                         <tr style="background: #6b8273; color: white;">
13749:                             <th style="border: 1px solid #ddd; padding: 10px;">#</th>
13750:                             <th style="border: 1px solid #ddd; padding: 10px;">المنتج</th>
13751:                             <th style="border: 1px solid #ddd; padding: 10px;">الكمية</th>
13752:                             <th style="border: 1px solid #ddd; padding: 10px;">سعر الوحدة</th>
13753:                             <th style="border: 1px solid #ddd; padding: 10px;">الإجمالي</th>
13754:                         </tr>
13755:                     </thead>

13757:                         ${itemsHtml}
13758:                     </tbody>

13763:                         <div></div>
13764:                         <div style="text-align: left;">
13765:                             <p style="margin: 10px 0;"><strong>المجموع:</strong> ${(invoice.subtotal || 0).toFixed(2)} جنيه</p>
13766:                             ${invoice.discount > 0 ? `<p style="margin: 10px 0; color: #dc3545;"><strong>الخصم:</strong> -${(invoice.discount || 0).toFixed(2)} جنيه</p>` : ''}
13767:                             <p style="margin: 10px 0; font-size: 1.2rem; font-weight: bold;"><strong>الإجمالي:</strong> ${(invoice.total || 0).toFixed(2)} جنيه</p>
13768:                             ${invoice.paidAmount ? `<p style="margin: 10px 0;"><strong>المدفوع:</strong> ${(invoice.paidAmount).toFixed(2)} جنيه</p>` : ''}

13795:                                 .no-print { display: none; }

13800:                 </html>

13806:         function downloadInvoicePDF() {
13807:             if (!currentPrintInvoice) return;
13808:             
13809:             const element = document.getElementById('printInvoiceContent');
13810:             const opt = {
13811:                 margin: 10,
13812:                 filename: `فاتورة_${currentPrintInvoice.invoiceNumber}.pdf`,
13813:                 image: { type: 'jpeg', quality: 0.98 },
13814:                 html2canvas: { scale: 2, useCORS: true },
13815:                 jsPDF: { orientation: 'portrait', unit: 'mm', format: 'a4' }
13816:             };
13817:             
13818:             html2pdf().set(opt).from(element).save();

13821:         function sendInvoiceToWhatsApp(invoiceId) {
13822:             const invoice = invoices.find(inv => inv.id === invoiceId);
13823:             if (!invoice) {
13824:                 showErrorMessage('الفاتورة غير موجودة');
13825:                 return;
13826:             }
13827:             
13828:             const product = products.find(p => p.id == invoice.productId);
13829:             const productName = product ? product.name : 'منتج';
13830:             
13831:             // Get customer phone (remove any non-numeric characters)
13832:             let phone = invoice.customerPhone || '';
13833:             phone = phone.replace(/[^0-9]/g, '');
13834:             
13835:             // Add country code if not present
13836:             if (phone.length === 11) {
13837:                 phone = '2' + phone; // Add Egypt country code
13838:             }
13839:             
13840:             // Build message
13841:             const message = `*فاتورة شراء - HANCES*\n\n` +
13842:                 `━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━\n` +
13843:                 `*رقم الفاتورة:* #${invoice.id}\n` +
13844:                 `*العميل:* ${invoice.customerName}\n` +
13845:                 `*المنتج:* ${productName}\n` +
13846:                 `*الكمية:* ${invoice.quantity}\n` +
13847:                 `*السعر:* ${invoice.total} جنيه\n` +
13848:                 `*المنصة:* ${invoice.platform || 'Direct'}\n` +
13849:                 `━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━\n` +
13850:                 `شكراً لتعاملكم مع HANCES`;
13851:             
13852:             // Encode message for URL
13853:             const encodedMessage = encodeURIComponent(message);
13854:             
13855:             // Open WhatsApp
13856:             const whatsappUrl = `https://wa.me/20${phone.slice(1)}?text=${encodedMessage}`;
13857:             window.open(whatsappUrl, '_blank');
13858:         }
13859:         
13860:         function printInvoiceById(invoiceId) {
13861:             openPrintInvoice(invoiceId);
13862:             setTimeout(() => {
13863:                 printInvoice();
13864:             }, 500);
13865:         }

13879:                     <p>إجمالي المبيعات: ${invoices.reduce((sum, inv) => sum + (inv.total || 0), 0).toFixed(2)} جنيه</p>
13880:                 </div>
13881:                 <table style="width: 100%; border-collapse: collapse; margin-top: 20px;">
13882:                     <thead>
13883:                         <tr style="background: #6b8273; color: white;">

13888:                             <th style="border: 1px solid #ddd; padding: 10px;">الإجمالي</th>
13889:                         </tr>
13890:                     </thead>
13891:                     <tbody>

13893:

13895:                 content += `
13896:                     <tr>
13897:                         <td style="border: 1px solid #ddd; padding: 10px;">${inv.invoiceNumber}</td>
13898:                         <td style="border: 1px solid #ddd; padding: 10px;">${inv.customerName}</td>
13899:                         <td style="border: 1px solid #ddd; padding: 10px;">${inv.date}</td>
13900:                         <td style="border: 1px solid #ddd; padding: 10px;">${inv.platform || '-'}</td>
13901:                         <td style="border: 1px solid #ddd; padding: 10px;">${(inv.total || 0).toFixed(2)}</td>
13902:                     </tr>
13903:                 `;
13904:             });
13905:             
13906:             content += '</tbody></table>';
13907:             
13908:             const opt = {
13909:                 margin: 10,
13910:                 filename: `تقرير_الفواتير_${new Date().toLocaleDateString('ar-EG')}.pdf`,
13911:                 image: { type: 'jpeg', quality: 0.98 },
13912:                 html2canvas: { scale: 2 },
13913:                 jsPDF: { orientation: 'landscape', unit: 'mm', format: 'a4' }
13914:             };
13915:             
13916:             const div = document.createElement('div');
13917:             div.innerHTML = content;
13918:             div.style.direction = 'rtl';
13919:             div.style.padding = '20px';
13920:             document.body.appendChild(div);
13921:             
13922:             html2pdf().set(opt).from(div).save().then(() => {
13923:                 document.body.removeChild(div);
13924:             });
13925:         }

13934:             function updateQuickStats() {
13935:             try {
13936:                 const today = new Date().toISOString().split('T')[0];
13937:                 
13938:                 // Today purchases
13939:                 const todayPurchases = purchases.filter(p => {
13940:                     const purchaseDate = p.purchaseDate ? p.purchaseDate.split('T')[0] : '';
13941:                     return purchaseDate === today;
13942:                 }).reduce((sum, p) => sum + (p.totalAmount || 0), 0);
13943:                 const todayPurchasesEl = document.getElementById('todayPurchases');
13944:                 if (todayPurchasesEl) todayPurchasesEl.textContent = todayPurchases.toFixed(2) + ' جنيه';
13945:                 
13946:                 // Today sales
13947:                 const todayInvoices = invoices.filter(i => {
13948:                     const invoiceDate = i.date ? i.date.split('T')[0] : '';
13949:                     return invoiceDate === today;
13950:                 });
13951:                 const todaySalesAmount = todayInvoices.reduce((sum, i) => sum + (i.total || 0), 0);
13952:                 const todaySalesEl = document.getElementById('todaySales');
13953:                 if (todaySalesEl) todaySalesEl.textContent = todaySalesAmount.toFixed(2) + ' جنيه';
13954:                 
13955:                 // Pending invoices
13956:                 const pendingInvoicesCount = invoices.filter(i => !i.deliveryStatus || i.deliveryStatus !== 'delivered').length;
13957:                 const pendingInvoicesEl = document.getElementById('pendingInvoices');
13958:                 if (pendingInvoicesEl) pendingInvoicesEl.textContent = pendingInvoicesCount;
13959:                 
13960:                 // Today returns
13961:                 const todayReturns = returns.filter(r => r.date && r.date.split('T')[0] === today).length;
13962:                 const todayReturnsEl = document.getElementById('todayReturns');
13963:                 if (todayReturnsEl) todayReturnsEl.textContent = todayReturns;
13964:                 
13965:                 console.log('Quick stats updated');
13966:             } catch (e) {
13967:                 console.error('Error updating quick stats:', e);
13968:             }
13969:         }
13970:         
13971:         // Update quick stats periodically
13972:         setInterval(updateQuickStats, 5000); // Update every 5 seconds
13973:

13975:             loadSupplierOptions();
13976:             loadExpenseTypeOptions();
13977:             loadCustomerOptions();
13978:

13984:             });

13993:             // ==== TAX REPORT FUNCTION ====
13994:             function generateTaxReport() {
13995:                 const period = document.getElementById('taxReportPeriod').value;
13996:                 const vatRate = parseFloat(localStorage.getItem('vatRate')) || 15;
13997:                 
13998:                 // Filter invoices by period
13999:                 let filteredInvoices = [];
14000:                 const now = new Date();
14001:                 
14002:                 filteredInvoices = invoices.filter(inv => {
14003:                     if (!inv.date) return false;
14004:                     const invDate = new Date(inv.date);
14005:                     
14006:                     if (period === 'daily') {
14007:                         return invDate.toDateString() === now.toDateString();
14008:                     } else if (period === 'monthly') {
14009:                         return invDate.getMonth() === now.getMonth() && invDate.getFullYear() === now.getFullYear();
14010:                     } else {
14011:                         return invDate.getFullYear() === now.getFullYear();
14012:                     }
14013:                 });
14014:                 
14015:                 // Calculate totals
14016:                 const totalSales = filteredInvoices.reduce((sum, inv) => sum + (inv.total || 0), 0);
14017:                 const totalTax = filteredInvoices.reduce((sum, inv) => sum + (inv.tax || 0), 0);
14018:                 const totalDiscount = filteredInvoices.reduce((sum, inv) => sum + (inv.discount || 0), 0);
14019:                 const netSales = totalSales - totalTax - totalDiscount;
14020:                 
14021:                 // Create report content
14022:                 const reportContent = `
14023:                     <div style="padding: 20px; background: #f8f9fa; border-radius: 10px; margin-bottom: 20px;">
14024:                         <h3 style="margin-bottom: 20px; color: #333;">تقرير الضرائب - ${period === 'daily' ? 'يومي' : period === 'monthly' ? 'شهري' : 'سنوي'}</h3>
14025:                         <table style="width: 100%; border-collapse: collapse;">
14026:                             <tr style="background: #e9ecef;">
14027:                                 <th style="padding: 12px; text-align: right; border-bottom: 2px solid #dee2e6;">البند</th>
14028:                                 <th style="padding: 12px; text-align: left; border-bottom: 2px solid #dee2e6;">القيمة</th>
14029:                             </tr>
14030:                             <tr>
14031:                                 <td style="padding: 12px; border-bottom: 1px solid #dee2e6;">عدد الفواتير</td>
14032:                                 <td style="padding: 12px; border-bottom: 1px solid #dee2e6;">${filteredInvoices.length}</td>
14033:                             </tr>
14034:                             <tr>
14035:                                 <td style="padding: 12px; border-bottom: 1px solid #dee2e6;">إجمالي المبيعات</td>
14036:                                 <td style="padding: 12px; border-bottom: 1px solid #dee2e6;">${totalSales.toFixed(2)} ر.س</td>
14037:                             </tr>
14038:                             <tr>
14039:                                 <td style="padding: 12px; border-bottom: 1px solid #dee2e6;">الخصومات</td>
14040:                                 <td style="padding: 12px; border-bottom: 1px solid #dee2e6;">${totalDiscount.toFixed(2)} ر.س</td>
14041:                             </tr>
14042:                             <tr>
14043:                                 <td style="padding: 12px; border-bottom: 1px solid #dee2e6;">صافي المبيعات</td>
14044:                                 <td style="padding: 12px; border-bottom: 1px solid #dee2e6;">${netSales.toFixed(2)} ر.س</td>
14045:                             </tr>
14046:                             <tr style="background: #fff3cd;">
14047:                                 <td style="padding: 12px; border-bottom: 1px solid #dee2e6;"><strong>ضريبة القيمة المضافة (${vatRate}%)</strong></td>
14048:                                 <td style="padding: 12px; border-bottom: 1px solid #dee2e6;"><strong>${totalTax.toFixed(2)} ر.س</strong></td>
14049:                             </tr>
14050:                             <tr style="background: #28a745; color: white;">
14051:                                 <td style="padding: 12px;"><strong>الإجمالي شامل الضريبة</strong></td>
14052:                                 <td style="padding: 12px;"><strong>${(netSales + totalTax).toFixed(2)} ر.س</strong></td>
14053:                             </tr>
14054:                         </table>
14055:                     </div>
14056:                     
14057:                     <div style="padding: 20px; background: #f8f9fa; border-radius: 10px;">
14058:                         <h4 style="margin-bottom: 15px; color: #333;">تفاصيل الفواتير</h4>
14059:                         ${filteredInvoices.length > 0 ? `
14060:                             <table style="width: 100%; border-collapse: collapse;">
14061:                                 <tr style="background: #e9ecef;">
14062:                                     <th style="padding: 10px; text-align: right; border-bottom: 2px solid #dee2e6;">رقم الفاتورة</th>
14063:                                     <th style="padding: 10px; text-align: right; border-bottom: 2px solid #dee2e6;">التاريخ</th>
14064:                                     <th style="padding: 10px; text-align: right; border-bottom: 2px solid #dee2e6;">العميل</th>
14065:                                     <th style="padding: 10px; text-align: left; border-bottom: 2px solid #dee2e6;">الضريبة</th>
14066:                                     <th style="padding: 10px; text-align: left; border-bottom: 2px solid #dee2e6;">الإجمالي</th>
14067:                                 </tr>
14068:                                 ${filteredInvoices.map(inv => `
14069:                                     <tr>
14070:                                         <td style="padding: 10px; border-bottom: 1px solid #dee2e6;">${inv.id || inv.invoiceNumber || '-'}</td>
14071:                                         <td style="padding: 10px; border-bottom: 1px solid #dee2e6;">${inv.date || '-'}</td>
14072:                                         <td style="padding: 10px; border-bottom: 1px solid #dee2e6;">${inv.customerName || '-'}</td>
14073:                                         <td style="padding: 10px; border-bottom: 1px solid #dee2e6;">${(inv.tax || 0).toFixed(2)} ر.س</td>
14074:                                         <td style="padding: 10px; border-bottom: 1px solid #dee2e6;">${(inv.total || 0).toFixed(2)} ر.س</td>
14075:                                     </tr>
14076:                                 `).join('')}
14077:                             </table>
14078:                         ` : '<p style="text-align: center; color: #666;">لا توجد فواتير في هذه الفترة</p>'}
14079:                     </div>
14080:                 `;
14081:                 
14082:                 // Show in modal
14083:                 document.getElementById('modalTitle').innerHTML = '<i class="fas fa-file-invoice-dollar"></i> تقرير الضرائب';
14084:                 document.getElementById('modalBody').innerHTML = reportContent;
14085:                 document.getElementById('modalFooter').innerHTML = `
14086:                     <button class="btn btn-secondary" onclick="closeModal()">إغلاق</button>
14087:                     <button class="btn btn-primary" onclick="exportTaxReportToExcel('${period}')"><i class="fas fa-file-excel"></i> تصدير Excel</button>
14088:                 `;
14089:                 openModal();
14090:             }
14091:             
14092:             function exportTaxReportToExcel(period) {
14093:                 const vatRate = parseFloat(localStorage.getItem('vatRate')) || 15;
14094:                 
14095:                 let filteredInvoices = [];
14096:                 const now = new Date();
14097:                 
14098:                 filteredInvoices = invoices.filter(inv => {
14099:                     if (!inv.date) return false;
14100:                     const invDate = new Date(inv.date);
14101:                     
14102:                     if (period === 'daily') {
14103:                         return invDate.toDateString() === now.toDateString();
14104:                     } else if (period === 'monthly') {
14105:                         return invDate.getMonth() === now.getMonth() && invDate.getFullYear() === now.getFullYear();
14106:                     } else {
14107:                         return invDate.getFullYear() === now.getFullYear();
14108:                     }
14109:                 });
14110:                 
14111:                 const totalSales = filteredInvoices.reduce((sum, inv) => sum + (inv.total || 0), 0);
14112:                 const totalTax = filteredInvoices.reduce((sum, inv) => sum + (inv.tax || 0), 0);
14113:                 const totalDiscount = filteredInvoices.reduce((sum, inv) => sum + (inv.discount || 0), 0);
14114:                 
14115:                 // Create CSV content
14116:                 let csv = 'تقرير الضرائب\n';
14117:                 csv += `الفترة,${period === 'daily' ? 'يومي' : period === 'monthly' ? 'شهري' : 'سنوي'}\n\n`;
14118:                 csv += 'البند,القيمة\n';
14119:                 csv += `عدد الفواتير,${filteredInvoices.length}\n`;
14120:                 csv += `إجمالي المبيعات,${totalSales.toFixed(2)}\n`;
14121:                 csv += `الخصومات,${totalDiscount.toFixed(2)}\n`;
14122:                 csv += `ضريبة القيمة المضافة (${vatRate}%),${totalTax.toFixed(2)}\n\n`;
14123:                 csv += `الإجمالي شامل الضريبة,${(totalSales).toFixed(2)}\n\n`;
14124:                 csv += 'تفاصيل الفواتير\n';
14125:                 csv += 'رقم الفاتورة,التاريخ,العميل,الضريبة,الإجمالي\n';
14126:                 
14127:                 filteredInvoices.forEach(inv => {
14128:                     csv += `${inv.id || inv.invoiceNumber || '-'},${inv.date || '-'},${inv.customerName || '-'},${(inv.tax || 0).toFixed(2)},${(inv.total || 0).toFixed(2)}\n`;
14129:                 });
14130:                 
14131:                 // Download
14132:                 const blob = new Blob([csv], { type: 'text/csv;charset=utf-8;' });
14133:                 const link = document.createElement('a');
14134:                 link.href = URL.createObjectURL(blob);
14135:                 link.download = `tax_report_${period}_${new Date().toISOString().split('T')[0]}.csv`;
14136:                 link.click();
14137:             }

14150:                     localStorage.setItem('hances_dashboard_logo', base64);
14151:                     document.getElementById('dashboardLogoPreview').innerHTML = '<img src="' + base64 + '" style="max-width: 150px; max-height: 80px; border-radius: 8px; border: 2px solid #eee;">';
14152:                     updateDashboardLogo();
14153:                 };
14154:                 reader.readAsDataURL(file);
14155:             }

14525:     <!-- IndexedDB for permanent data storage -->
14526:     <script>
14527:         // IndexedDB Database Name and Version
14528:         const DB_NAME = 'HancesERP_DB';
14529:         const DB_VERSION = 1;
14530:         let db;
14531:         
14532:         // Initialize IndexedDB
14533:         function initIndexedDB() {
14534:             return new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
14535:                 const request = indexedDB.open(DB_NAME, DB_VERSION);
14536:                 
14537:                 request.onerror = (event) => {
14538:                     console.error('IndexedDB error:', event.target.error);
14539:                     reject(event.target.error);
14540:                 };
14541:                 
14542:                 request.onsuccess = (event) => {
14543:                     db = event.target.result;
14544:                     console.log('IndexedDB initialized successfully');
14545:                     resolve(db);
14546:                 };
14547:                 
14548:                 request.onupgradeneeded = (event) => {
14549:                     const db = event.target.result;
14550:                     
14551:                     // Create stores for different data types
14552:                     if (!db.objectStoreNames.contains('products')) {
14553:                         db.createObjectStore('products', { keyPath: 'id' });
14554:                     }
14555:                     if (!db.objectStoreNames.contains('invoices')) {
14556:                         db.createObjectStore('invoices', { keyPath: 'id' });
14557:                     }
14558:                     if (!db.objectStoreNames.contains('expenses')) {
14559:                         db.createObjectStore('expenses', { keyPath: 'id' });
14560:                     }
14561:                     if (!db.objectStoreNames.contains('collections')) {
14562:                         db.createObjectStore('collections', { keyPath: 'id' });
14563:                     }
14564:                     if (!db.objectStoreNames.contains('suppliers')) {
14565:                         db.createObjectStore('suppliers', { keyPath: 'id' });
14566:                     }
14567:                     if (!db.objectStoreNames.contains('expenseTypes')) {
14568:                         db.createObjectStore('expenseTypes', { keyPath: 'id' });
14569:                     }
14570:                     if (!db.objectStoreNames.contains('customers')) {
14571:                         db.createObjectStore('customers', { keyPath: 'id' });
14572:                     }
14573:                     if (!db.objectStoreNames.contains('settings')) {
14574:                         db.createObjectStore('settings', { keyPath: 'key' });
14575:                     }
14576:                     if (!db.objectStoreNames.contains('autoBackup')) {
14577:                         db.createObjectStore('autoBackup', { keyPath: 'id' });
14578:                     }
14579:                 };
14580:             });
14581:         }
14582:         
14583:         // Save data to IndexedDB
14584:         function saveToIndexedDB(storeName, data) {
14585:             return new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
14586:                 const transaction = db.transaction([storeName], 'readwrite');
14587:                 const store = transaction.objectStore(storeName);
14588:                 
14589:                 if (Array.isArray(data)) {
14590:                     data.forEach(item => store.put(item));
14591:                 } else {
14592:                     store.put(data);
14593:                 }
14594:                 
14595:                 transaction.oncomplete = () => resolve();
14596:                 transaction.onerror = (event) => reject(event.target.error);
14597:             });
14598:         }
14599:         
14600:         // Get all data from IndexedDB store
14601:         function getAllFromIndexedDB(storeName) {
14602:             return new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
14603:                 const transaction = db.transaction([storeName], 'readonly');
14604:                 const store = transaction.objectStore(storeName);
14605:                 const request = store.getAll();
14606:                 
14607:                 request.onsuccess = () => resolve(request.result);
14608:                 request.onerror = (event) => reject(event.target.error);
14609:             });
14610:         }
14611:         
14612:         // Clear all data from IndexedDB store
14613:         function clearIndexedDBStore(storeName) {
14614:             return new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
14615:                 const transaction = db.transaction([storeName], 'readwrite');
14616:                 const store = transaction.objectStore(storeName);
14617:                 store.clear();
14618:                 
14619:                 transaction.oncomplete = () => resolve();
14620:                 transaction.onerror = (event) => reject(event.target.error);
14621:             });
14622:         }

14624:         // Auto Backup to IndexedDB - Saves complete system data
14625:         async function createAutoBackupToIndexedDB() {
14626:             try {
14627:                 // Clear old auto backup
14628:                 await clearIndexedDBStore('autoBackup');
14629:                 
14630:                 // Save current data
14631:                 await saveToIndexedDB('autoBackup', {
14632:                     id: 'full_backup',
14633:                     date: new Date().toISOString(),
14634:                     data: {
14635:                         products: typeof products !== 'undefined' ? products : [],
14636:                         invoices: typeof invoices !== 'undefined' ? invoices : [],
14637:                         expenses: typeof expenses !== 'undefined' ? expenses : [],
14638:                         collections: typeof collections !== 'undefined' ? collections : [],
14639:                         suppliers: typeof suppliers !== 'undefined' ? suppliers : [],
14640:                         expenseTypes: typeof expenseTypes !== 'undefined' ? expenseTypes : [],
14641:                         customers: typeof customers !== 'undefined' ? customers : [],
14642:                         capitalAmount: typeof capitalAmount !== 'undefined' ? capitalAmount : 0,
14643:                         backupDate: new Date().toISOString()
14644:                     }
14645:                 });
14646:                 
14647:                 console.log('Auto backup saved to IndexedDB');
14648:                 return true;
14649:             } catch (error) {
14650:                 console.error('Auto backup failed:', error);
14651:                 return false;
14652:             }
14653:         }
14654:         
14655:         // Restore from IndexedDB backup
14656:         async function restoreFromIndexedDBBackup() {
14657:             try {
14658:                 const backups = await getAllFromIndexedDB('autoBackup');
14659:                 const backup = backups.find(b => b.id === 'full_backup');
14660:                 
14661:                 if (!backup) {
14662:                     return { success: false, message: 'لا توجد نسخة احتياطية محفوظة!' };
14663:                 }
14664:                 
14665:                 return { success: true, data: backup.data };
14666:             } catch (error) {
14667:                 console.error('Restore failed:', error);
14668:                 return { success: false, message: 'حدث خطأ أثناء الاستعادة!' };
14669:             }
14670:         }

14675:                 const backups = await getAllFromIndexedDB('autoBackup');
14676:                 return backups.some(b => b.id === 'full_backup');
14677:             } catch (error) {
14678:                 return false;
14679:             }
14680:         }

14682:         // ============================================
14683:         // Enhanced Large Storage Database System (IndexedDB)
14684:         // Supports up to gigabytes of data storage
14685:         // ============================================
14686:         
14687:         // Use existing db variable, just update database
14688:         const LARGE_DB_NAME = 'HancesProLargeDB';
14689:         const LARGE_DB_VERSION = 2; // Incremented for new schema
14690:         const MAX_STORAGE_SIZE = 5 * 1024 * 1024 * 1024; // 5 GB max storage
14691:         let largeDB = null;
14692:         
14693:         // Initialize Enhanced IndexedDB with large storage support
14694:         function initLargeDB() {
14695:             return new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
14696:                 const request = indexedDB.open(LARGE_DB_NAME, LARGE_DB_VERSION);
14697:                 
14698:                 request.onerror = (event) => {
14699:                     console.error('Failed to open database:', event.target.error);
14700:                     reject(event.target.error);
14701:                 };
14702:                 
14703:                 request.onsuccess = (event) => {
14704:                     largeDB = event.target.result;
14705:                     console.log('✓ Large Database initialized successfully');
14706:                     console.log('📦 Storage quota: Up to ' + (MAX_STORAGE_SIZE / (1024*1024*1024)).toFixed(1) + ' GB');
14707:                     resolve(largeDB);
14708:                 };
14709:                 
14710:                 request.onupgradeneeded = (event) => {
14711:                     const db = event.target.result;
14712:                     
14713:                     // Main data stores
14714:                     if (!db.objectStoreNames.contains('products')) {
14715:                         const productStore = db.createObjectStore('products', { keyPath: 'id', autoIncrement: true });
14716:                         productStore.createIndex('barcode', 'barcode', { unique: true });
14717:                         productStore.createIndex('category', 'category', { unique: false });
14718:                         productStore.createIndex('name', 'name', { unique: false });
14719:                         productStore.createIndex('quantity', 'quantity', { unique: false });
14720:                         productStore.createIndex('price', 'price', { unique: false });
14721:                         console.log('✓ Products store created');
14722:                     }
14723:                     
14724:                     if (!db.objectStoreNames.contains('invoices')) {
14725:                         const invoiceStore = db.createObjectStore('invoices', { keyPath: 'id', autoIncrement: true });
14726:                         invoiceStore.createIndex('invoiceNumber', 'invoiceNumber', { unique: true });
14727:                         invoiceStore.createIndex('date', 'date', { unique: false });
14728:                         invoiceStore.createIndex('customer', 'customer', { unique: false });
14729:                         invoiceStore.createIndex('total', 'total', { unique: false });
14730:                         invoiceStore.createIndex('status', 'status', { unique: false });
14731:                         console.log('✓ Invoices store created');
14732:                     }
14733:                     
14734:                     if (!db.objectStoreNames.contains('expenses')) {
14735:                         const expenseStore = db.createObjectStore('expenses', { keyPath: 'id', autoIncrement: true });
14736:                         expenseStore.createIndex('date', 'date', { unique: false });
14737:                         expenseStore.createIndex('type', 'type', { unique: false });
14738:                         expenseStore.createIndex('amount', 'amount', { unique: false });
14739:                         console.log('✓ Expenses store created');
14740:                     }
14741:                     
14742:                     if (!db.objectStoreNames.contains('collections')) {
14743:                         const collectionStore = db.createObjectStore('collections', { keyPath: 'id', autoIncrement: true });
14744:                         collectionStore.createIndex('date', 'date', { unique: false });
14745:                         collectionStore.createIndex('customer', 'customer', { unique: false });
14746:                         collectionStore.createIndex('amount', 'amount', { unique: false });
14747:                         console.log('✓ Collections store created');
14748:                     }
14749:                     
14750:                     if (!db.objectStoreNames.contains('suppliers')) {
14751:                         const supplierStore = db.createObjectStore('suppliers', { keyPath: 'id', autoIncrement: true });
14752:                         supplierStore.createIndex('name', 'name', { unique: false });
14753:                         supplierStore.createIndex('phone', 'phone', { unique: false });
14754:                         console.log('✓ Suppliers store created');
14755:                     }
14756:                     
14757:                     if (!db.objectStoreNames.contains('customers')) {
14758:                         const customerStore = db.createObjectStore('customers', { keyPath: 'id', autoIncrement: true });
14759:                         customerStore.createIndex('name', 'name', { unique: false });
14760:                         customerStore.createIndex('phone', 'phone', { unique: false });
14761:                         customerStore.createIndex('balance', 'balance', { unique: false });
14762:                         console.log('✓ Customers store created');
14763:                     }
14764:                     
14765:                     if (!db.objectStoreNames.contains('expenseTypes')) {
14766:                         const expenseTypeStore = db.createObjectStore('expenseTypes', { keyPath: 'id', autoIncrement: true });
14767:                         expenseTypeStore.createIndex('name', 'name', { unique: true });
14768:                         console.log('✓ Expense Types store created');
14769:                     }
14770:                     
14771:                     // Large data storage for images and files
14772:                     if (!db.objectStoreNames.contains('images')) {
14773:                         const imageStore = db.createObjectStore('images', { keyPath: 'id' });
14774:                         imageStore.createIndex('relatedTo', 'relatedTo', { unique: false });
14775:                         imageStore.createIndex('type', 'type', { unique: false });
14776:                         imageStore.createIndex('date', 'date', { unique: false });
14777:                         console.log('✓ Images/Files store created (for large binary data)');
14778:                     }
14779:                     
14780:                     // System settings and configurations
14781:                     if (!db.objectStoreNames.contains('settings')) {
14782:                         db.createObjectStore('settings', { keyPath: 'key' });
14783:                         console.log('✓ Settings store created');
14784:                     }
14785:                     
14786:                     // Complete system backup store
14787:                     if (!db.objectStoreNames.contains('fullBackup')) {
14788:                         const backupStore = db.createObjectStore('fullBackup', { keyPath: 'id' });
14789:                         backupStore.createIndex('date', 'date', { unique: false });
14790:                         backupStore.createIndex('size', 'size', { unique: false });
14791:                         console.log('✓ Full Backup store created');
14792:                     }
14793:                     
14794:                     // Transaction logs for audit
14795:                     if (!db.objectStoreNames.contains('transactionLogs')) {
14796:                         const logStore = db.createObjectStore('transactionLogs', { keyPath: 'id', autoIncrement: true });
14797:                         logStore.createIndex('timestamp', 'timestamp', { unique: false });
14798:                         logStore.createIndex('type', 'type', { unique: false });
14799:                         logStore.createIndex('store', 'store', { unique: false });
14800:                         console.log('✓ Transaction Logs store created');
14801:                     }
14802:                     
14803:                     // Deleted items archive (soft delete)
14804:                     if (!db.objectStoreNames.contains('deletedItems')) {
14805:                         const deletedStore = db.createObjectStore('deletedItems', { keyPath: 'id' });
14806:                         deletedStore.createIndex('originalStore', 'originalStore', { unique: false });
14807:                         deletedStore.createIndex('deleteDate', 'deleteDate', { unique: false });
14808:                         console.log('✓ Deleted Items archive store created');
14809:                     }
14810:                     
14811:                     console.log('📦 Database schema upgraded to version ' + LARGE_DB_VERSION);
14812:                 };
14813:             });
14814:         }

14816:         // Get database storage estimate
14817:         async function getStorageEstimate() {
14818:             if (navigator.storage && navigator.storage.estimate) {
14819:                 const estimate = await navigator.storage.estimate();
14820:                 return {
14821:                     usage: estimate.usage,
14822:                     quota: estimate.quota,
14823:                     usagePercent: ((estimate.usage / estimate.quota) * 100).toFixed(2),
14824:                     usageMB: (estimate.usage / (1024 * 1024)).toFixed(2),
14825:                     quotaGB: (estimate.quota / (1024 * 1024 * 1024)).toFixed(2),
14826:                     usageGB: (estimate.usage / (1024 * 1024 * 1024)).toFixed(4)
14827:                 };
14828:             }
14829:             return null;
14830:         }
14831:         
14832:         // Display storage info
14833:         async function displayStorageInfo() {
14834:             const info = await getStorageEstimate();
14835:             if (info) {
14836:                 console.log('📊 Storage Information:');
14837:                 console.log(`   Used: ${info.usageGB} GB (${info.usagePercent}% of ${info.quotaGB} GB)`);
14838:                 console.log(`   Usage: ${info.usageMB} MB`);
14839:                 return info;
14840:             }
14841:             return null;
14842:         }
14843:         
14844:         // Generic CRUD operations for large database
14845:         function addToLargeDB(storeName, data) {
14846:             return new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
14847:                 if (!largeDB) {
14848:                     reject('Database not initialized');
14849:                     return;
14850:                 }
14851:                 
14852:                 const transaction = largeDB.transaction([storeName], 'readwrite');
14853:                 const store = transaction.objectStore(storeName);
14854:                 
14855:                 // Add timestamp and metadata
14856:                 data.createdAt = new Date().toISOString();
14857:                 data.updatedAt = new Date().toISOString();
14858:                 
14859:                 const request = store.add(data);
14860:                 
14861:                 request.onsuccess = () => {
14862:                     logTransaction(storeName, 'CREATE', data.id);
14863:                     resolve(request.result);
14864:                 };
14865:                 
14866:                 request.onerror = (event) => {
14867:                     console.error('Add failed:', event.target.error);
14868:                     reject(event.target.error);
14869:                 };
14870:             });
14871:         }
14872:         
14873:         function updateInLargeDB(storeName, data) {
14874:             return new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
14875:                 if (!largeDB) {
14876:                     reject('Database not initialized');
14877:                     return;
14878:                 }
14879:                 
14880:                 const transaction = largeDB.transaction([storeName], 'readwrite');
14881:                 const store = transaction.objectStore(storeName);
14882:                 
14883:                 data.updatedAt = new Date().toISOString();
14884:                 
14885:                 const request = store.put(data);
14886:                 
14887:                 request.onsuccess = () => {
14888:                     logTransaction(storeName, 'UPDATE', data.id);
14889:                     resolve(request.result);
14890:                 };
14891:                 
14892:                 request.onerror = (event) => {
14893:                     console.error('Update failed:', event.target.error);
14894:                     reject(event.target.error);
14895:                 };
14896:             });
14897:         }
14898:         
14899:         function deleteFromLargeDB(storeName, id, permanent = false) {
14900:             return new Promise(async (resolve, reject) => {
14901:                 if (!largeDB) {
14902:                     reject('Database not initialized');
14903:                     return;
14904:                 }
14905:                 
14906:                 if (permanent) {
14907:                     // Permanent delete
14908:                     const transaction = largeDB.transaction([storeName], 'readwrite');
14909:                     const store = transaction.objectStore(storeName);
14910:                     const request = store.delete(id);
14911:                     
14912:                     request.onsuccess = () => {
14913:                         logTransaction(storeName, 'DELETE_PERMANENT', id);
14914:                         resolve(request.result);
14915:                     };
14916:                     
14917:                     request.onerror = (event) => {
14918:                         reject(event.target.error);
14919:                     };
14920:                 } else {
14921:                     // Soft delete - move to deletedItems
14922:                     try {
14923:                         const item = await getFromLargeDB(storeName, id);
14924:                         if (item) {
14925:                             const deletedItem = {
14926:                                 ...item,
14927:                                 originalStore: storeName,
14928:                                 deleteDate: new Date().toISOString(),
14929:                                 deletedBy: 'system'
14930:                             };
14931:                             await addToLargeDB('deletedItems', deletedItem);
14932:                             
14933:                             // Now permanently delete from original store
14934:                             const transaction = largeDB.transaction([storeName], 'readwrite');
14935:                             const store = transaction.objectStore(storeName);
14936:                             store.delete(id);
14937:                             
14938:                             logTransaction(storeName, 'DELETE_SOFT', id);
14939:                             resolve(true);
14940:                         } else {
14941:                             reject('Item not found');
14942:                         }
14943:                     } catch (error) {
14944:                         reject(error);
14945:                     }
14946:                 }
14947:             });
14948:         }
14949:         
14950:         function getFromLargeDB(storeName, id) {
14951:             return new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
14952:                 if (!largeDB) {
14953:                     reject('Database not initialized');
14954:                     return;
14955:                 }
14956:                 
14957:                 const transaction = largeDB.transaction([storeName], 'readonly');
14958:                 const store = transaction.objectStore(storeName);
14959:                 const request = store.get(id);
14960:                 
14961:                 request.onsuccess = () => resolve(request.result);
14962:                 request.onerror = (event) => reject(event.target.error);
14963:             });
14964:         }
14965:

14971:                 }

14982:         function queryLargeDB(storeName, indexName, value) {
14983:             return new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
14984:                 if (!largeDB) {
14985:                     reject('Database not initialized');
14986:                     return;
14987:                 }
14988:                 
14989:                 const transaction = largeDB.transaction([storeName], 'readonly');
14990:                 const store = transaction.objectStore(storeName);
14991:                 const index = store.index(indexName);
14992:                 const request = index.get(value);
14993:                 
14994:                 request.onsuccess = () => {
14995:                     const result = request.result;
14996:                     resolve(result);
14997:                 };
14998:                 
14999:                 request.onerror = (event) => reject(event.target.error);
15000:             });
15001:         }

15008:                 }
15009:

15020:         // Store large binary data (images, files)
15021:         async function storeLargeBinary(id, data, type, relatedTo = null) {
15022:             const imageData = {
15023:                 id: id,
15024:                 data: data, // Base64 or Blob
15025:                 type: type,
15026:                 relatedTo: relatedTo,
15027:                 date: new Date().toISOString(),
15028:                 size: typeof data === 'string' ? data.length * 0.75 : data.size
15029:             };
15030:             
15031:             return addToLargeDB('images', imageData);
15032:         }
15033:

15035:         async function getLargeBinary(id) {
15036:             return getFromLargeDB('images', id);
15037:         }
15038:         
15039:         // Log transactions for audit
15040:         function logTransaction(store, type, recordId) {
15041:             const log = {
15042:                 timestamp: new Date().toISOString(),
15043:                 store: store,
15044:                 type: type,
15045:                 recordId: recordId
15046:             };
15047:             
15048:             // Don't await - fire and forget
15049:             const transaction = largeDB.transaction(['transactionLogs'], 'readwrite');
15050:             transaction.objectStore('transactionLogs').add(log);
15051:         }
15052:         
15053:         // Complete system backup
15054:         async function createCompleteSystemBackup() {
15055:             try {
15056:                 const backup = {
15057:                     id: 'backup_' + Date.now(),
15058:                     date: new Date().toISOString(),
15059:                     version: LARGE_DB_VERSION,
15060:                     data: {
15061:                         products: await getAllFromLargeDB('products'),
15062:                         invoices: await getAllFromLargeDB('invoices'),
15063:                         expenses: await getAllFromLargeDB('expenses'),
15064:                         collections: await getAllFromLargeDB('collections'),
15065:                         suppliers: await getAllFromLargeDB('suppliers'),
15066:                         customers: await getAllFromLargeDB('customers'),
15067:                         expenseTypes: await getAllFromLargeDB('expenseTypes'),
15068:                         settings: await getAllFromLargeDB('settings'),
15069:                         deletedItems: await getAllFromLargeDB('deletedItems')
15070:                     }
15071:                 };
15072:                 
15073:                 // Calculate backup size
15074:                 const backupSize = new Blob([JSON.stringify(backup)]).size;
15075:                 backup.size = backupSize;
15076:                 
15077:                 // Store backup
15078:                 await addToLargeDB('fullBackup', backup);
15079:                 
15080:                 console.log('✅ Complete system backup created');
15081:                 console.log(`📦 Backup size: ${(backupSize / (1024 * 1024)).toFixed(2)} MB`);
15082:                 
15083:                 return { success: true, backup: backup };
15084:             } catch (error) {
15085:                 console.error('Backup failed:', error);
15086:                 return { success: false, error: error.message };
15087:             }
15088:         }
15089:         
15090:         // Restore complete system from backup
15091:         async function restoreCompleteSystem(backupId) {
15092:             try {
15093:                 const backup = await getFromLargeDB('fullBackup', backupId);
15094:                 
15095:                 if (!backup) {
15096:                     return { success: false, message: 'Backup not found' };
15097:                 }
15098:                 
15099:                 // Restore each store
15100:                 for (const [storeName, data] of Object.entries(backup.data)) {
15101:                     // Clear current store
15102:                     const transaction = largeDB.transaction([storeName], 'readwrite');
15103:                     transaction.objectStore(storeName).clear();
15104:                     
15105:                     // Restore data
15106:                     for (const item of data) {
15107:                         await addToLargeDB(storeName, item);
15108:                     }
15109:                 }
15110:                 
15111:                 console.log('✅ System restored from backup');
15112:                 return { success: true, backup: backup };
15113:             } catch (error) {
15114:                 console.error('Restore failed:', error);
15115:                 return { success: false, error: error.message };
15116:             }
15117:         }
15118:         
15119:         // Export database to JSON file
15120:         async function exportDatabaseToJSON() {
15121:             try {
15122:                 const backup = await createCompleteSystemBackup();
15123:                 
15124:                 if (!backup.success) {
15125:                     throw new Error('Failed to create backup');
15126:                 }
15127:                 
15128:                 const dataStr = JSON.stringify(backup.backup, null, 2);
15129:                 const blob = new Blob([dataStr], { type: 'application/json' });
15130:                 
15131:                 const url = URL.createObjectURL(blob);
15132:                 const link = document.createElement('a');
15133:                 link.href = url;
15134:                 link.download = `hances_backup_${new Date().toISOString().split('T')[0]}.json`;
15135:                 document.body.appendChild(link);
15136:                 link.click();
15137:                 document.body.removeChild(link);
15138:                 URL.revokeObjectURL(url);
15139:                 
15140:                 console.log('✅ Database exported to JSON file');
15141:                 return { success: true };
15142:             } catch (error) {
15143:                 console.error('Export failed:', error);
15144:                 return { success: false, error: error.message };
15145:             }
15146:         }
15147:         
15148:         // Import database from JSON file
15149:         async function importDatabaseFromJSON(file) {
15150:             return new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
15151:                 const reader = new FileReader();
15152:                 
15153:                 reader.onload = async (e) => {
15154:                     try {
15155:                         const backup = JSON.parse(e.target.result);
15156:                         
15157:                         // Validate backup structure
15158:                         if (!backup.data || !backup.version) {
15159:                             throw new Error('Invalid backup file format');
15160:                         }
15161:                         
15162:                         // Restore
15163:                         const result = await restoreCompleteSystem(backup.id);
15164:                         
15165:                         if (result.success) {
15166:                             console.log('✅ Database imported successfully');
15167:                             resolve({ success: true });
15168:                         } else {
15169:                             throw new Error('Restore failed');
15170:                         }
15171:                     } catch (error) {
15172:                         console.error('Import failed:', error);
15173:                         reject({ success: false, error: error.message });
15174:                     }
15175:                 };
15176:                 
15177:                 reader.onerror = () => {
15178:                     reject({ success: false, error: 'Failed to read file' });
15179:                 };
15180:                 
15181:                 reader.readAsText(file);
15182:             });
15183:         }
15184:         
15185:         // Get all available backups
15186:         async function getAvailableBackups() {
15187:             return getAllFromLargeDB('fullBackup');
15188:         }
15189:         
15190:         // Delete a specific backup
15191:         async function deleteBackup(backupId) {
15192:             return deleteFromLargeDB('fullBackup', backupId, true);
15193:         }
15194:         
15195:         // Initialize large database on page load
15196:         window.addEventListener('load', async function() {
15197:             try {
15198:                 await initLargeDB();
15199:                 await displayStorageInfo();
15200:                 
15201:                 // Auto backup every 5 minutes
15202:                 setInterval(async () => {
15203:                     await createCompleteSystemBackup();
15204:                     console.log('📅 Auto backup completed at:', new Date().toLocaleString());
15205:                 }, 5 * 60 * 1000);
15206:                 
15207:                 // Backup before closing
15208:                 window.addEventListener('beforeunload', async () => {
15209:                     await createCompleteSystemBackup();
15210:                 });
15211:                 
15212:                 // Backup when tab becomes hidden
15213:                 document.addEventListener('visibilitychange', async () => {
15214:                     if (document.hidden) {
15215:                         await createCompleteSystemBackup();
15216:                     }
15217:                 });
15218:                 
15219:                 console.log('🚀 Large Database System is ready!');
15220:             } catch (error) {
15221:                 console.error('Failed to initialize Large Database:', error);
15222:             }
15223:         });
15224:     </script>
15225: </body>
15226: </html>
